# Accounting engine

This document owns the engine's exact runtime behavior: what each implemented treatment accepts, what it books and what it refuses. [IMPLEMENTATION.md](IMPLEMENTATION.md) owns company-level coverage; [AGENT_API.md](AGENT_API.md) owns the wire contract; [ROADMAP.md](ROADMAP.md) owns remaining work.

The engine is the kernel described in [ADR 0010](adr/0010-append-only-kernel.md). Its pure parts live in `packages/xfactura-core`; persistence, commands and transports live in `packages/xfactura-api/source/engine`.

## How anything reaches the books

1. **Evidence.** An original file is kept as immutable, content-addressed bytes (`evidence.record`). Every proposal cites the evidence it relies on.
2. **Documents.** Imports turn evidence into typed, immutable documents: e-Factura invoices and credit notes (`invoices.import`), bank statements (`bank.import`, `bank.importCsv`), BNR rates (`fx.importBnr`). What an agent reads from a PDF or a photo (invoices, receipts, contracts, statements) is recorded as data the engine checks against the file ([Documents from PDFs and photos](#documents-from-pdfs-and-photos)).
3. **Treatment.** A pure function evaluates a typed input against the state it declares it reads: documents, open items, movements, the company profile, FX rates. It produces an **effect**: journal lines with dimensions and currency amounts, open items, tax entries and schedules, together with any issues.
4. **Proposal.** The engine validates the effect, resolves stable ids and hashes the version over its treatment, input, read-set, rules and effect. Issues are `block`, `ack` (must be acknowledged at approval) or `info`. A company has at most one proposal holding a subject (`document:<id>`, `movement:<id>`, …): open, or posted by an entry that stands.
5. **Binding.** A person approves the exact version and effect hash (`proposals.approve`), acknowledging each `ack` issue, or approves up to 200 versions without warnings at once (a batch, below). Or a human-approved posting policy covers the treatment and rule versions (`policies.create`, or `policies.request` once a template graduated) and acknowledges each `ack` issue by code (`acknowledges`); it never posts an extracted document unless it allows that source, nor while it, its template or the company is paused. Under the company's segregation of duties (`controls.segregation`), the approver, or the policy's approver, is someone other than whoever created the version: `on` for a version a person created themselves; `strict` also for one an agent created under that person's grant. Without the fact, segregation is `off`.
6. **Posting.** `ledger.post` checks that the approver is still an active owner or reviewer under the same membership epoch (`APPROVER_INACTIVE`), reloads the read-set and re-evaluates the treatment. If anything differs it refuses with `STALE`. Otherwise it writes the rows and then the entry header. The database's invariants check the rows against the approved effect, the balance, the binding (including the policy's acknowledgements and segregation), the period and tax-period locks and the numbering, then seal the entry.

Posted records are never changed. A correction is a reversal (`ledger.reverse`) plus a new proposal: once the reversal is posted, the reversed entry no longer stands and its subject is free, so the same document or movement is proposed again (the worklist lists it again as unbooked or unexplained). A reversal proposed but not posted frees nothing. The database keeps a subject to one standing entry (`subject_posted`) and one proposal holding it (`subject_taken`, and `identity_taken` across a document's copies).

Between proposal and posting the books may move on. `Proposals.recheck` re-evaluates the latest version against the current books exactly as posting would, writing nothing; the worker caches the result in `acc_proposal_checks` and the proposal shows `stale` (see [Worker, jobs and the change feed](#worker-jobs-and-the-change-feed)). Posting still re-evaluates on its own.

A proposal that recognizes a document records the one its input names (`documentId`), else its subject's. Copies of one document share their group's root as subject ([Copies and groups](#copies-and-groups)).

A version may rely on facts a person stated rather than on the books: its **basis**, the answered questions it cites ([Questions to people](#questions-to-people)). The engine, not the treatment, adds each to the read set at its latest answer, so a later answer makes the version stale and the database refuses to post it (`stale_read`), and loads those answers, and only those, for the treatment to read (`Loaded.answers`): `invoices.recognize` reads a vehicle's use from them. The basis is hashed into the version only when there is one.

Reviewed actions (closing or reopening a month, authorizing a return, approving a batch, creating a template, granting a policy from a graduation) bind the same way: a person approves the exact preview, and under segregation `on` that person is not who requested it (`strict`: nor who issued the requesting agent's grant). Execution recomputes the preview and refuses `APPROVER_INACTIVE` when the approver lost their role since; the database checks the decider again (`inv:decider`).

## Money, dates and rates

- Amounts cross the API as decimal strings and are stored as signed 64-bit integer minor units (bani for RON). Lines carry a signed amount on one side; a negative amount is a red storno.
- Accounting dates are calendar dates; Europe/Bucharest is applied when an instant must become a date.
- FX rates are BNR reference rates kept with the hash of the file they came from. Which publication applies on a date is a rule parameter (`ro.fx.rate_date.accounting`, `ro.fx.rate_date.vat`), currently `on_or_before` and unreviewed. A rate older than 7 days counts as missing. Whose rate a credit note in a currency takes is two more (`ro.fx.adjustment.vat`: its VAT at its invoice's rate; `ro.fx.adjustment.books`: its revenue or expense at its own date's), also unreviewed ([`invoices.credit`](#invoicescredit-credit-note-against-the-original)).

## Rules and their review status

Fiscal parameters come from the source register (`xfactura-core/src/ro/vat.ts`, `src/ro/fx.ts`, `src/ro/payroll/parameters.ts`). Resolution never defaults:

| Gap | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `NO_VERSION` | No version covers the date or scope (for example a 9% VAT category). |
| `BEYOND_HORIZON` | The open-ended version is reviewed only to an earlier date (VAT rates: 2026-12-31). |
| `INSUFFICIENT_REVIEW` | The version's review status is below what the company's mode requires. |

The company's `books.assurance` fact selects the mode:
- `preview`, the default, accepts unreviewed (`extracted`) rules;
- `shadow` requires interpreted rules;
- `live` requires signed rules.

Every current parameter is `extracted`. Each proposal lists the parameter versions it used and their status.

A status rises only through a review record. The procedure is in [GOVERNANCE.md](GOVERNANCE.md#rule-review-in-two-tiers); the code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/reviews`.
- **Tiers:** `interpreted` and `cases_approved` need a partner accountant's record; `signed` needs the expert contabil's signed packet.
- **Records:** each answers a review packet generated from a per-parameter spec: two readings, three to five cases computed by the treatments, and a multiple-choice question. Records are JSON files in `src/ro/reviews/records/` and hold the packet they answered.
- **Binding check:** `reviewProblems`, a core test over the default register, refuses a status that no record binds. A record binds while:
  - the reviewer chose the engine's reading; and
  - the version's packet item, rebuilt from today's register, spec and sources, hashes the same as the one reviewed.

  Changing the value, dates, clauses, excerpt anchors, readings or any case outcome voids the record, and the check names the change.
- **Today:** there are no records. `src/ro/register.ts` lists the same parameter families as the engine's `defaultRegister()`, and a scripts test checks the two agree.

Each version cites clauses from the source register (`xfactura-core/src/ro/sources`). A source is an act or an official document with the hashes of the copies read. A clause is `located` when its text was read in such a copy, and `pending` when only its place in the act is known; neither is a review. `bun run sources:fetch` fetches the sources that allow it into `.sources/` and reports drift from the registered hashes; acts on sites that refuse automated access are provided by hand in `.sources/inbox/`. `rules.parameters` lists every value with its citations, and `rules.horizon` the values whose reviewed period ends within a number of days.

Accounting policies the law requires in writing are dated company facts with their evidence. `policy.storno` (red or black) decides a reversal whose method is not given; without either, the reversal is blocked. `policy.payroll.employer_share` (635 or 6458) is where contributions borne in an employee's name are expensed; a payroll that needs it is blocked without it.

The chart is OMFP 1802/2014 as published, plus the accounts later orders added, each with the clause that adds it: 4315, 4316, 436 and 646 from OMFP 1243/2018, used from 2018; 463 (dividends distributed during the year) from OMFP 3067/2018, used from 17.09.2018. A company's chart is written when the company is created; from 5 November 2026 it also keeps 456 (the associates' capital settlements, interim dividends owed included) and 463 per associate, as open items. The charts written before are brought level by migration 0025 (written by hand, like 0024's 4481 and 4482): it adds 463 to every chart seeded from the reference chart that lacks it, and gives 456 and 463, synthetics and analytics alike, their control only where nothing was ever posted on the account, so no posted line loses its match. A company whose 456 holds posted lines keeps it uncontrolled, and an interim distribution there is refused by the kernel (`OPEN_ITEM_NOT_CONTROL`).

## Implemented treatments

### `ledger.general`: explicit journal entry

- **Input:** date, purpose (`opening`, `general`, `adjustment`), reference, description, evidence, and 2–2000 lines (account, side, amount, optional currency amount and rate, partner, open item or new open item, cost centre, project, memo).
- **Openings** must be dated on the first day of a financial year, and only of the books' first: the ledger is continuous, so a later year opens with its previous year's balances, and an opening entry there would count them twice. Once the books hold a financial year that ends before its date, an opening entry is refused (`OPENING_EARLIER_YEAR`; the database too, `inv:opening_first_year`, a storno of one excepted), and no financial year is added before an opening entry that stands (`YEAR_BEFORE_OPENING`, `inv:year_before_opening`): it is reversed first. The trial balance reports openings as initial balance.
- **Open items:**
  - A line on an open-item control account (4111, 401, 408, 418, 419, 4092, 421, 4311–4314, 4423, 4424, 444, 457, 461, 462, 471, 472, …) must settle an existing open item or create one for a partner.
  - Other lines may not name an open item.
  - Analytical accounts inherit their synthetic parent's control.
- **VAT accounts** (4426, 4427, 4428) cannot be posted by a general entry; the line needs tax entries from a VAT treatment.
- **Subject:** `opening:<date>` or `journal:<purpose>:<reference>`.

### `ledger.reverse`: full storno

- Reverses a posted entry in full, red (same sides, negative amounts) or black (swapped sides). It needs a reason of at least 10 characters and evidence.
- Tax entries are mirrored in the reversal's own tax period and linked to their origin (`originTaxEntry`, key `reversal:<key>`), with `correctsTaxPeriod` naming the period they correct.
  - **Signed attributes:** the attributes that hold an amount or a count of their entry (`signedAttributes`: `prorataTax`, `prorataDeducted`, a margin item's `price`, `cost`, `margin`, a travel period's `sales`, `costs`, `trips`, an OSS sale's `baseMinor`, `taxMinor`, a Z report's `receipts`) carry the entry's sign, as its base and tax do: a credit note writes them negative and a storno negates them, so every reader (the pro-rata, D394's margins and receipts, D398, D406) sums them as written.
  - **Facts, not amounts** (the definitive pro-rata of an ADJ-300): a storno withdraws them. D300's header, `vat.proRata`'s history of a capital good and `vat.proRata.compute` read the adjustment that stands (`standingTaxEntries`), and the worklist asks for the year's adjustment again.
  - **Another year's:** a storno whose `correctsTaxPeriod` is of another year is that year's correction: the year's pro-rata leaves it out, as a credit note across years is refused (`PRORATA_CREDIT_YEAR`).
- A reversal that would push a settled open item past zero is refused.
- Once posted, the reversed entry no longer stands: its subject (the document or movement it booked) is free for a new proposal, its open items are cleared, and the open items it settled are open again for the new booking to settle. A document's open items, as a treatment reads them (`openItems: [{ documentId }]`, a credit note's original), are those of its bookings that stand, so a credit note reduces the new booking's receivable or payable, never the reversed one's.
- A reversal is never reversed in turn (`REVERSAL_OF_REVERSAL`; the database too, `reversal_of_reversal`): a wrong storno is corrected by booking its subject again, which it freed. So one level decides whether an entry stands.
- **Stock moves** are mirrored on the same layers and lines, each at the number of the move it mirrors. Goods a storno takes out (a receipt's) are valued on the storno's date, as a return to the supplier is ([The stock register](#the-stock-register-g1)): the layer must hold them on that date and on every later day (`STOCK_INSUFFICIENT`, a pool allowed below zero excepted), and a difference against what the layer held then goes to the stock account's expense on lines after the mirrored ones.

### `invoices.recognize`: domestic invoice

- **Document:** an imported invoice (380) issued or received by the company.
- **Input:**
  - **One account** for every line: a revenue (70x) or expense (6xx) account, or for a purchase a fixed-asset account (20x, 21x), whose supplier is then held on 404. For purchases, the deduction is `full` or `none`.
  - **Or `allocations`:** each VAT category's taxable amount (`S-21`, `S-11`, `O`) split over accounts in the document's currency, each part with its own deduction. A category's parts must add up to its amount; allowances and charges are inside it.
  - **Deduction:** `full`, `none`, or `limited`.
    - `limited` deducts the register's share (`ro.vat.deduction.limited`, 50%) of a part's VAT. The share is rounded half up to the ban. The rest of the VAT stays in the expense, and D300 rd. 31 holds only what was deducted.
  - **Vehicles (Codul fiscal art. 298, norme pct. 68).** The VAT on buying, leasing or renting a motorized road vehicle of at most 3,500 kg and 9 seats (the driver's included), and on the costs directly attributable to it (fuel, repairs, upkeep, lubricants, spare parts), is limited to the share unless the vehicle is used only for the business, shown by its log (foaia de parcurs: category, purpose and place of each trip, kilometres, its own fuel norm), or the article excepts it.
    - A part names its vehicle: `vehicle` beside `account`, or `allocations[].vehicle`, the registration as written. It is read without spaces or dashes and in capitals (`B-123-XFA` is `B123XFA`), as the `vehicle.use` question names it.
    - A purchase part that deducts VAT (`full` or `limited`, standard-rated) and names a vehicle needs the owner's answer about that vehicle in the proposal's basis ([Questions to people](#questions-to-people)). Without one, or with one whose `from` is after the tax point, it is blocked by `VEHICLE_USE`, which asks `vehicle.use` about the vehicle. The answer decides: `business_only` (its log attached) or an exception deducts `full`; `mixed` deducts `limited`. Another deduction is refused (`VEHICLE_DEDUCTION`). A part deducting nothing (`none`, no VAT) needs no answer, nor does a purchase that cannot deduct (a non-payer, an inactive supplier outside the exceptions).
    - Exceptions, which the answer states with their proof (`exception`, `exceptionEvidenceId`): over 3,500 kg or more than 9 seats (alin. (2)); used by a sales or purchasing agent, for paid passenger transport, for paid services, rented out or leased, or as goods (alin. (3) lit. b)–f), where personal use is negligible, pct. 68 alin. (5)). Emergency, security and courier vehicles (lit. a)) must be used only for those services: they are `business_only`, with the log.
    - A part that deducts the limited share, or is on an account that suggests a vehicle, without naming one, is confirmed by the reviewer (`VEHICLE_UNNAMED`, ack): 6022 (fuel) and 2133 (vehicles) always; 611, 612, 6024 and 167 when a line names a vehicle (auto, autoturism, vehicul, mașină, carburant, motorină, benzină, anvelope, ITP, rovinietă, or a registration).
    - The explanation lists each vehicle with the answer that decided it and its evidence. Not covered: advances naming a vehicle (blocked), credit notes and cash receipts (their deduction stays as given), the adjustment of a vehicle bought as a capital good whose use changes (art. 305), and the profit-tax limit on vehicle costs (art. 25 alin. (3) lit. l)).
  - **Advances:**
    - An advance invoice puts its part on 419 (received from a customer) or 4091/4092 (paid to a supplier), opening an advance of the partner.
    - The final invoice's negative line deducting the advance goes to the same account and names the advance it settles (`advance`). The negative line is in the advance's VAT category, as e-Factura writes it, so the VAT follows.
    - Only advance parts may be negative.
    - An advance can't be settled beyond what it holds (`OPEN_ITEM_REVERSED`).
    - Purchase advances are deductible in full.
    - Advances in foreign currency are not settled yet.
  - Optionally the service date.
  - **Goods** (`stock`): a purchase received into a warehouse, its parts then on the items' stock accounts, with the reception's differences, or a sale's goods discharged at the policy's value ([The stock register](#the-stock-register-g1)); without `stock` a stock account is refused as before.
  - **Supplier status:**
    - For a purchase from a Romanian supplier, the treatment reads the supplier's ANAF status snapshot for the invoice's tax point.
    - If the snapshot shows the supplier inactive, no part may deduct VAT (`SUPPLIER_INACTIVE`, Codul fiscal art. 11 alin. (7)). The input `inactiveException` (`forced_execution`, `bankruptcy`) allows deduction behind an acknowledgement.
    - Without a snapshot, nothing is refused, but the month's close warns (`SUPPLIER_STATUS_UNCHECKED`).
    - Snapshots come from `parties.checkStatus`. It calls ANAF's public taxpayer-status service (`PlatitorTvaRest` v9: at most 100 CUIs per request, 1 request per second) and records snapshots per party and date in the append-only `acc_party_status`.
    - A snapshot is recorded only when ANAF's answer differs from the latest snapshot for that party and date in some field it stores (name, VAT, cash-accounting VAT, inactive with its dates, split VAT, e-Factura, struck off). An identical answer records nothing: the standing snapshot stays the one read, and ANAF's raw answer is kept as evidence only when a snapshot is recorded from it.
    - With `month`, only the suppliers without a snapshot for their invoice's tax point are asked about; the others are reported from their snapshot, so the close warning's re-check of a month does not ask about the suppliers already checked. With `partyIds` and `date`, ANAF is always asked.
    - The snapshot read is for exactly the date asked. A newer snapshot for that date, recorded because ANAF's answer changed, makes a pending proposal stale.
    - Not covered yet: purchases already posted when a later snapshot shows the supplier inactive, advance invoices from an inactive supplier, and purchases through cash expense reports or the general journal.
- **Supported:** the categories of the document's VAT breakdown, each S at the register's standard rate for the tax point or at its reduced rate, O, or an exempt one (E, K, G: [Exempt supplies, the pro-rata and capital goods](#exempt-supplies-the-pro-rata-and-capital-goods-v2)). The reduced rate applies only to the supplies of Codul fiscal art. 291 alin. (2), so it is an `ack` warning (`REDUCED_RATE`): the reviewer confirms the operation qualifies. For agricultural inputs and services (lit. e) and f)) the list is the joint order the law refers to, Ordinul nr. 1.155/868/2016 (the NC codes of fertilizers, pesticides and seeds, and the 34 services of its annex), registered in `src/ro/sources/schemes.ts`; issued for the former lit. h) at 9%, it is cited by the reduced-rate packet, and whether a newer joint order replaced it after Legea nr. 141/2025 is not verified.
  - **The tax point** (`chargeabilityOf`) is the date VAT becomes chargeable, so the date the rate is read on, the VAT period and the VAT exchange rate: the rate in force when the chargeable event occurs (fapt generator, art. 281), unless an invoice or an advance came first (art. 282 alin. (1)–(2), art. 291 alin. (4)–(5)). By order of evidence:
    - the supply's date the document states (BT-72), unless the issue date, or a stated tax point (BT-7, an advance collected earlier), is earlier. A July supply invoiced in August with BT-72 takes July's rate and July's VAT period; the entry is still dated on the invoice;
    - without BT-72, the stated tax point (BT-7), else the issue date, standing for the supply's date;
    - the invoiced period (BG-14) is not used: for a continuous supply the chargeable event is the payment date in the contract or the invoice (art. 281 alin. (8)), for another service its completion (alin. (12)), and the document does not say which.
  - **Warned (`ack`)** about that date:
    - `SUPPLY_DATE_ASSUMED`: the issue date stands for the supply's date and the standard rate changed since the first day of the previous month (or the invoiced period's start), so the supply may predate the change. A 19% invoice of August 2025 without BT-72 is also refused (`VAT_RATE`) until a document states the supply's date;
    - `RATE_REGULARIZATION`: the invoice or the advance came before a supply at another rate; art. 291 alin. (6) requires the regularization at the supply's rate, which the engine doesn't compute;
    - `TAX_POINT_DATES`: a stated tax point later than the supply or the issue date, which the engine doesn't follow.
  - The same date gives the rate checked when the invoicer issues an invoice (`sales.issue`) and when an extracted document is checked (`VAT_RATE`).
  - The company's `vat.status` on the tax-point date must be recorded.
  - A VAT payer's sale must use S, or an exempt category with its legal basis (`exemption`); a non-payer's sale must use O.
  - A non-payer cannot deduct: a purchase with VAT books it into the expense and records a non-deductible tax entry.
- **Postings:**

  | Case | Lines |
  |---|---|
  | Sale | Dr 4111 gross / Cr revenue net / Cr 4427 VAT |
  | Purchase, deductible | Dr expense net / Dr 4426 VAT / Cr 401 gross |
  | Purchase, non-deductible | Dr expense gross / Cr 401 gross |

  - There is one line per part, one VAT line per category, and one tax entry per category (key `vat`, or `vat:S-21` and so on when there are several).
  - A non-deductible part takes its share of its category's VAT.
  - In foreign currency, the document's totals in lei are split in proportion to its own amounts, so the totals stay exact.
  - The receivable or payable is an open item of the counterparty, linked to the document.
- **Foreign currency:**
  - The document must state its VAT in lei (BT-6 = RON, BT-111).
  - Revenue or expense converts at the accounting rate on the issue date. VAT in lei is the stated BT-111 amount, and a difference from the BNR VAT rate is an `ack` warning.
  - Every line records its currency amount and rate, and the open item is held in the document currency.
- **Refused:** allocations that don't cover each category exactly, fixed assets and expenses on one invoice (404 and 401), other categories (AE outside the reverse-charge treatments, Z, L, M, …), a purchase in K (an intra-community acquisition of goods, `INTRA_EU_GOODS`: booked with `goods.euAcquisition`, [below](#goods-across-the-border-g3-intra-community-supplies-and-acquisitions-imports-exports)), prepaid amounts (advances), payable rounding and missing rates.
- **Warned (`ack`):** a service date in another month than the tax point, which may need a regularization.

### `invoices.credit`: credit note against the original

- **Document:** an imported or issued credit note (381, or a negative 380 normalized by the parser), in its invoice's currency.
- **Input:** the original document, and the account it used with the deduction, or `allocations` splitting each VAT category over the original's accounts (positive amounts in the credit note's currency, lei for one in lei, each part with its deduction).
- **Rules:**
  - The credit note must reference the original's number. Each of its VAT categories must be one of the original's, at the rates in force on the original's tax point (its supply's date when it states one): a credit note after 1 August 2025 for a supply before keeps 19% (art. 282 alin. (9)). The credit note's own VAT is chargeable on its date (BT-7, else the issue date), even if it repeats the supply's date.
  - Each category's tax entry points to the original's entry of the same category.
  - Amounts reverse the original accounts by red storno, in the credit note's own tax period, and reduce the original open item by what it still holds, never past zero.
  - **Beyond what remains open** (the invoice collected or paid already): the rest is a refund, a new item of the partner, acknowledged by the reviewer (`CREDIT_REFUND`): owed to the customer on 462 ("sumele încasate și necuvenite"), owed by the supplier on 461 ("alte creanțe"), to pay back, collect or offset (OMFP nr. 1.802/2014, the functions of 462 and 461; 4111 is an asset account and 401 a liability one, and 419 and 409 hold advances, pct. 311 alin. (1), not revalued, pct. 316 alin. (2)). Which account is the expert's to confirm. `bank.settle` and `items.offset` settle it like any item; D406 reports its lines as the partner's (the customer or supplier on 462 or 461), in the period it opens and in every later one it stays open or moves in ([D406](#declarations)).
- **Foreign currency** (5 November 2026). Sources read on that date: ANAF's consolidated Code with the norms of HG nr. 602/2025 (`ANAF/Cod-fiscal-consolidat`), the portal's consolidated Code of 25.10.2026 (`LJR/Cod-fiscal-2025`, art. 282 alin. (9) in the same words), OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 as ANAF publishes it; clauses in `src/ro/sources/credit.ts` and the main list, review packets in `src/ro/reviews/specs/credit.ts`.
  - The credit note is in its invoice's currency (`CURRENCY` otherwise) and states its VAT in lei (BT-6 = RON, BT-111; `VAT_CURRENCY`), as an invoice does.
  - **Its VAT base and VAT** (`ro.fx.adjustment.vat`, value `base_operation`, extracted) are converted at the rate its invoice's base was determined at, the VAT rate of the invoice's tax point as the invoice's VAT entry records it, never at the credit note's own. Codul fiscal art. 282 alin. (9): at the events of art. 287 the tax is chargeable on the event's date, "iar regimul de impozitare, cotele aplicabile și cursul de schimb valutar sunt aceleași ca și ale operațiunii de bază"; the norms, pct. 25 alin. (2) (still so numbered, under art. 282): "cursul de schimb valutar utilizat pentru ajustarea bazei de impozitare este același ca al operațiunii de bază …, respectiv cursul de schimb valutar utilizat pentru determinarea bazei de impozitare a taxei pe valoarea adăugată pentru operațiunea de bază", the event's rate only when the base operation cannot be determined, which a credit note naming its invoice never is. The base operation's rate is art. 290 alin. (2) and pct. 35 alin. (6)'s, of its exigibility. The VAT in lei is the amount the note states, compared with that rate: a difference is acknowledged (`VAT_FX`). The tax entries record their amounts in the currency at that rate, in the credit note's own VAT period.
  - **Its revenue or expense** (`ro.fx.adjustment.books`, value `event`, extracted) is recorded at the accounting rate of the credit note's date: a transaction in a currency is recorded at the rate of the day it takes place (OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 319), and the credit note is the document of an event of its own date (pct. 314 alin. (2)–(3)). The order does not say so of a credit note: the alternative, the invoice's rate, as a correction of its accounts (pct. 330 alin. (1)), is the expert's question.
  - **The receivable or payable** is reduced at its carrying amount (`releaseAtCarrying`, as `bank.settle`: the invoice's rate, or the last month-end revaluation's; in proportion when it is carried at no single rate, a line D406 writes at the rate the receivable was recorded at and the rest in lei, `ro.d406.conversion`). A refund beyond it opens on 462 or 461 in the currency at the credit note's rate, its lei exactly its currency at that rate: a monetary item (pct. 315), revalued each month by `fx.revalue` (pct. 325) and paid back or collected by `bank.settle` at its carrying amount.
  - What the partner's lines release beyond the note's revenue or expense and VAT is a realized exchange difference (pct. 322), a line in lei only: 6651 for a loss, 7651 for a gain. Every other line records its currency amount and the rate it was converted at: the revenue or expense the books' rate, the VAT line the VAT rate, the partner's lines their carrying rate.
  - Worked: EXP-1 of 10 March 2026, 1,000.00 + 210.00 EUR at 4.9765 (VAT 1,045.07 lei); after March's revaluation at 4.9900, CN-2 of 8 April at 5.0100 credits 200.00 + 42.00 EUR: Dr 4111 −1,207.58 [−242.00 at 4.9900] / Cr 704 −1,002.00 [−200.00 at 5.0100] / Cr 4427 −209.01 [−42.00 at 4.9765] / Cr 7651 3.43, its tax entry −995.30 / −209.01 in April's VAT period (D300 rd. 9: −995 / −209; D394: the customer's L group −995 / −209). `fx.integration.test.ts` holds the other cases: a partial note on an unpaid invoice, a note on a collected invoice (a refund on 462 revalued and paid back), a supplier's note on a paid purchase (461); `invoices.integration.test.ts` a note on two rates and an invoice whose supply came first.
  - **Returns:** D300 and D394 take the note's tax entries in its own period, negative rows and groups included; D406 states, on the note's base line, its VAT (TaxAmount) at the invoice's rate with its own ExchangeRate, each amount its currency at its rate, and as its TaxBase the VAT base at the invoice's rate, in the ledger and on the invoice's lines ([D406](#declarations)). All three pass ANAF's validators.
  - **e-Factura:** `sales.issue` writes a credit note in a currency with BT-5, BT-6 = RON and the VAT total in lei (BT-111, a second TaxTotal) as CIUS-RO asks (6 November 2026): it computes that VAT itself, the note's VAT in the currency at the rate its invoice's base was determined at (`creditVatInLei` over `adjustmentVatRate`, read as `invoices.credit` reads it: the entries of the invoice's receivable, the rates of both tax points), so its recognition books it without `VAT_FX`; a stated amount other than that is refused (`VAT_FX`), and so is a note in another currency than its invoice's (`CURRENCY`). An imported note at another amount is still acknowledged at recognition (`VAT_FX`). The EN 16931 corpus holds such a note (`scripts/validators/official.test.ts`), and `sales.integration.test.ts` runs an issued one through the UBL 2.1 schema and the EN 16931 rules (CIUS-RO's when installed).
  - **Goods** (`stock`, 6 November 2026): they move in lei at cost, not at a rate, on lines without a currency amount. Goods a customer brings back return at the cost the original sale took them out at (Cr 371 / Dr 607 in red), as in lei. Goods back to the supplier leave at the cost their receipt entered at, the original's own moves of the item in the warehouse (the invoice's rate, so not the note's): the stock part's line becomes that cost, and what the payable releases at its carrying amount beyond it and the VAT is the exchange difference. Worked (`fx.integration.test.ts`): P-1 of 10 March, 10 bags for 500.00 + 105.00 EUR at 4.9765 (2,488.25 lei), revalued on 31 March at 4.9900; CN-P1 of 8 April returns 2 for 100.00 + 21.00 EUR: Dr 371 −497.65 / Dr 4426 −104.51 [−21.00 at 4.9765] / Cr 401 −603.79 [−121.00 at 4.9900] / Cr 7651 1.63, not 100.00 × 5.0100 = 501.00 on 371. Refused: the original's entry missing (`STOCK_RETURN_ORIGINAL`), an item its receipt did not bring into the warehouse (`STOCK_RETURN_RECEIPT`), a line of a stock part no item names (`STOCK_CREDIT_CHARGES`: a charge credited with the goods, whose lei cost the note does not give). D406's stock file lists them as 40 and 50, as in lei.
  - **Refused:** VAT on collection (`CASH_VAT_CURRENCY`: the system takes no invoice in a currency, so no credit note of one either).

### `bank.settle`: explanation of a bank movement

- **Input:**
  - the movement;
  - allocations to open items (amount in the movement currency);
  - lines to accounts without partners (fees 627, interest, own transfers 581, …);
  - new items of a person in lei (`opens`): an advance paid on 542 or another debtor (461) by a payment; an associate's money on 4551 or another creditor (462) by a receipt;
  - card settlements (`cards`, G2): the Z reports' card sums on 5125 the acquirer pays, less its fee on 627.
- **Rules:**
  - The explained total must equal the movement exactly.
  - Allocations must be in the open item's currency.
  - Partner accounts are settled only through open items.
- **Foreign currency:**
  - The bank line converts at the booking-date accounting rate.
  - Each allocation releases the item at its carrying amount: the currency settled at the rate the item is carried at (`carryingRate`: its own line's, the invoice's accounting rate, or the last month-end revaluation's; OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 322), the line stating that rate, so its lei are its currency amount at its rate, as D406 writes it (CurrencyAmount × ExchangeRate = Amount). Settling the full balance releases all of it: the lei earlier partial releases left by rounding go on a line of the item in lei only (`Rotunjirea valorii contabile în lei`). An item carried at no single rate (an invoice whose VAT in lei follows the VAT rate, BT-111, before any revaluation), or without its history, is released in proportion to its balance: that line's lei are no currency amount at a rate, and D406 writes it at the rate the item was recorded at and the rest in lei only (`ro.d406.conversion`, [D406](#declarations)). Allocations of one movement to the same item add up: each releases from what the earlier ones left, so those that settle the item between them release its whole carrying amount, with no residue in lei.
  - The difference from the bank's lei at the booking date's rate is a realized exchange gain (7651) or loss (6651), a line in lei only.
  - Paying a foreign-currency item from a RON account is not supported.

### `invoices.crossBorder`: services across the border

- **Document:** an imported invoice (not a credit note) whose counterparty is established abroad; for an EU customer, with a VAT code of its state (the general rule applies between businesses).
- **Input:** the account, `supply: services_general_rule` (services taxed where the customer is established, Cod fiscal art. 278 alin. (2); the exceptions and goods are other treatments), the deduction for purchases, and optionally the tax point when the service was provided.
- **Purchase:** expense and payable at the accounting rate; Romanian VAT self-assessed at the standard rate on the base at the BNR rate for VAT, collected (4427) and deducted (4426) at once, or added to the expense without deduction. Tax entries `RC-EU-SERV` or `RC-NONEU-SERV`, in both directions.
- **Sale:** receivable and revenue, no Romanian VAT; tax entry `EXP-EU-SERV` or `EXP-NONEU-SERV` with the base only.
- **Outside VAT** (`vat.status` `non_vat` at the tax point, V1):
  - **Purchase:** the VAT is owed all the same (art. 307 alin. (2)), at the standard rate, and is never deducted: `deduction` must be `none` (`DEDUCTION`). It is owed to the budget through the special return (D301, art. 324), on the account `ro.vat.special.payable_account` gives (4423), as an open item of the tax authority (`authorityId`, required) due by the 25th of the next month (`ro.deadline.D301`): Dr expense net / Cr 401; Dr expense VAT (non-deductible) / Cr 4423. Its tax entries keep the codes `RC-EU-SERV` or `RC-NONEU-SERV`, without a VAT line, marked `regime: special`.
  - **Sale** to an EU business: no Romanian VAT, as for a payer (`EXP-EU-SERV`).
  - **The art. 317 code** (`vat.eu`, `special`): needed before buying services from a supplier established in another member state or supplying them to a business there (art. 317 alin. (1) lit. b)–c)). Without it the booking warns (`ART317_REQUIRED`, ack: the VAT is owed anyway); with the fact unknown it asks (`profile.fact` `vat.eu`). Services from outside the Union need no code (norme pct. 90 alin. (4)).
- **Refused:** a domestic partner, an invoice that already charges VAT, an EU customer without a VAT code, a non-payer's purchase that deducts or names no authority.
- **Open for review:** which e-Factura VAT category a Romanian supplier uses for services sold to EU businesses (ANAF's guide reserves AE for art. 331); the tax point of continuous services; the account of a non-payer's D301 VAT (4423 directly, or 4427 settled into 4423: `ro.vat.special.payable_account`'s review packet).

### `receipts.recognize`: a fiscal receipt (V3)

A fiscal receipt (bon fiscal) recorded with `documents.record` (kind `receipt.received`) is booked with its payment in one entry. Code in core `src/ro/receipts/receipts.ts`.

- **A simplified invoice or not.** A receipt of a company registered for VAT is a simplified invoice, whose VAT is deducted under art. 297–299, when the cash register printed the company's code on it and its value, VAT included, is at most `ro.vat.simplified_invoice.limit_eur` (100 euro) at the BNR rate valid on its date (art. 319 alin. (12) lit. a), art. 290 alin. (2), `ro.fx.rate_date.vat`; norme pct. 69 alin. (3) and (6)). The limit has not changed since the Code took effect: the portal's consolidated form notes for alin. (12) only the lit. c) OG nr. 22/2025 added from 01.09.2025 (receipts of the small-enterprise scheme). Between 100 and 400 euro only a minister's order for the supplier allows a simplified invoice (alin. (13)); the engine knows of none.
  - The code printed without the RO prefix (`CIF: 18547290`) deducts behind a confirmation (`RECEIPT_BUYER_CODE`, ack): the norms ask for the VAT code, an interpretation awaiting the expert (`ro.vat.simplified_invoice.limit_eur`'s packet).
  - Without the euro rate the limit is not guessed (`FX_RATE`).
- **Input:** the receipt, one expense account (6xx) for every rate or `allocations` (each rate's amount with VAT split over accounts, each with its deduction and vehicle), `deduction` (`full`, `none`, `limited`), `vehicle`, the `payment` and, for an employee, the expense report (`reportEvidenceId`, required).
- **Refused deductions** (`DEDUCTION`, block, with `reason`): a non-payer (`non_payer`), a receipt without the company's code (`no_buyer_code`, with the info `RECEIPT_NOT_SIMPLIFIED_INVOICE`), one over the limit (`over_limit`, with `RECEIPT_INVOICE_EXPECTED`, ack: the seller issues an e-Factura for it; if it arrives, reverse this booking and book the invoice). Booked with `deduction: none`, the VAT stays in the expense and no tax entry is recorded: such a receipt is no document for the VAT return.
- **The usual conditions:** each rate standard or reduced at the receipt's date (`REDUCED_RATE`, ack; another is `VAT_RATE`); the seller inactive on the day deducts nothing (`SUPPLIER_INACTIVE`, art. 11 alin. (7), no exceptions on receipts); a vehicle's fuel or upkeep follows the answer about its use (`vehicle.use`, art. 298) exactly as `invoices.recognize` does: `VEHICLE_USE`, `VEHICLE_DEDUCTION`, `VEHICLE_UNNAMED` on 6022 or a limited share without a vehicle.
- **Several rates:** one 4426 line and one tax entry per rate (`vat:S-21`, `vat:S-11`); each part takes its rate's VAT in proportion to its amount.
- **Payment, booked with the receipt (one entry per receipt):**

  | `payment` | Credit | Then |
  |---|---|---|
  | `cash` (account 531x) | the cash desk, memo `BF <number>`: the cash register lists it | Legea nr. 70/2015's 5,000 lei per operator (`CASH_LIMIT`, ack); the desk never negative (`CASH_NEGATIVE`) |
  | `card` | 401, a payable of the seller | the bank movement settles it (`bank.settle`, or a template matching by amount) |
  | `advance` (an open item on 542) | 542, settling the employee's advance | more than the advance holds is refused (`EXCESS`); what is left returns through `cash.record` |
  | `employee` (an individual) | 542, a new item of the employee in credit: what the company owes them | repaid through `cash.record` or `bank.settle`, allocating the item |

  An employee's own money stays on 542 in credit: 4282 is the claim account (Alte creanțe în legătură cu personalul), and 4281 has no open items in the books' chart (giving it some would change existing companies' accounts, a migration).
- **Postings:** Dr 6xx gross less the VAT deducted / Dr 4426 the VAT deducted / Cr the payment. A deductible receipt's tax entry is input `S-<rate>` with `simplified: receipt`, the receipt's number and register: D300 rows 24–25 and 31, D394's rezumat2 (`bazaBFAI`, `TVABFAI`), SAF-T like a purchase at that rate. D406 needs the seller's address (`parties.recordDetails`): a receipt has no UBL to read it from.
- **The profit tax:** the VAT not deducted is part of the expense and follows its account's classification (`profit.account`): deductible when the expense is (art. 25 alin. (1)), a vehicle's 50% applied to it too (norme pct. 16 alin. (3)). The explanation shows it (`profitTax.vatInExpense`).
- **Identity and copies:** `documents.record` identifies a receipt by seller, cash register, number and date, so a second photo joins the first one's group and cannot be booked twice (`SUBJECT_EXISTS`). Without the register it has no identity: the reviewer confirms it is not booked already (`RECEIPT_IDENTITY`, ack). A receipt and an invoice of one purchase are documents of different kinds and are not grouped.
- **Not covered:** receipts in another currency (`CURRENCY`), fixed assets and stock bought on a receipt (6xx only), single-entry books (`SINGLE_ENTRY_EXCLUDED`: what a receipt is for a PFA's income tax is not built).

### `invoices.reverseCharge`: the domestic reverse charge (V3)

A domestic invoice in category AE (e-Factura's „taxare inversă”) under Codul fiscal art. 331, sale or purchase. Code in core `src/ro/receipts/reverse-charge.ts`.

- **What it covers** (`ro.vat.reverse_charge.domestic`, read at the tax point, the regime of the exigibility, norme pct. 109 alin. (17)): waste and scrap (lit. a), timber (b), cereals and technical plants by NC code (c, norme pct. 109 alin. (7)), greenhouse-gas certificates (d), electricity (e) and natural gas (l) to a trader, green certificates (f), real estate taxed by law or option (g), gold (h), mobile phones (i), integrated circuits (j), consoles, tablets and laptops (k). Letters c)–f) and i)–l) apply until 31 December 2026 (alin. (6), last set by OUG nr. 85/2022): the version ends then, so 2027 is a `RULE_GAP` until the December acts are read. Phones, circuits and laptops are reverse-charged only when, together on one invoice, they reach 22,500 lei without VAT (alin. (7), norme pct. 109 alin. (19)): `REVERSE_CHARGE_THRESHOLD` otherwise.
- **Input:** the invoice and its `parts`: each part's category (`waste`, `timber`, `cereals` with `nc`, `emission_certificates`, `electricity`, `green_certificates`, `real_estate`, `gold`, `mobile_phones`, `integrated_circuits`, `consoles_tablets_laptops`, `natural_gas`), account and amount in lei; for a purchase the rate the company self-assesses (`standard`, or `reduced` with `REDUCED_RATE`) and the deduction (`full`, `none`).
- **Conditions:** both parties registered for VAT (art. 331 alin. (1)): the company's `vat.status`, the partner's RO code on the invoice, and ANAF's snapshot when one is recorded (`REVERSE_CHARGE_PARTIES`; without a snapshot `REVERSE_CHARGE_PARTNER`, ack). Electricity and gas to a trader in ANAF's list of form 089 (`REVERSE_CHARGE_TRADER`, ack); real estate taxed by law or option (`REVERSE_CHARGE_REAL_ESTATE`, ack).
- **Postings:**

  | Case | Lines | Tax entries |
  |---|---|---|
  | Purchase | Dr 6xx or 2xx / Cr 401 (404 for assets); Cr 4427 VAT / Dr 4426 deducted, or Dr the account when not deducted (norme pct. 109 alin. (1): 4426 = 4427) | output and input `TI-A-<rate>` per rate, category and product: D300 rd. 12 and 12.1–12.5, repeated on rd. 26; rd. 31 the deducted; D394 C |
  | Sale | Dr 4111 / Cr 70x | output `TI-L`, base only: D300 rd. 13; D394 V |

  Each entry carries the category, its letter, its D394 product code (22–31, 36; the NC code for cereals) and whether it is goods or services. The D300 header's `bifa_cereale`, `bifa_mob`, `bifa_disp` and `bifa_cons` are set from the period's reverse-charged supplies.
- **Goods that need stock** (waste and metals, timber, cereals, gold, phones and electronics) are booked without stock movements until the stock module (G1) exists: a purchase on a stock account (3xx) is refused, a sale books its revenue without its cost.
- **Issuing:** `sales.issue` takes `reverseCharge: { category, nc? }` for lines in category AE without VAT, with the mention „taxare inversă” (the AE breakdown's reason BT-120 or a note; `REVERSE_CHARGE_MENTION` otherwise); the preview refuses a mixed or foreign-currency invoice, a buyer without its RO code, a category not in force and phones under the threshold. Its recognition is proposed as `invoices.reverseCharge`.
- **SAF-T (D406):** an acquisition is one operation, its collected VAT and its deduction reported once, with the acquisition, by its deduction: Achizitii ded 100% 3009xx (300906 at 21%, 300907 at 11%, 300901, 300902, 300903 at 19, 9 and 5%) or Achizitii neded 3509xx, "Achiziţii de bunuri şi servicii supuse măsurilor de simplificare"; the collected side (`TI-A-<rate>` output) has no code of its own. A supply (`TI-L`) is Livrari 310312, "supuse masurilor de simplificare (taxare inversa)", without VAT. Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator from the books.
- **Not covered:** an invoice mixing AE with taxed goods (`REVERSE_CHARGE_MIXED`, the norms' own example), credit notes, advances, prepaid amounts and foreign currency; an invoice that wrongly charges VAT on these goods (`VAT_CHARGED`: the deduction would be lost, norme pct. 109 alin. (4)).

### `vat.lateRegistration` and `vat.capitalGoods`: a change of VAT regime (V1)

A company outside VAT that exceeds the small-enterprise threshold (395,000 lei, art. 310 alin. (1)) must ask to register by the day it exceeds it; the registration is valid from that day, whether it is asked on time, late (alin. (6^2) lit. a)) or made by ANAF (art. 316 alin. (1^1) lit. b), f)), and the normal regime applies from the transaction that exceeds it (alin. (6)). The `vat.status` determination proposes the registration from that day; `vat.registration {year}` (read) shows the crossing day and invoice, the supplies of that day before it (which may stay exempt), whether the books show the registration late (sales without VAT after the crossing day), and the VAT owed for it.

- **`vat.lateRegistration`** (art. 310 alin. (6^2)–(6^3)): the VAT the company should have collected from the crossing day to the last sale it made without VAT (`from`, `until`), less what it could have deducted, booked on the first return after registration.
  - It reads the books over the range: revenue on 70x (the supplies), less the supplies abroad (`EXP-…` bases) and the supplies of the first day booked before the transaction that exceeded the threshold, which the company leaves exempt (`excluded`, alin. (6), last sentence).
  - **The price**: the customers did not pay VAT, so the price is taken to include it (21 × 100 / 121, norme pct. 36 lit. b) pct. 4), with the company's statement that it cannot recover it (`considerationEvidenceId`). VAT added on top of the price is invoiced to customers by correction invoices (art. 330), which are not covered (`CONSIDERATION_ADDED`).
  - **The deduction**: every purchase of the range that charged VAT and was booked without deduction is listed with what may be deducted now (`full`, `limited`, `none`, art. 297–298); one left out blocks (`PURCHASE_UNDECIDED`).
  - **Booking** (`ro.vat.late_registration`): Dr 635 / Cr 4427 for the VAT collected (tax entry `REG-310`, D300 rd. 16) and Dr 4426 / Cr 7588 for the VAT deducted (rd. 33), in the posting date's VAT period; `vat.settle` settles them with the period.
  - **Refused:** a registration not recorded from `from` (`vat.status` `normal_vat`, with the certificate), an invoice with VAT inside the range (`RANGE_CHARGED`), a change of the standard rate inside it (`RATE_CHANGE`), stock or assets in progress at the crossing day (`STOCK_ADJUSTMENT`, art. 304: not covered).
  - The VAT ANAF establishes by decision (alin. (6^1)) is booked from the decision by the tax account (`tax.decision`, an assessment expensed on 635; its rd. 39 when unpaid), not here.
- **`vat.capitalGoods`** (art. 305 alin. (5) lit. a) and c)): on entering the normal regime from the exemption, or leaving it, the capital goods' VAT is adjusted once for the whole adjustment period left, the change's year included: a fifth (a twentieth for buildings) of the VAT not deducted (entering) or deducted (leaving) for each year left of the period that starts on 1 January of the acquisition's year (`ro.vat.capital_goods`).
  - **Input:** the event, the change's date, every tangible fixed asset (21x) held then with its acquisition invoice and VAT period (one left out blocks, `CAPITAL_GOOD_UNLISTED`), the inventory at the change (norme pct. 83 alin. (7), (11)).
  - **Booking:** entering, Dr 4426 / Cr 7588 in the first return after registration; leaving, Dr 635 / Cr 4426 in the last return (its tax point is the day before the cancellation), both tax entry `ADJ-305`, D300 rd. 34 (`R31_2`), signed.
  - **Stock, services not used and advances** (art. 304; norme pct. 78, pct. 83 alin. (7) lit. a), d), (11) lit. a)), V2: the inventory's stock and assets in progress, per account (3x, 231) at their value and the VAT rate of their acquisition (`stock`, norme pct. 78 alin. (13), the reviewer confirming the rates, `STOCK_RATE`), each account equal to its balance the day before (`STOCK_UNLISTED`); the intangible assets not wholly depreciated, for the VAT of their value left (`services`, pct. 78 alin. (2), each listed, `SERVICE_UNLISTED`); on leaving, the advances paid still open, for the VAT deducted on what is left of them (`advances`, adding up to 4091 and 4092, `ADVANCE_UNLISTED`). Deferred expenses (471) are services used when bought and are not adjusted (an info). Booked Dr 635 / Cr 4426 leaving, Dr 4426 / Cr 7588 entering (`ro.vat.stock_adjustment`), tax entries `ADJ-304` by rate, rd. 34.
  - **Assets bought before 2016** (art. 306, norme pct. 80): a movable asset wholly depreciated, or with a fiscal life of five years or more whose period ended, is not adjusted; a building bought after the accession follows art. 305 (20 years from its year).
  - **Not covered (UNSUPPORTED):** a movable asset bought before 2016 and not wholly depreciated with a fiscal life under five years (`ART306`: the proportion to the value left); a building bought before the accession (`ART332`); stock held at sale price or with price differences or impairment (308, 348, 368, 378, 388, 39x: `STOCK_DIFFERENCES`); advances at a registration.
- **Leaving VAT** (art. 310 alin. (7), art. 316 alin. (11) lit. g), (13)): the cancellation is recorded as `vat.status` `non_vat` from the decision's communication, with the decision; the `vat.status` determination checks it is allowed (neither the previous year's turnover nor this year's up to it above the threshold). The last D300 covers the period up to the day before, due by the 25th after the month of the communication, and holds the adjustments; `vat.registration` shows it and the capital goods' adjustment to book.
- **Parameters** (all `extracted`, from 2026, reviewed through 2026-12-31, each with a review packet for the expert contabil, `src/ro/reviews/specs/nonpayers.ts`): `ro.vat.special.payable_account` (4423), `ro.vat.late_registration` (rd. 16 and 33; 635 and 7588), `ro.vat.registration.annualized` (days), `ro.vat.capital_goods` (5 and 20 years, rd. 34; 635 and 7588); and `ro.deadline.D301`.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/nonpayers/agent.ts`): the documents it takes (the art. 316 and art. 317 certificates, the cancellation decision, ANAF's VAT decision, the statement that VAT cannot be recovered from customers, the inventory, the foreign suppliers' invoices), its questions (`determination.confirm` for `vat.status` and `vat.eu`, `profile.fact`), its detectors (`threshold.approaching`, `determination.missing` for a registration or a code due, `obligation.due` for D301, D390 and the last D300) and no template fields (`invoices.crossBorder` is not a template treatment yet).

### Exempt supplies, the pro-rata and capital goods (V2)

The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/exempt` (`codes.ts`, `prorata.ts`, `capital-goods.ts`, `facts.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `agent.ts`) and the treatments `invoices.recognize`, `invoices.credit`, `vat.proRata` (`src/ro/treatments/prorata.ts`) and `vat.capitalGoods`; the engine module is `modules/exempt.ts`. Sources: the Fiscal Code in ANAF's consolidated text (art. 266, 292, 294–297, 300, 304–306, 319 alin. (20) lit. l) and norme pct. 57–59, 70, 78–80), the portal's 2025 consolidation for the repeal of art. 305 alin. (9) (OUG nr. 78/2024), OPANAF nr. 174/2026 (D300 rd. 1, 14, 15, 29, 31, 34 and the pro-rata header), OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 (D394's lists) and the EN 16931 VATEX code list (CEN validation artifacts 1.3.17).

- **Exempt purchases** (categories E and G): no VAT, the base on its account, a tax entry `EX-IN` with the base only (D300 rd. 29, "achiziții scutite de taxă sau neimpozabile"), carrying the supplier's exemption reason (`reason`: its VATEX code, BT-121, else its text, BT-120) and category. Insurance, medical services, financial services and exempt rents are booked so. A purchase in K is an intra-community acquisition of goods, self-assessed by the buyer: refused (`INTRA_EU_GOODS`). A credit note on an exempt purchase reverses its base under the original's code. D394 lists only taxable operations and acquisitions from persons not registered for VAT: an exempt purchase from a registered supplier is not listed; one from a supplier not registered is an acquisition of type N ([D394](#declarations)).
- **Purchases outside the scope of VAT** (category O: a supplier not registered for VAT, under the special scheme of art. 310, or an operation not taxable): booked like an exempt purchase, without VAT, with a tax entry `O` holding the base only (and a credit note's negative base, where the original recorded one). D300 rd. 29 ("neimpozabilă sau supusă unui regim special de taxă", OPANAF nr. 174/2026), D406 308302 like `EX-IN`, D394 type N from a supplier not registered, not listed from a registered one.
- **Exempt sales** name their legal basis (`exemption`), which decides the right of deduction (art. 297 alin. (4)) and the row:

  | Basis | Category | Code | D300 | SAF-T |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `art.292(1)`, `art.292(2)(a)`–`(g)`: interest-general activities, financial, insurance, gambling, stamps, rent of property, old buildings and land, goods of an exempt activity | E | `EX-292` | rd. 15 | 310326 |
  | `art.297(4)(d)`: financial or insurance services to a customer outside the Union (a customer inside it acknowledges `EXEMPTION_PROOF`: only with exported goods) | E | `EX-294` | rd. 14 | 310314 |
  | `art.294(1)(c)-(p)`, `art.295`, `art.296` | E | `EX-294` | rd. 14 | 310314 |
  | `art.294(1)(a)-(b)`: exports of goods | G or E | `EX-EXPORT` | rd. 14 | 310313 |
  | `art.294(2)(a)`: intra-community supply of goods, to a customer with a VAT code of another member state (`EXEMPTION_CUSTOMER`) | K | `EX-IC` | rd. 1, D390 L | 310301 |

  - The invoice's exemption reason must agree with the basis (`EXEMPTION_REASON`: a VATEX code of another basis, or none, art. 319 alin. (20) lit. l)); a reason given only as text is confirmed by the reviewer (`EXEMPTION_TEXT`); an export and an intra-community supply acknowledge their proof (`EXEMPTION_PROOF`, the documents of OMFP nr. 103/2016). A basis must fit the category (`EXEMPTION_CATEGORY`); one exempt category per invoice.
  - `sales.issue` takes the same `exemption` for lines in E, K or G (`EXEMPTION_BASIS` without it), passes it to the recognition, and the invoice's checks ask each exempt breakdown for its reason (BR-E-10, BR-IC-10, BR-G-10) and an intra-community supply for both VAT codes (BR-IC-02).
  - The pro-rata's numerator and the VAT turnover of art. 322 alin. (2) (the VAT period) and art. 282 alin. (3^1) count the exempt supplies with the right of deduction; the closure lists D390 for a month with an intra-community supply.
- **The option to tax** a rent or a supply of property (art. 292 alin. (3); norme pct. 57–59): the sale is invoiced with VAT (S) and recognized with the notification filed (`optionEvidenceId`, recorded as `option: art.292(3)` on its tax entries). Its effect on deduction is the building's use: a person states the share used for operations with the right of deduction (`capitalGood.use`), and `vat.capitalGoods` adjusts its VAT (pct. 57 alin. (8)–(9)).
- **The pro-rata** (art. 300) of a company with taxable and exempt-without-deduction supplies:
  - **The provisional pro-rata** is the year's fact `vat.prorata` (a whole percent, from 1 January or from the day the company became mixed, with its notification to ANAF by 25 January or the first return: alin. (9)–(10)); a fact of an earlier year does not carry. `special` (a pro-rata the Ministry approved or imposed, alin. (16)–(18)) is UNSUPPORTED; `opted_out` (alin. (20)) makes the purchases for both kinds deduct nothing.
  - **Each purchase** is allocated: `deduction: full` (destined only to operations with the right of deduction, alin. (3)), `none` (only to the others, alin. (4)), or `prorata` (both, or not known, alin. (5)), which deducts the provisional share, half up to the ban, the rest in the expense. A mixed company's purchase allocated directly is confirmed by the reviewer (`PRORATA_DIRECT`). The tax entry records the VAT of the parts under the pro-rata and what they deducted (`prorata`, `prorataTax`, `prorataDeducted`). Without the year's fact the part asks for it (`PRORATA_MISSING`, `profile.fact` `vat.prorata/<year>`). Refused: a pro-rata under VAT on collection (`PRORATA_CASH_VAT`, norme pct. 79 alin. (18)–(20)), a pro-rata part on a vehicle (the answer's `full` or `limited` decides it), a credit note in another year than its purchase (`PRORATA_CREDIT_YEAR`). `prorata` is a template field.
  - **`vat.proRata {year, date, evidenceId, accessory, subsidies, goods}`** books the year-end adjustment in the year's last VAT period, or the last one before a cancellation in the year (alin. (14) lit. b)). The definitive pro-rata (alin. (6)–(8)) is the supplies with the right of deduction (taxable, `EX-294`, `EX-EXPORT`, `EX-IC`, and abroad, which the reviewer confirms would be taxable in Romania, `PRORATA_ABROAD`) over all supplies, `EX-292` included, and the budget sums of alin. (6) lit. b) (`subsidies`, with their decision), by tax point in the year (VAT on collection at its general-rule tax point, alin. (19)); exempt financial or real-estate supplies a person shows accessory (`accessory`, with the analysis of norme pct. 70 alin. (4), acknowledged `PRORATA_ACCESSORY`) are left out (alin. (7) lit. c)). It is rounded up to the next whole percent, a whole one kept (`ro.vat.prorata`: "se rotunjește până la cifra unităților imediat următoare"). The year's VAT under the pro-rata times the definitive, less what the provisional deducted, per VAT code: Dr 4426 / Cr 7588 for more, Dr 635 / Cr 4426 for less, tax entries `ADJ-300` with the definitive, rd. 34. Capital goods acquired in an earlier year of their period under a pro-rata are adjusted to this year's (alin. (15)): every tangible asset in its period is listed (`goods`, `CAPITAL_GOOD_UNLISTED`), those deducted otherwise are left to art. 305 alin. (4) lit. a). A year without supplies (alin. (10) lit. d), (13)) is UNSUPPORTED (`PRORATA_NO_SUPPLIES`); nothing to adjust is `NOTHING`.
  - **`vat.proRata.compute {year, accessory?, subsidies?}`** (read) shows the provisional, the supplies by their right of deduction, the definitive, the adjustment per code, what is booked, and the definitive as next year's provisional (alin. (9)).
  - **The closure** (`scope.prorata@<year>`): a year whose books hold `EX-292` supplies while registered needs the year's `vat.prorata` (NEEDS_EVIDENCE `FACT_UNKNOWN` `vat.prorata/<year>`), and `special` is UNSUPPORTED (`PRORATA_SPECIAL`).
- **Capital goods in the general case** (art. 305 alin. (4)–(5), (7); norme pct. 79): `vat.capitalGoods` takes, beside the change of regime, the events
  - `use`: the share of a good's use for operations with the right of deduction changed (a lease of part of a building exempt, a good moved to an exempt activity or back). The share is a person's answer (`capitalGood.use`, subject the asset, with its evidence: the lease, the option notified, a decision), cited in the proposal's basis; without it the proposal asks (`CAPITAL_GOOD_USE`). The year's fifth (twentieth for buildings) of the VAT at the share, less a fifth of what was deducted, less what the year was already adjusted for the good, in the event's period (`date` the answer's `from`, `USE_DATE`);
  - `year`: the same, in the year's last period, for each later year the use holds (pct. 79 alin. (1)); a year adjusted at its event, or a use equal to the initial deduction, adjusts nothing;
  - `supply` (`saleDocumentId`): exempt (E), once for the period left with the year (alin. (5) lit. a)); taxable after a limited deduction, once for the period left, at most the VAT collected (lit. c), alin. (4) lit. c)); taxable after a full deduction, nothing;
  - `cessation` (`cause`): a shortage is adjusted once for the period left (alin. (4) lit. d), pct. 79 alin. (10)); a good scrapped, destroyed or stolen with its proof is not (pct. 79 alin. (11), (17), acknowledged `CESSATION_PROOF`).
  - Each good's adjustment is its own tax entry (`ADJ-305`, attributes `assetId`, `year`, `share`, `rate`), so the register reads them back. Goods deducted under a pro-rata are adjusted by `vat.proRata` (`CAPITAL_GOOD_PRORATA`). The threshold of norme pct. 79 alin. (4) (1.000 lei "în sensul art. 305 alin. (9)") is read as gone with alin. (9), repealed from 01.07.2024 (`ro.vat.capital_goods.minimum` = 0); the expert decides.
  - **`vat.capitalGoods.register {date?}`** (read): the register of norme pct. 79 alin. (2), each tangible fixed asset with its acquisition's date, base, VAT and deduction (the purchase invoice's tax entries), its period, whether deducted under a pro-rata, the initial share, the use stated and the adjustments posted, and its state (`adjusting`, `unchanged`, `expired`, `no_vat`, `disposed`).
- **Parameters** (all `extracted`, from 2026, reviewed through 2026-12-31, each with a review packet, `src/ro/reviews/specs/exempt.ts`): `ro.vat.prorata` (rounding up to the unit, rd. 34, 635 and 7588), `ro.vat.stock_adjustment` (rd. 34, 635 and 7588), `ro.vat.capital_goods.minimum` (0 lei); `ro.vat.capital_goods` now cites the general case too.
- **SAF-T (D406):** `EX-292` 310326, `EX-294` 310314, `EX-EXPORT` 310313, `EX-IC` 310301, `EX-IN` 308302, the adjustments at the rate of the VAT they adjust: `ADJ-300` 307327/307328/307311 ("Ajustări conform pro-rata"), `ADJ-305` and `ADJ-304` 307329/307330/307321 ("Ajustări de taxă"). A purchase deducted under a pro-rata (its whole tax entry, `prorata`) takes its code from ANAF's sheet Achizitii baserate ("operațiuni de achiziție cu pro-rată", 36xxxx, extracted from the pinned workbook of 16.02.2026, the same as 05.02.2026's): 361104 at 21%, 361105 at 11%, 361101 at 19%, and 361305, 361306, 361301 from a supplier applying VAT on collection; the TaxTable gives the code the provisional pro-rata as its BaseRate, a fraction of 1 (60% is 0.6000: ANAF's guide, section 2.5, and answers v1.2, MF.TT.11), every other code 1. A purchase only partly under the pro-rata has no single code (`D406_TAX_CODE`). Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator from the books.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/exempt/agent.ts`): document types (exempt invoices, the option's notification, the pro-rata's notification and computation, the document of a capital good's use, the inventory), questions (`profile.fact` for `vat.prorata`, `capitalGood.use`), detectors (`vat.prorata`: a mixed year without its provisional, blocking, or whose last month has come without the adjustment, blocking once the year ended; `vat.capitalGoods`: a good whose stated use differs from its initial deduction in a year of its period without the year's adjustment), and the template field `invoices.recognize.deduction` (`prorata`).
- **Not covered:** the special pro-rata; the pro-rata under VAT on collection; the regularization of years without supplies; a partially taxable person (art. 300 alin. (2)); a good whose use turns undetermined (norme pct. 79 alin. (16): pro-rata goods are `vat.proRata`'s); transformations and modernizations of buildings as capital goods of their own (art. 305 alin. (1) lit. a), (3) lit. d)); the sale of a fixed asset itself (7583 is refused by `invoices.recognize`, so the `supply` event reads the invoice without its booking); a purchase only partly under the pro-rata in D406. Goods across the border are G3's ([below](#goods-across-the-border-g3-intra-community-supplies-and-acquisitions-imports-exports)).

### Special VAT schemes (V4): the margin schemes and the agricultural flat rate

The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/schemes` (`margin.ts`, `invoices.ts`, `travel.ts`, `agriculture.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `agent.ts`); the engine module is `modules/schemes.ts`. Sources: the Fiscal Code in ANAF's consolidated text (art. 311 and norme pct. 85, art. 312 and pct. 86, art. 315^1 and pct. 87^1, art. 319 alin. (18), (20) lit. k), n), o) and pct. 96), OPANAF nr. 174/2026 (D300 rd. 9, 27–29, 31) and ANAF's D394 structure of 15.09.2025 (LS, AS and the margins' fields of `informatii`). OSS and IOSS are another module's.

**Tax codes:** `MS-311-<rate>` a travel agency's VAT of a period's margin; `MS-311-L` its sale under the scheme, at its price, no VAT of its own; `MS-311-A` a single service bought from another agency under the scheme; `MS-312-<rate>` an item sold under the margin scheme, base its margin less its VAT; `MS-312-A` an item bought for resale; `FR-315` the flat-rate compensation paid.

**Travel agencies (art. 311).** An agency acting in its own name for the traveller with goods and services of other taxable persons makes a single service, taxed on its margin, without VAT shown on the invoice (alin. (2)–(4), (8)).
- **`margin.sale`** (`scheme: travel`): an issued invoice without VAT (`MARGIN_VAT_SHOWN` otherwise) with the mention «regimul marjei - agenții de turism» (art. 319 alin. (20) lit. n); the engine reads it in the exemption reason, BT-120, and asks the reviewer to confirm it otherwise, `MARGIN_MENTION`). Books Dr 4111 / Cr 704 at the price; tax entry `MS-311-L` (base the price, no row of D300, D394 LS).
- **The costs** for the traveller's direct benefit are booked with `invoices.recognize` and `deduction: none` (art. 311 alin. (6)): their VAT stays in the expense. A single service bought from another agency under the scheme (norme pct. 85 alin. (7)) is **`margin.purchase`** (`scheme: travel`): Dr 6xx / Cr 401, `MS-311-A` (D300 rd. 29, D394 AS); its supplier must be registered for VAT.
- **`margin.travel {period, round, date, account, evidenceId, trips}`**, subject `margin.travel:<period>:<round>`: the VAT of the single services supplied in a VAT period, computed per period as norme pct. 85 alin. (4) says (`ro.vat.margin.travel`: `basis: period`): the sales of each trip (its `margin.sale` invoices, advances included) less its costs (each purchase invoice's total with VAT, or the part of it the trip bears), together for the period; VAT = margin × 21 ÷ 121 (the standard rate at the date, "procedeul sutei mărite"; the norms still write 19 × 100 / 119), base the margin less it. Dr the revenue account / Cr 4427, tax entry `MS-311-21` on D300 rd. 9 (OPANAF nr. 174/2026: "operaţiunile supuse regimurilor speciale … determinate pe baza situaţiilor de calcul"), its attributes holding the period's sales, costs and margin (D394 `incasari_ag`, `costuri_ag`, `marja_ag` the base, `tva_ag`) and the documents counted.
  - Each trip enters the period it is supplied in (`supplied`, `TRAVEL_SUPPLIED` otherwise); a trip at a loss reduces the others' margin (an info, `TRAVEL_TRIP_NEGATIVE`); the reviewer confirms the costs are direct ones and the sales complete (`TRAVEL_COSTS`).
  - Refused: a cost booked with a deduction (`TRAVEL_COST_DEDUCTED`) or not booked, a sale not booked under the scheme, a document counted twice or in an earlier computation that stands (`TRAVEL_DOCUMENT_USED`, read from the ledgers of the period's year and the year before), a trip without bought costs (own means, norme pct. 85 alin. (2): `TRAVEL_OWN_MEANS`), a date outside the period, a company outside VAT.
  - **UNSUPPORTED:** a trip with goods or services outside the Union (art. 311 alin. (5), its part exempt: `TRAVEL_OUTSIDE_EU`), a period whose margin is negative (the texts do not say: `TRAVEL_MARGIN_NEGATIVE`), the normal regime by option (alin. (10)–(11)), the agency as intermediary (alin. (9)).
- **The worklist** (`margin.travel`, subject the VAT period): sales under the scheme that no computation counted, once their period ended, warning, blocking from the 25th of the next month; its step proposes `margin.travel` for the period. The month close warns of the same (`TRAVEL_MARGIN_UNCOMPUTED`).

**Second-hand goods, works of art, collectors' items and antiques (art. 312).** A dealer resells, taxing each supply on its margin, the goods it bought in the Union from a person not taxable, from a taxable person whose supply was exempt under art. 292 alin. (2) lit. g), from a small enterprise selling a capital good, or from another dealer that applied the scheme (alin. (2)).
- **Individual items, not the stock register.** A dealer states that it keeps only individual items, each its purchase document, without quantity stock: `activity.goods` `margin_goods` (a `profile.fact`; `yes` is refused here, `MARGIN_STOCK`: the stock register of G1 does not hold margin-scheme items yet, and its close check leaves their tagged lines apart). Each item is on 371, its lines tagged `margin:<goods>:<purchase document>`; the register is built on read from those lines and their tax entries. A purchase document with several lines is one item (`MARGIN_ITEM_LINES`, ack).
- **`margin.purchase`** (`scheme: goods`, `goods` `second_hand`, `works_of_art`, `collectibles_antiques`, `supplier` `individual`, `dealer`, `exempt`, `small_enterprise`): a document without VAT (`MARGIN_VAT_CHARGED` otherwise); Dr 371 / Cr 401 at the purchase price, `MS-312-A` (D300 rd. 29). From an individual the document is the dealer's self-invoice (norme pct. 86 alin. (6) lit. d), confirmed, `MARGIN_SELF_INVOICE`); a dealer is registered for VAT and states the mention (`MARGIN_SUPPLIER`, `MARGIN_MENTION`); an exempt supply is in category E (`MARGIN_EXEMPT_SUPPLY`); a small enterprise sells only its capital goods (`MARGIN_SMALL_ENTERPRISE`).
- **`margin.sale`** (`scheme: goods`, `items: [{purchaseDocumentId, price}]`, `account` 707, `costAccount` 607): each item's VAT on its own margin, price less purchase price, by the hundred-plus method, a margin of zero or less giving none and reducing nothing else (norme pct. 86 alin. (4)–(5), `ro.vat.margin.goods`: `basis: supply`, `negative: zero`). Dr 4111 the price / Cr 707 the price less the VAT / Cr 4427 the VAT; Dr 607 / Cr 371 the purchase price, the item leaving the goods; one tax entry `MS-312-21` per item (D300 rd. 9; D394 LS at its price and `pret_vanzare`, `pret_cumparare`, `marja_antic` the base, `tva_antic`). The invoice shows no VAT and the mention of art. 319 alin. (20) lit. o) (`MARGIN_MENTION`, ack). Refused: an item not bought under the scheme (`MARGIN_ITEM_UNKNOWN`), sold already or whose purchase was reversed (`MARGIN_ITEM_SOLD`), prices not adding up to the invoice (`ALLOCATION`).
- **The normal regime instead** (alin. (7)): `regime: normal`, the invoice at the standard rate on the whole price (`S-21`), the item leaving 371 all the same; a reduced rate or a mixed invoice is refused (`MARGIN_NORMAL_RATE`).
- **The global margin** is not in the Romanian text (art. 312 alin. (4) and pct. 86 alin. (4) tax each supply; the Directive's art. 318 is an option Romania did not take): not offered.
- **`margin.goods.register {from?, to?}`** (read): each item with its purchase (document, date, supplier kind, price) and its sale (document, regime, price, margin, base, VAT), and per VAT period and rate the base and VAT of the supplies (norme pct. 86 alin. (6) lit. c)).
- **UNSUPPORTED:** the option of alin. (3) (imported works of art, collectors' items and antiques, works bought from their authors or other taxable persons) with the deduction of alin. (8)–(9); public auctions (alin. (15), pct. 86 alin. (8)–(10)); a margin at the reduced rate (D300 places special regimes on rd. 9 only); credit notes; purchases from abroad; the year-end inventory of the items held and the D406 stock file (G1).

**The agricultural flat rate (art. 315^1).** A farmer (an individual, PFA, II or IF with agricultural activities only, or others under the art. 310 threshold) in ANAF's register collects no VAT and deducts none; it invoices its products and agricultural services to other taxable persons with a compensation of 8% of the price without VAT from 2019 (alin. (2)–(3), (8); `ro.vat.flat_rate.agriculture`). The agricultural services are the 34 of the annex of Ordinul nr. 1.155/868/2016 (norme pct. 87^1 alin. (3)), which `FLAT_RATE_PRODUCTS` cites. An individual farmer under the regime does not have to use RO e-Factura, nor do its suppliers for invoices to it (OUG nr. 120/2021 art. 10^7): its invoice may come on paper.
- **`invoices.flatRate`**, a purchase (`parts`: the products' accounts and amounts, `deduction` `full` or `none`): the invoice shows no VAT; the compensation is its total less the products and must be the percent of their price within a ban per line (`FLAT_RATE_AMOUNT`, `FLAT_RATE_MISSING`). The buyer registered for VAT deducts it like VAT (alin. (17)) only when the farmer is in the register on the supply's day (norme pct. 87^1 alin. (15)): the person's answer to `farmer.register` (subject the party, with the register's extract) is cited in the basis (`FLAT_RATE_REGISTER` asks it, and refuses an inscription after the supply). Dr 6xx (or 2xx) the products / Dr 4426 the compensation / Cr 401 (404) the total, or the compensation in the expense when not deducted (a non-payer: `deduction: none`); tax entry `FR-315` with the products as base and the compensation as tax: D300 rd. 29 (the products, "supuse unui regim special"), rd. 27 (the compensation, VAT column only) and rd. 31 (deducted); D394 N (a person not registered for VAT, by invoice); SAF-T 308301 deducted, 358301 not ("Achiziții de produse și servicii agricole de la furnizori care aplică regimul special pentru agricultori"). The reviewer confirms the products and the invoice's mention (`FLAT_RATE_PRODUCTS`) and, unless the partner is recorded as an individual or an individual enterprise, its legal form (`FLAT_RATE_SUPPLIER_FORM`). Refused: a supplier registered for VAT (its code, or ANAF's snapshot), stock accounts (G1), a company with a pro-rata (`FLAT_RATE_PRORATA`).
- **The farmer's own sale** (`invoices.flatRate` on an issued invoice, `compensationAccount`): a PFA, II, IF or individual outside VAT with `vat.agriculture` `special` (its inscription in the register, a `profile.fact`), to a taxable person: Dr 4111 / Cr 70x the products / Cr the compensation's account (the accountant's choice: no text names one), no VAT entry. The income tax of agricultural activities (art. 103–107) and the art. 310 turnover without the agricultural supplies (alin. (4) lit. c)) are not built: the closure reports the farmer UNSUPPORTED (`AGRICULTURE_FARMER`).
- **Not covered:** intra-community operations of the farmer (alin. (8) lit. c)–e), (9)–(12)), the restitution to buyers not established (alin. (18)), credit notes, foreign currency.

**Issuing:** `sales.issue` takes `margin: { scheme: 'travel' }` or `margin: { scheme: 'goods', items }` for lines in category E without VAT whose exemption reason (BT-120) states the scheme's mention (`MARGIN_LINES`, `MARGIN_MENTION`, `MARGIN_ITEMS`, `MARGIN_CREDIT`); the recognition is proposed as `margin.sale`. The worklist proposes `margin.sale` or `margin.purchase` for an unbooked invoice whose reason states «regimul marjei» (the scheme from the mention); a farmer's mention is in a note (BT-22) the parser does not read, so its invoice is the agent's to recognize with `invoices.flatRate`.

**The closure** (`scope.schemes@<year>`): the months with these entries are covered for D300 and D394; the SAF-T has no code for the margin schemes, so a company's D406 of those months is UNSUPPORTED (`MARGIN_SCHEME_D406`; D406 itself refuses the codes, never guesses one); entries on a day outside VAT are UNSUPPORTED (`SPECIAL_SCHEME_NON_VAT`). A dealer's tagged items on 371 are not the stock (`GOODS_STOCK`) the closure reports.

- **Parameters** (all `extracted`, from 2026, reviewed through 2026-12-31, each with a review packet, `src/ro/reviews/specs/schemes.ts`): `ro.vat.margin.travel` (per period, standard rate, inclusive, negative unsupported, rd. 9), `ro.vat.margin.goods` (per supply, negative zero, rd. 9), `ro.vat.flat_rate.agriculture` (8%, rd. 27, 4426).
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/schemes/agent.ts`): the documents (the traveller's invoice, the travel costs, the period's computation, the self-invoice to an individual, a dealer's invoice, the sale of an item, the farmer's invoice, the register's extract), the questions (`farmer.register`; `profile.fact` for `vat.status`, `activity.goods` and `vat.agriculture`), the detectors (`margin.travel`, `document.unproposed`, `question.needed`, `obligation.due`), no template fields.

### Sales to consumers in other Member States and the One Stop Shop (V4): `invoices.euConsumer`, D398
