Distance sales of goods and telecommunications, broadcasting and electronic (TBE) services to consumers in other Member States, their €10,000 threshold, and the Union scheme's special VAT return. The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/oss` (`parameters.ts`, `rates.ts` with the TEDB data in `rates-data.ts`, `treatment.ts`, `determine.ts`, `closure.ts`, `agent.ts`) and `src/ro/declarations/d398.ts`; the engine module is `modules/d398.ts`. Sources (`src/ro/sources/oss.ts`, read on 5 November 2026): ANAF's consolidated Fiscal Code (art. 266 alin. (1) pct. 35, 275 alin. (2), 278 alin. (5) lit. h) and norme pct. 21, 278^1, 314, 315, 315^2, 319 alin. (5) lit. d), (6) lit. b)), OPANAF nr. 174/2026 (D300 rd. 3, 17, 18 and the information section), OPANAF nr. 1.699/2021 (it repeals form 085: the option is declared on the registration forms), ANAF's form 398 (page, structure A1.0.10, schema d398_20211101.xsd, validator J1.0.11), ANAF's SAF-T sheet Livrari (310340, 310346…), the Commission's TEDB (the `retrieveVatRates` web service, kept in `.sources/EC_TEDB`), the ECB's reference rates, OUG nr. 120/2021 in its consolidated form of 01.06.2026 (art. 2, 10–10^10: who reports to RO e-Factura and by when) and Regulation (EU) No 282/2011 in EUR-Lex's consolidation of 14.04.2025 (art. 24a–24f, 57d, 61), both downloaded by the owner.

- **The place of supply** (`vat.distance`, a fact: `origin`, `destination`, `option`). Art. 278^1: a supplier established only in Romania keeps the place of these sales in Romania while their total without VAT stays within `ro.vat.distance.threshold` (10.000 €, which alin. (5) fixes at **46.337 lei**) in the current year and did not exceed it in the previous one; from the sale that exceeds it the place is the customer's state (art. 275 alin. (2), art. 278 alin. (5) lit. h); alin. (2)). Below it, the company may opt for the destination for at least two calendar years (alin. (3), `ro.vat.distance.option_years`; the option is the `option` value with the registration form 010/700 as evidence). The total is cumulated in lei over every sale the treatment books (`distance` on the tax entry), whatever its regime.
- **The determination `vat.distance`** computes it each year: not applicable without such sales or a fact; the previous year's total from the books, or from `year.figures` (`distanceTurnover`, from the year's last D300) when the year is not kept, never assumed zero; a company set up during the year starts from zero; an option within its two years gives `option`; the crossing gives a change to `destination` from the sale that exceeds the threshold. `threshold.approaching` warns at 80% (`ro.vat.distance.threshold/<year>`) and blocks over it while the place is recorded in Romania.
- **The Union scheme** (`vat.oss`: `union`, `none`, `non_union`). The determination `vat.oss` is needed once the place is the destination: unknown, it asks (`profile.fact` from the first day at the destination); `none`, or a registration after that day, is UNSUPPORTED (`EU_DESTINATION_VAT_UNREGISTERED`: the VAT is owed in each Member State, through a registration there the engine does not keep); `non_union` is UNSUPPORTED with its reason (`OSS_NON_UNION`: art. 314 is only for a person without a seat or a fixed establishment in the Union); `union` from a date is the scheme, and its D398 is owed every quarter, with or without sales (art. 315 alin. (6)). That date is the first day of the quarter after ANAF was informed, or the first sale under the scheme when ANAF was informed by the 10th of the next month (Regulation (EU) No 282/2011 art. 57d), not the decision's date: the question and the `EU_DESTINATION_VAT_UNREGISTERED` message say so.
- **The import scheme** (`vat.ioss`: `none`, `registered`, `intermediary`; art. 315^2, consignments up to `ro.vat.ioss` 150 €, a monthly return): UNSUPPORTED (`IOSS_SCHEME`): it is for goods imported from third countries, and imports, customs and stock are not built; a small company without stock does not use it. Asked only of a company that declares goods (`activity.goods`); its monthly returns are listed UNSUPPORTED (`D398.import`).
- **`invoices.euConsumer {documentId, account, supply, location?, dispatchEvidenceId?, categories?, reducedRateBasis?, originalDocumentId?, authorityId?}`** books an issued invoice (or its credit note) to a customer of another Member State without a VAT code of that state (one with a code is a business: `CUSTOMER_TAXABLE`, the B2B rules). `supply` is `electronic`, `telecom`, `broadcasting` or `distance_goods`; other services to consumers abroad (immovable property, events, transport) are not covered. A service needs two items of the customer's location that agree (`location`: billing address, IP, bank, SIM, fixed line, other; norme pct. 21 alin. (4) lit. d), (6); Regulation (EU) No 282/2011 art. 24b lit. (d), 24f; `CUSTOMER_LOCATION`), or one: the customer's fixed line or SIM the service is supplied through (the presumptions of art. 24b lit. (a)–(b), acknowledged `LOCATION_PRESUMED`), or an item other than "other" a third party involved in the supply gave (`thirdParty`: the bank, a payment or network provider), acknowledged `LOCATION_SINGLE_ITEM` that the company's such services did not exceed €100,000 in the current year so far and the previous one (art. 24b second and third paragraphs; the lei equivalent at the ECB's rate of 5 December 2017 is not in the register, so the approver confirms it); goods acknowledge their dispatch from Romania (`DISPATCH_PROOF`) and are booked without their cost (`GOODS_WITHOUT_STOCK`, stock is not built). The company must be registered for VAT (a non-payer's sales are `EU_CONSUMER_NON_PAYER`, not covered); the place is read from `vat.distance` on the supply's date (a credit note: its sale's), and asked when missing.
  - **Below the threshold**: Romanian VAT at the Romanian rate of the tax point (the reduced one with `reducedRateBasis` and `REDUCED_RATE`; another rate is `ORIGIN_RATE`): Dr 4111 / Cr 70x / Cr 4427, tax entry `S-<rate>` with `distance: origin` and the customer's state. The sale that would carry the year past the threshold is not booked so: `DISTANCE_THRESHOLD_CROSSED` asks for `vat.distance` `destination` from its date. Info: the invoice goes to RO e-Factura as B2C within 5 working days of its issue (OUG nr. 120/2021 art. 10^1 alin. (2)–(2^1): supplies with the place in Romania; a customer without a code is written with 13 zeros, alin. (3)) (`EFACTURA_B2C`).
  - **At the destination, in the Union scheme**: each of the invoice's rates must be the consumer state's on the tax point (`ro.vat.oss.rates`, below; `OSS_RATE` otherwise, a state or date the register lacks is `RULE_GAP`), a reduced one naming its TEDB category (`categories`, acknowledged `OSS_REDUCED_RATE`), the VAT within a cent of base × rate (`OSS_VAT_AMOUNT`). Dr 4111 (the gross at the accounting rate, in the invoice's currency) / Cr 70x (the rest) / Cr `ro.vat.oss.payable_account` (4423) the VAT, an open item of the authority (`authorityId`) in the invoice's currency, due on the quarter's D398 deadline: the VAT is paid in euro to a special account (art. 315 alin. (13)). The tax entry `EXP-OSS` (abroad with the right of deduction, so every `EXP-…` rule applies: the VAT period's and the pro-rata's turnover, outside art. 310's) has no VAT line (`ledgerBani` 0: `vat.settle` never moves it) and records the state, rate, rate type, category, supply type, quarter and the amounts in the invoice's currency. Info: not transmitted to RO e-Factura (the place is abroad); invoicing follows Romania's rules as the state of registration (art. 319 alin. (5) lit. d)) (`EFACTURA_NOT_REQUIRED`).
  - **A credit note** (`originalDocumentId`) mirrors its sale by red storno at its own rates: the original's receivable reduced by what it still holds, at its carrying amount (the rate it is carried at, its invoice's or the last month-end revaluation's, as `bank.settle` releases it), the difference from the note's lei a realized exchange difference (7651, 6651); beyond it (the sale collected already), a refund owed to the consumer on 462 in the invoice's currency (`CREDIT_REFUND`, as `invoices.credit`); at the destination, a new item of the authority that may cross zero (what is deducted from the next payment), its entry carrying the quarter it corrects (`corrects`). At the place of origin, the Romanian VAT base and VAT the note reverses keep its sale's rate, as `invoices.credit`'s (art. 282 alin. (9), norme pct. 25 alin. (2); `ro.fx.adjustment.vat`; from 5 November 2026, before at the note's own rate), its 4427 line the same amount, its revenue what is left of the receivable; D406 gives that base as the TaxBase of its revenue line and of its invoice's lines (not their lei at the receivable's rate, which after a revaluation is no longer the sale's), so the rate applied to it gives its VAT. In the Union scheme the VAT is the consumer state's, declared in euro (art. 315 alin. (11)–(12)), so its entry's lei are the books' alone, at the note's own rate as its 4423 line: its base is its revenue line and its VAT the state's rate of it, as D406 states them (310340).
- **The rates** (`ro.vat.oss.rates`, scoped by state, from 1 January 2026): the 26 Member States' standard rate and their reduced, super-reduced and parking rates with TEDB's categories, regional rates (Canary Islands, Madeira, Azores) left out. TEDB was asked on 1 January and 5 November 2026 and on the day before and after each change; versions change on 1 March 2026 (Belgium's accommodation to 12%, France), 1 April (Sweden's food to 6%) and 1 July (Austria's 4,9%, Ireland's 9%). The standard rates do not change in 2026.
- **D300** (`ro.vat.oss.declarations`): the sales with the place in Romania go on rd. 17 (`R64_1`/`R64_2`, credit notes included as negatives), never rd. 9; rd. 18 (later regularizations) is not produced. The information section (`total_precedent`, `total_curent`) holds the bases of rd. 17–18 of the previous year and of the current one to the period's end, the previous year from the books or from `year.figures` (`D300_DISTANCE_PREVIOUS` until answered). The Union scheme's sales are on no row (`d300: none`): ANAF's SAF-T sheet maps their code 310340 to D398 alone; rd. 3's general wording is the alternative in the expert's packet. **D394** lists the sales with the place in Romania like any sale to a person not registered (by the address), and none of the Union scheme's (`EXP-…` stays out: D394 covers the national territory). **D390** is not concerned (B2C). **D406**: the sales with the place in Romania take 310346 (21%), 310356 (11%), 310320, 310343, 310328 by rate (`distance`). The Union scheme's (`EXP-OSS`) take what `ro.d406.oss` reads in ANAF's SAF-T texts: 310340, "Livrări de bunuri expediate/ transportate din RO sau servicii prestate din RO" (sheet Livrari, D398 column 1, no D300 row), at the consumer state's rate ("În funcție de Cota TVA din statul membru în care sa fac livrările") as the line's TaxPercentage, with the VAT owed there; the TaxTable lists 310340 once per state and rate, the state (ISO 3166-1: GR for the prefix EL) as its Country ([D406](#declarations)). A credit note and a storno are the same code at their own rate, their amounts negative; an invoice at two of its state's rates (19% and 7%) puts both on its one revenue line in the ledger, and each invoice line at its rate. Every amount is lei as the books hold it, at the BNR's rates (the tax entry's base and VAT at its tax point's, art. 290; the revenue at the document's, which its 4423 line carries, a credit note's receivable at its original's carrying rate); only D398 converts, at the ECB's rate. The consumer is a customer of its own in the file, 04 with a code the company assigns it (MF.C.3; the 05.06.2025 country codes EU and IM are for partners enrolled in OSS and IOSS, not consumers).
- **D398** (`declarations.prepare` kind `D398`, period `YYYY-Qn`; `ro.vat.oss.return`): the Union scheme's return, `moes_voes_imp` 1, `e_int` 0, `vat_id_no` the CUI with RO (ANAF's R10), `trade_type` 1 without `vat_id_no_msest` (R24.4). Per state of consumption, supply type, rate type and rate, the quarter's sales in euro: as invoiced in euro, or another currency at the ECB's rate of the quarter's last day or the next publication (art. 315 alin. (12); option `ecbEvidenceIds`, the eurofxref XML recorded as evidence; `ECB_RATE` without it). A credit note counts in its sale's quarter: for an earlier quarter, the return carries a CORRECTION of that quarter per state (art. 315 alin. (11)): what the books now hold for it against what its authorized return and the later ones declared (each return's summary keeps its VAT per state and its corrections), within three years (`D398_CORRECTION_LATE`); an earlier quarter without its return in the engine is `D398_PREVIOUS`. Negative operations in the quarter are `D398_SUPPLY_NEGATIVE` (ANAF's structure takes only positive amounts). `checkD398` repeats the structure's rules, R29.1 as validator J1.0.11 applies it (the VAT between the whole euros of base × rate and one euro above). A nil return is written for a registered quarter without sales. No rectifying return (`d_rec` is only 0); authorizing locks no tax ledger, so a sale booked late is a correction in the next return. Validated by ANAF's D398 validator in the SAF-T kit (`saftKinds`: it needs the kit's newer `dec` library). Its summary's `due` is the filing deadline (`ro.deadline.D398`), not the quarter's last day. Its validator lists Estonia at 20%, not TEDB's 24%: a warning only.
- **The closure and the calendar**: the D406 of a period with sales in the Union scheme is covered like any other; `D398` for each quarter registered on some day (`ro.deadline.D398`: the last calendar day of the next month, `fixed`, not moved off a weekend, art. 315 alin. (6^1)), needs-evidence while `vat.oss` or `vat.distance` is unknown; the determinations' UNSUPPORTED triggers and conflicts as above.
- **Parameters** (all `extracted`, from 2026, reviewed through 2026-12-31): `ro.vat.distance.threshold`, `ro.vat.distance.option_years`, `ro.vat.oss.rates`, `ro.vat.oss.return`, `ro.vat.oss.payable_account`, `ro.vat.oss.declarations`, `ro.vat.ioss`, `ro.deadline.D398`; and `ro.d406.oss` (from 2025-07, with D406's parameters). Review packets (`reviews/specs/oss.ts`): the D300 row (none or rd. 3), the account (4423 or 4427 closed into 4423), the sale that crosses the threshold (at the destination or not yet), a refund of an earlier quarter (a correction of that quarter or a reduction of the current one), the D406 code (310340 at the consumer state's rate, or 310325 at 0% without the VAT, which ANAF's answers v1.2, Răspuns 6, give for distance sales declared in Bulgaria and Hungary; and RO as the TaxTable's Country, MF.TT.12's default, which the parameter admits).
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/oss/agent.ts`): the documents (the consumer invoice, the customer's location, the transport document, ANAF's OSS registration, the option's form, the ECB rates, the D398 receipt), the questions (`profile.fact` for `vat.distance`, `vat.oss`, `vat.ioss`; `determination.confirm`; `year.figures` for `distanceTurnover`), the detectors (`threshold.approaching`, `determination.missing`, `obligation.due` for D398, `document.unproposed`, which proposes `invoices.euConsumer` for an issued invoice to a customer of another Member State without its VAT code), no template fields.
- **Not covered:** a non-payer's sales to consumers abroad; other services with the place in another Member State; goods dispatched from another Member State or services from a fixed establishment abroad (trade type 2); electronic interfaces (art. 270 alin. (16), `e_int` 1); the non-Union and import schemes; registrations in other Member States; paying a lei OSS item from a euro account; the presumption of the place where the customer must be present (a hotspot, a hotel, on board: Regulation (EU) No 282/2011 art. 24a), rebutting a presumption with three items (art. 24d), and whether the €100,000 of art. 24b counts the company's domestic electronic services too (the approver's confirmation decides it).

### Goods across the border (G3): intra-community supplies and acquisitions, imports, exports

The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/trade` (`codes.ts`, `documents.ts`, `proof.ts`, `supply.ts`, `acquisition.ts`, `import.ts`, `export.ts`, `goods.ts`, `intrastat.ts`, `etransport.ts`, `questions.ts`, `closure.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `agent.ts`); the engine module is `modules/trade.ts` with the VIES adapter `adapters/vies.ts`. Sources (`src/ro/sources/trade.ts`, read on 5 November 2026): the Fiscal Code in ANAF's consolidated text (art. 268 alin. (3)–(4), 270^1, 273, 276, 283–285, 289, 290 alin. (1), 294 alin. (2^1), 299 alin. (1) lit. c)–e), 307 alin. (4), 326 alin. (2)–(5)), OMFP nr. 103/2016 (art. 1 alin. (4)–(7), art. 2 alin. (2), art. 10, as ANAF publishes its text of MO 106/2016, not consolidated), Regulation (EU) No 282/2011 art. 45a (EUR-Lex's consolidation of 14.04.2025), OPANAF nr. 174/2026 (rd. 5–7, 20–22, 24–25), OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 (the functions of 446 and 442), ANAF's SAF-T sheets (3002xx, 3006xx, 3012xx and their 35xxxx), INS's Intrastat orders for 2025 and 2026, its norms (OPINS nr. 1.623/2022), its deadline page and XML guide, OUG nr. 41/2022 in ANAF's 2022 text, and the Commission's VIES REST contract.

**Tax codes** (`tradeCodes`): `AIC-<rate>` an intra-community acquisition (output and input), `IMP-<rate>` an import's VAT paid at customs (input), `IMP-DEF-<rate>` an import's VAT not paid at customs (output and input). Supplies keep V2's `EX-IC` and `EX-EXPORT`.

- **`goods.euSupply {documentId, account | allocations, arrangement, supplyDate?, viesDocumentId, transport?, stock?}`**: an issued invoice in category K to a business with a VAT code of another Member State (art. 294 alin. (2) lit. a)), booked by `invoices.recognize` with the exemption `art.294(2)(a)` (revenue, the goods out of the stock register at the policy's value, `EX-IC`), and what the exemption rests on:
  - **the tax point of art. 283 alin. (1)**: the invoice, or the 15th of the month after the supply when the invoice came later (`INVOICE_LATE`, ack); the supply's date from BT-72 or `supplyDate` (the transport document), asked otherwise (`SUPPLY_DATE`). The entry's tax point, exigibility and VAT period are that date, so D300 rd. 1 and D390 L follow it;
  - **VIES** (`ro.vat.intra_eu.vies`, `checkBy: exigibility`): a `vies.check` document about exactly the invoice's code (`VIES_CODE`), valid (`VIES_INVALID`), dated by the tax point (`VIES_LATE`); dated after the supply, the approver confirms (`VIES_AFTER_SUPPLY`); more than a month before it, too (`VIES_STALE`, a product choice that decides nothing). `parties.checkVies {partyId}` asks VIES for today with the company's own code (for the consultation number), keeps the response as evidence and records it as the document; a VIES page saved by a person is recorded with `documents.record` (kind `vies.check`). VIES answers only for the day asked;
  - **the transport** (`ro.vat.intra_eu.transport`): `transport.documents` (each a CMR, bill of lading, air waybill, carrier's invoice, insurance, bank payment, public authority's or warehouse keeper's document, with its issuer, independence, destination and date) and `buyerStatement`. Art. 45a's presumption holds with two non-contradictory items from two issuers independent of the seller, of the buyer and of each other, one of them the transport's own (lit. (a)), and, for a transport by the buyer, its written statement by the 10th of the next month (`TRANSPORT_PRESUMED`, info). Without it the exemption rests on OMFP nr. 103/2016 art. 10's documents, which the approver confirms (`TRANSPORT_PROOF`, ack; `presumption: required` would block). No documents yet: `TRANSPORT_PENDING` (ack), the entry's `proof: pending` with `proofDue` 90 days after the supply (art. 1 alin. (4)), and the worklist's `goods.proof` until a person answers `goods.proof` with them;
  - `D390_REQUIRED` (info): art. 294 alin. (2^1) takes the exemption away from a supplier whose D390 is missing or wrong.
- **`goods.euAcquisition {documentId, account | allocations, arrangement, supplyDate?, rate, reducedRateBasis?, deduction, stock?}`**: a received invoice without VAT from a supplier with a VAT code of another Member State (`SUPPLIER_VAT_ID`, `VAT_CHARGED`, `NOT_EU_SUPPLIER`), a VAT payer's (`EU_GOODS_NON_PAYER` otherwise: a non-payer's acquisitions over art. 268 alin. (4)'s 10.000 euro, with D301, are not covered). The tax point is the invoice, or the 15th of the month after the supply (art. 284 alin. (2), `ro.vat.intra_eu.acquisition`, `INVOICE_LATE`); the rate the one at the supply (standard, or reduced with `REDUCED_RATE`); the goods at the issue date's accounting rate, the VAT base at the BNR rate of the tax point (art. 290 alin. (2)). Books Dr the goods (a stock account with `stock`: the NIR, each invoice line an item, the transport invoiced with the goods spread over them, `STOCK_CHARGES`) / Cr 401 in the currency; Cr 4427 / Dr 4426 the VAT (`deduction: none` puts it in the cost). Tax entries `AIC-<rate>`: D300 rd. 5 and 5.1, repeated on rd. 20 and 20.1 by the return, rd. 31 the deducted; D390 type A.
- **`goods.import {documentId, declarationId, account | allocations, deduction, authorityId, certificateEvidenceId?, stock?}`**: the supplier's invoice from outside the Union and its customs declaration of import (a `customs.declaration` document, procedure import, recorded with `documents.record` and its citations: MRN, acceptance date, office, the importer (the company), the invoices it covers, the customs rate, per line the commodity code, customs value, duties, other taxes, ancillary costs, VAT base, rate and VAT, and `vatPayment`; its arithmetic is checked, the VAT base being the customs value with the duties and costs, art. 289). One declaration for exactly this invoice (`DECLARATION_INVOICE`), one rate in force (`IMPORT_RATE`). On the declaration's acceptance (art. 285, the tax point): Dr the goods at the invoice's price (the issue date's BNR rate, `ro.vat.import.invoiceRate`) plus the duties and other taxes (capitalized, `IMPORT_DUTY_COST`, OMFP nr. 1.802/2014, the function of 446) / Cr 401 in the currency / Cr 446 the customs debt (an item of the customs authority, due on the acceptance) holding the duties and the VAT paid at customs, Dr 4426 that VAT (`IMP-<rate>`, D300 rd. 24 or 25). Not paid at customs under the deferral certificate (art. 326 alin. (4) lit. a), `certificateEvidenceId` required, `DEFERRAL_CERTIFICATE`): Cr 4427 / Dr 4426 (`IMP-DEF-<rate>`, rd. 7 and 22). The goods enter the stock register as for a purchase.
- **`goods.export {documentId, account | allocations, declarationId?, stock?}`**: an issued invoice in G (or E with VATEX-EU-G) to a customer outside the Union (`NOT_EXPORT`), booked by `invoices.recognize` with `art.294(1)(a)-(b)` (`EX-EXPORT`, D300 rd. 14, the goods out of stock), and a customs declaration of export listing the invoice: its exit certified (`exit`, `EXPORT_EXIT_CERTIFIED`, OMFP nr. 103/2016 art. 2 alin. (2)); not yet, `EXPORT_EXIT_PENDING` with 90 days (`ro.vat.export.proof`) and the worklist's `goods.proof`.
- **Triangulation and call-off stock** (`arrangement`): only `standard` is covered. A triangular operation (art. 276 alin. (5), art. 307 alin. (4): D390 T, the intermediary's acquisition not taxable, the final customer liable) is `TRIANGULATION`, the call-off stock of art. 270^1 (its transfer, register and D390 reporting) `CALL_OFF_STOCK`, both blocked as not covered.
- **Intrastat** (INS, filed outside the engine): the determinations `intrastat.arrivals` and `intrastat.dispatches` (profile axes, `none` or `provider`) from the books' AIC and EX-IC bases by the month the goods moved, against `ro.intrastat.thresholds` (1.000.000 lei each flow in 2025 and 2026): a provider for the whole year when the previous year exceeded that year's threshold (the order's art. 2), else from the month the year's cumulated flow exceeds it (art. 3); the previous year from the books, or asked (`year.figures` `intrastatArrivals`, `intrastatDispatches`). `threshold.approaching` warns at 80% and blocks over it while no provider is recorded. The closure lists `Intrastat.arrivals` and `Intrastat.dispatches` for each provider month, due on the 15th of the next month, not moved off a non-working day (`ro.deadline.Intrastat`, INS's „întotdeauna data de 15”), marked filed once a person answers `obligation.filed`. `intrastat.data {month, flow}` gives the month's lines from the books (CN8 code from the item, invoiced value in lei, partner and its code) and lists what the books do not hold (net mass, nature of transaction, delivery terms, mode of transport, origin). INS's XML format exists (intrastat4.xsd, its 2026 implementation guide); the engine does not write it.
- **RO e-Transport** (tracked, not integrated): `etransport.list {from, to}` lists each transport of the books (an acquisition, a supply, an import, an export) with who declares it (OUG nr. 41/2022 art. 8 alin. (1)), the window (at most 3 days before the start, the UIT valid 5 days: `ro.etransport`, art. 11) and the UIT code a person recorded (`etransport.uit`, or why none was needed); the worklist's `etransport.uit` lists those without. Whether the goods are of high fiscal risk is the person's answer: ANAF's order listing them and the ordinance's consolidation are not read.
- **D406**: `AIC-<rate>` with its deduction 300204/300205/300201 (none 3502xx), `IMP-DEF-<rate>` 300604/300605/300601 (3506xx), the collected sides reported with them; `IMP-<rate>` 301204/301205/301201 (3512xx). A sale's goods discharged at cost (the stock account against 60x) are no VAT base line, an import's customs debt (446) neither; stock accounts' lines are goods; a partner outside the Union is written 02 with its VAT code in its state when its details record one (`parties.recordDetails` `vatId`), otherwise `D406_PARTY_CODE` as before (06, a code the company assigns, is not built). Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator from the books.
- **Parameters** (all `extracted`, from 2026 unless said, reviewed through 2026-12-31): `ro.vat.intra_eu.acquisition`, `ro.vat.intra_eu.supply`, `ro.vat.intra_eu.vies`, `ro.vat.intra_eu.transport`, `ro.vat.import`, `ro.vat.export.proof`, `ro.intrastat.thresholds` (2025 and 2026), `ro.etransport`, `ro.deadline.Intrastat` (from 2025). Review packets (`reviews/specs/trade.ts`): the VIES check's date (exigibility or supply), the presumption (one way of proof or the only one), the import invoice's rate (the invoice's BNR rate or the customs rate).
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/trade/agent.ts`): the documents (the supply's, acquisition's, import's and export's invoices, the VIES check, the transport documents, the DVI, the deferral certificate, the export declaration, INS's notice, the RO e-Transport confirmation), the questions (`goods.proof`, `etransport.uit`, `determination.confirm`, `year.figures`, `obligation.filed`), the detectors (`document.unproposed` proposing `goods.euSupply`, `goods.export`, `goods.euAcquisition`, `goods.import`; `goods.proof`; `etransport.uit`; `threshold.approaching`; `obligation.due`), no template fields.
- **Not covered:** triangular operations and call-off stock; a non-payer's acquisitions and imports; credit notes and advances of these operations (a supply's or an acquisition's correction is a reversal and a new booking); a declaration for several invoices or an invoice in several declarations; the deferral grounds of art. 326 alin. (4) lit. b)–d); a separate carrier's invoice capitalized into goods already received (its cost is booked by its own treatment); new means of transport and excise goods; the Intrastat XML writer; RO e-Transport's API.

### `vat.settle`: VAT settlement of a period

- **Input:** the VAT period (a month, or a quarter for quarterly payers), the posting date on or after the period's end, the tax authority as a partner (a public institution), the return or worksheet as evidence, and a round number.
- **Effect:** clears what the period's tax entries booked on 4427 (output) and 4426 (deductible input), net of earlier rounds, with settlement lines; the difference is VAT payable (4423) or recoverable (4424), an open item of the authority. VAT payable is due with the period's return (`ro.deadline.D300`, the 25th of the next month moved off a non-working day), the date the item carries; a refund carries none. VAT not yet exigible (4428) stays.
- **Reads** the whole tax period as one read-set entry (its tax entries, settlement lines and last sequence). An entry reaching the period after approval makes the settlement stale, in the application and in the database. A later round settles only what arrived after the first.
- **Carried forward:** with `offsetCarried`, VAT to recover from earlier periods (open 4424 items of the authority, oldest first) is offset against this period's payable, as the return carries it. A refund is received through the bank, settling the 4424 item.
- **Refused:** a period of the wrong kind for the company's `vat.period`, a date before the period ends, a period with nothing to settle.

### `payroll.recognize`: a month's payroll

- **Input:** a prepared payroll run, its month and round, and the tax authority as a partner; for a correction round, the run it corrects (`corrects`) and the posting date (`date`, no earlier than the month's end).
- **Effect:** salaries in money owed to each employee (641 = 421, an open item per contract under the employee's party); CAS, CASS and tax withheld from them (421 = 4315, 4316, 444); contributions the company bears in an employee's name (the part-time minimum) on the account of `policy.payroll.employer_share`; CAM (646 = 436). The contributions and the tax are open items of the authority. Meal vouchers are taxed by the run and booked when handed over.
  - **Benefits in kind** (W1): the taxable value is salary not paid in money, 641 = 421 and 421 = 7588 (the cost is already in the expenses: the car's depreciation, the rent, the premium), an interpretation awaiting review; the tax and contributions on it come out of the money salary.
  - **Garnishments** (W1): what each order takes from the net pay, 421 = 427, an open item of the creditor per order, paid through `bank.settle`.
  - **Indemnities FNUASS bears** (every code): a claim on it (Dr 4382), recovered on the employer's request.
- **A correction** (W1) is a later round of the month (`payroll:<month>:<round>`): its run is the month computed again, and the posting books only the difference from the run it corrects, account by account and employee by employee, red storno where an amount fell; the employee's and the authority's items may turn (an amount overpaid to recover). The tax and contributions keep the month's due date. A correction that changes a garnishment is refused (`CORRECTION_GARNISHMENT`).
- **Reads** the run (and the corrected run) as read-set entries (their result hash and state): a run computed again after approval makes the posting stale, in the application and in the database.
- **Paid** through `bank.settle`: the net salary settles the employee's 421 item, and one transfer to ANAF can settle the 4315, 4316, 444 and 436 items together.
- **Refused:** a run with unresolved problems or superseded, a company not recorded as an employer, union dues (the union is not modelled yet), a run needing the employer-share policy without it, a correction without its date or base, and a correction that changes nothing.

### `tax.micro`: micro-enterprise tax of a quarter

- **Input:** the quarter, a posting date on or after its end, the tax authority as a partner, a round, determinations for accounts whose revenue the law excludes only in some cases, and, once the year has revenue on 7583, the year's transfers of fixed assets (by subgroup of the catalogue, with their revenue) and of land (`assetTransfers`, with evidence).
- **Base** (Codul fiscal art. 53, read in ANAF's consolidated text): the quarter's revenues of any source (class 7, net of corrections and of 709), less those excluded by their nature (711, 712, 721, 722, 725, 741x, 7584 and FX gains 765), less what the determinations exclude, plus commercial discounts received (609) and, in the fourth quarter, the year's net favorable FX difference (765 less 665). Revenues on 781x, 786x, 7581, 7588, 768, 761x and 762 need a determination, with its evidence and the letter of art. 53 alin. (1) that applies, before the tax is computed. Revenue from selling assets (7583) stays in the base: art. 53 doesn't exclude it. The base is taxed in lei and bani at `ro.micro.rate` (1%); only the tax is set in whole lei, half up at 50 bani (`ro.declaration.rounding`, OUG nr. 59/2005 art. 1 alin. (1)). Rounding the base first is the alternative reading in the rate's review packet.
- **Effect:** Dr 698 / Cr 4418, an open item of the authority, due with the quarter's D100 (`ro.deadline.D100.micro`).
- **Refused:** a company not recorded as micro (`income_tax`), a year's turnover (70x) above `ro.micro.revenue_limit_eur` (100,000 euro from 2026) at the BNR rate of the previous year's end, which makes the company owe profit tax from that quarter, a missing rate, a year with revenue on 7583 and no `assetTransfers` (`MICRO_ASSET_TRANSFERS`), and a zero tax. The reviewer acknowledges each quarter that the company had a full-time-equivalent employee (art. 47 alin. (1) lit. g)).
- **Asset sales and the limit** (art. 54 alin. (1)): the turnover checked against the limit includes the year's revenue from transferring fixed assets once more than one asset of the same subgroup was transferred, and from transferring land once more than one plot was. Assets of different subgroups are the reading the text leaves open ("mai mult de un activ din oricare subgrupă"): the engine doesn't add them, and when adding them would exceed the limit the reviewer acknowledges the open question (`MICRO_LIMIT_READING`), which the limit's review packet asks the expert contabil. The asset register doesn't record whether a disposal was a sale, so the transfers are stated.
- **Reads** the quarter's and the year's turnover of classes 6 and 7: an entry posted in either range after approval makes the posting stale. Year-end closing entries, when built, will have to be left out of these reads.

### `tax.profit`: profit tax of a quarter or of the year (P1)

The profit tax of a Romanian legal person (Codul fiscal title II) under the quarterly system (art. 41 alin. (1)). The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/profit` (the bridge, fiscal depreciation, loss vintages, the fiscal register, `agent.ts`) and `src/ro/treatments/profit-tax.ts`.

- **Input:** the period (`2026-Q1`…`2026-Q3`, or the year `2026`: the fourth quarter is the year's), the posting date (from the quarter's end within the year; the year's last day for the year, in its own books, before the result is closed), the tax authority, a round, `determinations` (amounts no account holds apart, each with its evidence and letter of the law), `legalReserve` (the decision by which the company sets aside the legal reserve) and `rectification`.
- **Where it starts:** 1 January, or the first day of the quarter in which the recorded `income_tax` became `profit` after the company was a micro-enterprise (art. 52 alin. (1)–(4), (6), (7)); then the revenues and expenses are those from that quarter on, and the favourable FX difference of the micro months (765 − 665) is an item similar to revenue (art. 53 alin. (2) lit. b)), unless the exit was in the first quarter.
- **The system** (`profit.system`): quarterly, by law after a micro year (art. 41 alin. (6) lit. d)), otherwise the company's fact; the annual system with prepayments (alin. (2)–(3), (8)–(10^1)) is refused (`PROFIT_ANNUAL_SYSTEM`).
- **The bridge**, row by row as D101 declares it, cumulated from the start: every account of classes 6 and 7 by its nature (`profit/accounts.ts`, the longest prefix of the chart deciding; analytics take their synthetic's nature unless classified themselves):
  - by their nature: 691 not deductible (rd. 23, art. 25 alin. (4) lit. a)); book depreciation (6811) and the book value of assets disposed of (6583) added back (rd. 28) and replaced by fiscal depreciation (rd. 11, art. 28 alin. (17)); provisions and impairments (6812–6817, 6863–6864) added back (rd. 29) unless a determination states what art. 26 allows (the receivables' 30% of lit. c) computed from the stated base, guarantees, insolvent debtors, insured assets); their reversals taxed unless stated non-taxable (art. 23 lit. d), rd. 20); 641 the base of the social limit;
  - by a person's classification (`profit.account`, held until it changes) of the accounts whose treatment depends on facts (623, 642, 6456–6458, 654, 6581, 6582, 6587, 6588, 668, 698, 761, or any other account a person classifies): protocol within 2% of the accounting profit with the profit tax and the protocol added (rd. 26, art. 25 alin. (3) lit. a)), social expenses within 5% of the salaries (rd. 33, lit. b)), fines and penalties owed to authorities (rd. 25, alin. (4) lit. b)) or contractual penalties (deductible), sponsorship (rd. 27, and the credit below), a vehicle's costs (50% not deductible, the VAT kept included, when the `vehicle.use` answer limits it, lit. l) and norme pct. 16; never on a depreciation account), non-deductible with the letter of art. 25 alin. (4) (rd. 33), dividends from a Romanian company (rd. 17.1, art. 23 lit. a), which then require the allocation of their expenses as a determination, art. 25 alin. (4) lit. e), rd. 32), other non-taxable revenue with its letter (rd. 20);
  - an account that needs a classification and has none blocks with the question (`PROFIT_CLASSIFICATION`), never guessed;
  - the legal reserve, when the company sets it aside: 5% of the accounting profit with the profit tax, until a fifth of the capital (1012) less the reserve at the year's start (1061), at each quarter (norme pct. 19 alin. (1)) (rd. 13); without it a company with a profit acknowledges `LEGAL_RESERVE` while the reserve has room (below a fifth of the capital at the year's start); once it reached it, nothing is set aside or deducted, and nothing is asked.
- **Rounding:** each row is rounded once from bani to whole lei (`ro.declaration.rounding`); the derived rows are derived from those, so the tax booked is the tax the return declares; limits (70% of the losses, the sponsorship's) are rounded down, never above the law's.
- **Losses** (art. 31): the vintages at the year's start come from the latest earlier annual computation, else from a person's answer for the books' start (`profit.losses`, with the D101s filed; `PROFIT_LOSSES` until then), whose amounts are taken in whole lei rounded half up, as D101's rows are, never cut. Each is usable in the calendar years its version allows after its own (`ro.profit.loss_carryforward`: 5 for losses from 2024, what is left of 7 for those of 2016–2023), recovered in order at each payment within 70% of the profit, cumulated over both kinds (alin. (8)) (rd. 39, 39^1). The year's loss becomes a vintage (rd. 40^1). Whether years spent as a micro-enterprise count is an expert question.
- **Tax and credits:** 16% of the taxable profit (`ro.profit.rate`); the sponsorship credit, the least of the sponsorship, 0.75% of the turnover (70x net of 709, plus 7411) and 20% of the tax (rd. 43, art. 25 alin. (4) lit. i)), at each quarter; nothing is carried forward (rd. 43.2 is not covered). Other credits and exemptions (rd. 42, 44, 45) are not covered.
- **Scope checks:** the minimum turnover tax (art. 18^1) applies only above 50 million euro of the previous year's turnover, until 31.12.2026: with the previous year in the books it is checked (gross 70x credits against the threshold at that year's close rate) and refused above it; without it, a cited answer about that year's figures (`year.figures`, its `turnover`, else its `netTurnover`) is checked the same way, and only without one does the reviewer acknowledge `PROFIT_MINIMUM_TAX`. Exceeding borrowing costs (666 − 766) above 500,000 euro at the period end's rate are refused (`PROFIT_INTEREST_LIMIT`, art. 40^2).
- **Effect:** a quarter books the cumulated tax less what the year's earlier quarters booked (Dr 691 / Cr 4411, an open item of the authority, due with the quarter's D100, `ro.deadline.D100.profit`; the year's and its rectification's with D101); a quarter with nothing due, and a year whose tax equals its quarters', post a memorandum (no lines) carrying the computation; a year that owes less books the difference back (Dr 4411 / Cr 691, an item to recover). A quarter after one never computed acknowledges `PROFIT_QUARTER_MISSING`.
- **The position** each posting records (`explanation.details.position`: the D101 rows, the tax cumulated, declared and due, the loss vintages before, used and after) is what the next computation, the returns and the registers read (`ReadPlan.profitPostings`: the postings that stand of `tax.profit` and `correction.priorYear`).
- **Refused:** a company not on profit tax at the period's end (`NOT_PROFIT`), a profit start that is not a quarter's first day after micro (`PROFIT_START`), a year whose result is closed (121 moved: `YEAR_CLOSED`, the year's tax is booked before the closing), a missing parameter (`RULE_GAP`: every key is read from 2026, the loss years excepted).

### `correction.priorYear`: a material error of an earlier year (FP-03)

The correction on retained earnings (1174) of a material error of an approved earlier year (OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 65–68), never through this year's classes 6 or 7: the lines (an omitted supplier invoice opens its payable), the year corrected and the change of its taxable result (`fiscalChange`, negative for a deductible expense omitted). It acknowledges `PRIOR_YEAR_D101`: the year's D101 is rectified (art. 19 alin. (3) lit. a)). `tax.profit` with `rectification` for that year then starts from the year's last position, adds the corrections posted since as items similar to revenue or expenses (rd. 8, rd. 9), recomputes the losses from the vintages the year started with and the tax, keeps the sponsorship credit within 20% of the new tax, and books the difference on 1174 (Dr 4411 / Cr 1174 for less tax), acknowledging `PROFIT_DOWNSTREAM` (the later years' loss vintages are not re-evaluated automatically). A year the books do not cover refuses (`PROFIT_YEAR_OUTSIDE`): its rectifying D101 is filed outside the engine, which the worklist asks a person to record (`obligation.filed`, D101).

### `dividends.*`: dividends, interim ones included

- **Distribution** (`dividends.distribute`): the shareholders' decision as evidence, its date, and each shareholder's gross dividend. Dr 1171 / Cr 457, an open item owed to each shareholder. The total may not exceed the retained profit on 1171 at the decision date.
- **Interim distribution** (`dividends.interim`, O1): the decision approving the interim financial statements of a quarter (I–III) and distributing dividends during the year (Legea nr. 82/1991 art. 19 alin. (3^1), Legea nr. 31/1990 art. 67 alin. (2) as Legea nr. 163/2018 amended them), with the decision and the statements as evidence. Dr 463 / Cr 456 per shareholder, an open item on each (OMFP nr. 3.067/2018, pct. 423^1). The total may not exceed the year's result to the quarter's end (classes 6 and 7, the profit tax included) plus the retained profit (1171) and the available reserves withdrawn (`fromReserves`, within 1068), less the reserves the law or the articles require (`reserves`, stated) and the year's earlier interim distributions (463's debits). The reviewer confirms the interim statements exist and are filed within 30 days (OMFP nr. 3.067/2018 art. V; `INTERIM_STATEMENTS`): the engine neither draws them up nor lists them in the calendar. Refused: Q4 (the annual statements' quarter), a decision after the financial year, a financial year not starting on 1 January.
- **Withholding** (`dividends.withhold`), when a dividend is paid (or on the year's last day for dividends distributed and still unpaid, art. 43 alin. (3)): the gross paid, the distribution it comes from (annual on 457, or interim on 456), and the tax authority. Dr 457 (or 456) / Cr 446, owed to the authority and due with the D100 of the month it was withheld in (`ro.deadline.D100.dividends`), at `ro.dividend.tax_rate` for the distribution's date (the interim decision's for interim dividends, not recomputed at the regularization: the reading in the rate's review packet): 16% for individuals (art. 97 alin. (7)) and for Romanian companies (art. 43), from 2026. The net is then paid through `bank.settle` against the same item. Its explanation records what D100, D205 and D406 declare (`withholding`: 604 or 150, the WHT code, base and tax).
- **Regularization** (`dividends.regularize`): once the year's statements are approved and the annual dividends distributed in the next year, the interim dividends of the year are regularized against them, per shareholder: Dr 457 / Cr 463 (pct. 423^2). The annual dividend left over is paid from 457 with its tax as usual; the interim dividends still owed stay on 456. Refused (`INTERIM_EXCESS`): interim dividends above a shareholder's annual dividend, whose return within 60 days (art. 19 alin. (3^2)) and the tax withheld on them are not covered.
- **Refused:** paying more than is owed or before the decision. Non-resident shareholders are withheld with `nonresidents.withhold`. For a company shareholder, the reviewer confirms that the exemption of art. 43 alin. (4) does not apply; an exempt dividend is paid gross with nothing withheld (and D406 reports no 0% WHT code for it). The 10% of interim dividends distributed in 2025 (Legea nr. 141/2025 art. VII alin. (2)) is not in the register: their payments are `RULE_GAP`.

### Withholding at source (O1): `payees.*` and `nonresidents.withhold`

The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/treatments/withholding.ts`, the parameters and ANAF's WHT codes in `src/ro/withholding` (with `agent.ts`), the sources in `src/ro/sources/withholding.ts`. Every value is cited and `extracted`, read in ANAF's consolidated Codul fiscal (to Legea nr. 204/2026), from 2026 only.

- **Rent paid to an individual** (Codul fiscal art. 84^1). Since the income of 2024 (OUG nr. 115/2023 art. LIII pct. 64, read in the legislative portal's consolidation notes; D100's nomenclature: „se aplica din 01.2024”), a company or any entity keeping accounts withholds the landlord's tax at each payment: the net income is the gross less 20% (`ro.rent.expense_rate`, alin. (3)), the tax 10% of it (`ro.rent.tax_rate`, alin. (5)), final, due by the 25th of the next month (alin. (6)).
  - `payees.recognize` (kind `rent`) books what the contract makes owed for a month: Dr 612 / Cr 462 (creditori diverși), an open item of the landlord, tagged with what it is for. The contract is the evidence; there is no invoice.
  - `payees.withhold` at the payment: the tax in whole lei, half up (`ro.declaration.rounding`), Dr 462 / Cr 446; the net is paid through `bank.settle` on the same item. 2,500 lei pay 200 lei; 1,234.56 lei have 987.65 net and pay 99 lei.
  - Declared in D100 (628) and D205 (tip 29, the net income and the tax); a VAT payer lists the rent in D394 as an acquisition N from the individual (a contract, services).
  - The landlord's CASS is theirs (the declaration unică): the posting only notes it (`RENT_CASS`).
  - Refused: a landlord that is a company or a PFA (invoiced), a non-resident landlord (art. 223 alin. (2) lit. g), not covered), another expense account than 612. Not covered: rent in a currency (alin. (2): the BNR rate of the day before payment), rent paid only in kind and a guarantee kept for it (alin. (8)–(9), D205's VB and GAR).
- **Intellectual property paid to an individual** (art. 72). The payer sets the net income, the gross less 40% (`ro.ip.expense_rate`, alin. (2)), and withholds 10% of it (`ro.ip.tax_rate`, alin. (4)), final (alin. (5)).
  - `payees.recognize` (kind `royalties`, on the class 6 account given: the chart has no account of its own) and `payees.withhold`, which needs the contract's facts (`royalties`): whether it makes the payer withhold CAS and CASS (art. 151 alin. (6)–(7), art. 174^1 alin. (3)–(4)), whether the author opted for the real system (art. 73), whether the income is the heirs', the resale right's or the private copy's (art. 72 alin. (3)); each of these is refused (`IP_CONTRIBUTIONS`, `IP_REAL_SYSTEM`, the expert's question, `IP_COLLECTIVE`). 10,000 lei pay 600 lei.
  - Not declared in D100 or D205: art. 132 alin. (2) sends its payers to D112, where the engine does not write it yet. The closure lists it as UNSUPPORTED (`D112_INTELLECTUAL_PROPERTY`); D406 reports the line under the generic 000; D394 lists it as an acquisition N, like rent.
- **Non-residents** (`nonresidents.withhold`, art. 223–224, 229–231): when a non-resident (a partner `eu_taxable` or `non_eu`) is paid what is owed on its item (401, 404 or 462 for royalties, commissions and services; 1682, 1685–1687, 4558, 5198 for interest; 457 or 456 for dividends):
  - What the payments are and who receives them is a per-supplier determination a person answers (`nonresident.payee`, with the contract): dividends, interest, royalties (art. 7 pct. 36; a software licence is one only when it grants more than the right to use the program, norme art. 223 pct. 2), commissions, services taxable in Romania (management or consulting from a resident, services performed in Romania, professions there: art. 223 alin. (1) lit. i), k), l)), or not taxable in Romania (refused: nothing to withhold or declare). Without the answer the proposal asks it (`NONRESIDENT_PAYEE`), never guessed.
  - The domestic rate on the gross: dividends 16% (`ro.dividend.tax_rate`, beneficiary `nonresident`, by the distribution's date; art. 224 alin. (4) lit. b)); the other income 16% (lit. d)), or 10% for an individual resident in an EU or a treaty state (lit. c^1)), which the engine grants only with the residence certificate (an expert question). Paid by the 25th of the next month, in lei (alin. (5)).
  - A treaty's rate: from the person's answer (`nonresident.certificate`): the certificate (its state and the year it attests, the day it was presented), the treaty as published and its article and rate. It applies when lower than the domestic rate (art. 230 alin. (1)) and the certificate speaks for the payment: presented by then, valid in the year presented and the first 60 days of the next (`ro.nonresident.certificate_days`, alin. (2)); a certificate of another state than the partner's is refused. Without a valid certificate the domestic rate applies, and the proposal asks for one (`WHT_CERTIFICATE`, acknowledged at approval; the worklist shows it as a question). A treaty's 0% books nothing: a memorandum records the payment for D207 (exempt).
  - Dr the item / Cr 446 in whole lei; declared in D100 (631 dividends, 632 interest, 633 royalties, 634 commissions, 636 services) and D207.
  - The reviewer confirms that an EU company's dividends, interest or royalties are not exempt under the directives (art. 229 alin. (1) lit. c), g); `WHT_DIRECTIVE_*`), which the engine does not apply.
  - Refused: the tax borne by the payer (the gross-up, non-deductible under art. 25 alin. (4) lit. a); `WHT_BORNE_BY_PAYER`), the 50% rate (lit. c); `WHT_50`), an item in a currency (`WHT_CURRENCY`), services under a treaty's rate (D207 has no code for them; `WHT_TREATY_SERVICES`), a resident partner. Not covered: regularizing the tax when a certificate is presented later (alin. (2)), dividends to non-residents left unpaid at year end, the other incomes of art. 223 (sport and entertainment, premiums, liquidation).
- **The tax account:** each tax withheld here is an obligation paid into the single account (`ro.taxaccount.obligations`, [The tax account](#the-tax-account-t2)), its 446 line tagged with its D100 obligation (`wht:628`, `wht:611` for intellectual property, `wht:631`–`wht:636`): rent from 09.05.2024 (OPANAF nr. 874/2024, poz. 33^3), intellectual property and the non-residents' income since OPANAF nr. 551/2021 (poz. 11, 18–21, 24), each due by the 25th of the month after it was withheld (art. 84^1 alin. (6), 72 alin. (5), 224 alin. (5)).
- **Due dates:** the 446 item each withholding opens carries its due date (`taxDue`, `xfactura-core/src/ro/treatments/due.ts`), the one the tax account reads, so the worklist and `taxaccount.position` agree: rent and the non-residents' income by the deadline of the month's D100 (`ro.deadline.D100.withholding`), intellectual property by D112's (`ro.deadline.D112`, which declares it), moved off a non-working day (`ro.deadline.shift`); the tax on interest paid to an individual under a civil contract (`loans.withhold`) on the day the interest is paid (art. 97 alin. (2): „la momentul plății dobânzii”, the catalogue's `withholding_date`). A deadline the register does not cover leaves the item without one, as the tax account leaves it.

### `assets.depreciate`: a month's depreciation

- **Register:** `assets.register` records a fixed asset's card: inventory number, the catalogue code (HG 2139/2004, a text still to obtain), its account (20x, 21x), depreciation (28x) and expense (68x) accounts, entry value, accounting and fiscal lives in months, commissioning date with its record, and the purchase invoice. `assets.list` shows each with the depreciation booked and the value remaining.
- **Effect:** for the month, straight-line depreciation of every asset commissioned in an earlier month (OMFP 1802/2014 pct. 238): Dr 68x / Cr 28x, each asset's line carrying it as its item, so the ledger holds its accumulated depreciation. The monthly parts are rounded so they add up, and the last month reaches the full value.
- **Refused:** a month in which an asset's booked depreciation is not that of the months before (a month missed or booked twice), and a month with nothing to depreciate.
- **The locked year:** a card commissioned on or before the end of a locked financial year, and a disposal dated by then, are refused (`YEAR_CLOSED`; the database too, `inv:asset_locked_year`): that year's register and the snapshot its close recorded hold the asset as it was, so the year is reopened first (`years.reopen.request`); an asset found at a later inventory is registered as a surplus, at the inventory's date.
- **Disposal:** `assets.dispose` records that an asset left use, with its record; `assets.derecognize` then takes it off the books (Dr 28x depreciation booked, Dr 6583 the value not recovered / Cr its account). Whether the disposal month is depreciated is the company's policy `policy.assets.disposal_month` (included or excluded); without it, depreciation and derecognition of that asset stop. A sale is invoiced separately.
- **Fiscal depreciation (art. 28)**, kept apart from the book depreciation: `assets.recordFiscal` records an asset's fiscal basis, versioned in the append-only `acc_asset_fiscal` (guard `asset_fiscal_version`): the method (linear, degressive with the 1.5/2.0/2.5 coefficients and the switch to linear of norme pct. 25, accelerated at most 50% in the first year, superaccelerated at most 65% for new assets of subgroups 2.1 and 2.4 put into use in 2026, deducted in full when put into use for work safety, medical offices or below the 5,000 lei value, or none), the fiscal value, the normal life (the card's fiscal life unless given), the first year's share and whether it is an M1 car capped at 1,500 lei a month (alin. (14); at disposal the value left up to 1,500 lei times the months left, norme pct. 27). Fiscal depreciation runs from the month after commissioning to the month before disposal, when the fiscal value left is deducted. `assets.list` shows each asset's basis; the profit tax blocks on an asset in use without one (`ASSET_FISCAL`), and the worklist lists it (`asset.fiscalMissing`). A basis recorded after a year's close changes none of that year's computations, which are posted, and the next year's opening does not compare it.
- **Impairment** (`impairment.assets`, Y1): on an evidenced test at the year's end ([Year-end in full](#year-end-in-full-y1)). Revaluation is not built yet.

### RO e-TVA

`vat.etva.check` compares the period's latest VAT return with the pre-filled return RO e-TVA (P300), row by row: a row differs significantly at both 20% and 1,000 lei (`ro.etva.significance`, OUG 70/2024 art. 5, still in force after OUG 89/2025), on its VAT, or its value where it has none, leaving out regularization rows. Which amount the 20% is taken of the ordinance does not say; the pre-filled one is this engine's reading. The pre-filled amounts are given by hand from the P300 until a P300 file can be read (its format is not published). It also lists the e-Factura invoices of the period that are not in its VAT ledger, and the invoices in the ledger that did not come through e-Factura.

### Year-end: `year.closeResult`, `year.legalReserve`, `year.transferResult`

- **Closing:** on the financial year's last day, every expense (6xx) and revenue (7xx) account is brought to zero against 121 in the closing journal (INCHIDERE), so the next year opens without them; the ledger is continuous, and every other balance carries over. A year's result is closed once; modified financial years are not supported yet.
- **Legal reserve:** on the same day, Dr 129 / Cr 1061 for the amount the company sets aside. Legea 31/1990 art. 183 asks for at least 5% of the profit each year until the reserve reaches a fifth of the share capital (1012), without saying whether before or after income tax: an amount below 5% of the year's result, while under the cap, asks the reviewer to confirm. The result is the 121 line the year's closing booked (tagged `year-result:<yearEnd>`, a reversal of the closing netting it out), not 121's turnover over the year or on its last day, which holds the previous year's result moved out once its statements were approved, on 31 December too.
- **Transfer:** once the shareholders approve the financial statements, the allocations already made (129) are cleared against 121 and the rest of the result goes to retained earnings (1171), from which `dividends.distribute` pays; a loss is carried there too.
- **Registers** (OMFP 2634/2015 pct. 44–47): `ledger.journal` (Registrul-jurnal) gives every entry in recording order with its justifying document's type, number and date; `ledger.generalLedger` (Cartea mare) gives, for a month, each synthetic account's opening balance, debit and credit turnover by corresponding account (`%` lists several) and closing balance; `ledger.inventoryRegister` (Registrul-inventar) gives, at a date, every asset, liability and equity account with a balance and what makes it up (open items by partner, fixed assets by inventory number), at the books' value until inventory differences are recorded.
- **Annual statements (microentities, S1005 for 2025):** the core writer produces the balance sheet (F10), the profit and loss account (F20), the informative data (F30) and the fixed-asset statement (F40), and checks the form's controls, ANAF's validator's codes included. The chart maps to F10, F20 and F30 chapter XIX by the formulas of ANAF's form and OMF 2036/2025 anexa 4. Where the form, ANAF's structure and the order differ, the choice is recorded with the formula. Only 2025 is supported; other years are a gap until ANAF publishes their package.
  - **From the books** (declaration `S1005`, period `2025`):
    - F10 is read from the closing balances, kept per partner on the accounts held on open items, so the forms' debit and credit balances are the analytic ones.
    - F20 and F30 chapter XIX are read from the year's turnover of classes 6 and 7, without the closing journal. Classes 6 and 7 still open, an unmapped balance (VAT accounts left unsettled, for example) or a 121 that differs from the result are problems.
    - The previous year's column comes from the engine's previous year when it keeps one. Otherwise its balance sheet comes from the opening entries, and the preparer gives its F20 and F30 chapter XIX rows as filed.
    - From the preparer: the identification the books don't hold, and the row of each account the form spreads over several rows (debts due within a year or later). A balance belonging to several rows is kept on analytic accounts.
    - F40 (fixed assets) is read from the same balances as F10, so it ties to F10 to the leu, plus the year's movements: increases are the debits of the fixed-asset accounts, reductions their credits, and the same for depreciation and impairment, as OMF 2036/2025 defines the columns.
      - Each row takes the accounts the order's detailed balance sheet gives the same category (for example 213 + 223, and 214 + 224).
      - The part of a reduction that was scrapping is given by the preparer.
      - 267 and the accounts the microentities' balance sheet doesn't list are problems.
    - F30's other chapters come from the registers (below); still given rather than read: F20 rd. 01a and chapter XIX's rows without accounts.
- **Annual statements (small entities, S1003 for 2025):** OMF nr. 2.036/2025 anexa nr. 1 pct. 2.2: the abridged balance sheet (F10, the microentities' form F10_BS, read with the same formulas), the profit and loss account in full (F20, OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 600, rows 01–70), F30 for the larger entities (anexa nr. 4 pct. 7) and F40 (the microentities' form). Core `src/ro/annual/s1003*.ts` on the statements' shared machinery (`form.ts`: identification, rule DSL, completion, checks, XML; S1005 runs on it unchanged). F20's account formulas are chapter XIX's of S1005, row for row (`s1003F20FromS1005Xix`); the rows, codes and labels are transcribed from ANAF's form and structure and checked against the schema s1003_20260210.xsd; the controls are the structure's (F20_1–42, F30_1–100, F10_F20_1–4, F10_F30_1–4, F10_F40_1–6, with F10_BS and F40 shared). The structure's null rows (F20 rd. 01b, 05, 38, 67; F30 rd. 170e, 170f, 165b col. 1) are never written. ANAF's validator J14.0.0 for 2025 checks the schema, the identification (R22a, the CAEN lists, the namespace) and the ranges, but its 2025 rule set holds none of the form's arithmetic (2024's did): the arithmetic is the writer's check alone, as errors, and the official test pins that the validator accepts what only the writer rejects. Only 2025: 2026 is a `FORMAT_GAP` until ANAF publishes its package.
- **F30 from the registers** (both statements; core `informative.ts`, engine `modules/annual.ts`): chapter I from F20; chapter II's supplier debts past their due date by days overdue (over 30, 90, 365; 401/403/408 current activity, 404/405 investment), asked when an invoice has no due date; chapter III from the employment contracts (the 12-month mean, rounded half up, and the employees on 31 December); chapter IV's overdue receivables from the open items, the state or private split asked; chapter VIII's balances on their side (gross, per partner on the accounts kept on open items), the non-resident parts from the partners' country, the "din ct." parts 0 when the accounts have no balance and asked otherwise, an SRL's capital all social parts; chapter IX from 621; chapters XV and XVI from the dividends booked (Dr 117 or 463 / Cr 457); S1003's chapter XIX (calamities) from 6587. Everything else is asked by chapter (`annual.informative`: the rows asked, or "nothing to report", with the documents), the previous year's flow rows too when that year is not kept. A chapter unanswered refuses the preparation (`F30_CHAPTER`); an answer for a row not asked is refused. Informative rows with nothing to report are left out of the XML (ANAF's parser refuses an element with more than 200 attributes); chapter I and S1005's chapter XIX keep their zeros.
- **Notes and accompanying documents** (`documents.ts`): owed with the statements are the explanatory notes (small entities; microentities the pct. 576 alin. (2) items), the administrators' report (Legea 82/1991 art. 28 alin. (7), art. 29 alin. (1); OMFP pct. 489–492, without pct. 491 alin. (1)'s non-financial items), the proposal to distribute the profit or cover the loss (art. 29 alin. (1)), the declaration of art. 30, and the audit or censors' report when the statements are audited or verified. The engine drafts the first four from the books, the accounting-policy facts (`policy.*`), F40 and a person's disclosures (`annual.disclosures`: parent company, commitments and off-balance-sheet arrangements, advances to management, exceptional items, debts after five years and secured debts, events after the balance sheet date, related-party transactions, going concern); what is a judgment (risks, the foreseeable development, the profit's destination) is left marked for the administrator. Each is owed until a person supplies the signed file (`annual.document`, tied to the draft's hash). The notes follow the stored OMFP nr. 1.802/2014, which is its 2014 text (pct. 468–469 and 471 lit. a), m), p), q), r) for small entities); the consolidated numbering is for the expert.
- **Signatures** (art. 28 alin. (10), (11), (13); OMF pct. 1.6): the prepared statement records its signature roles: the administrator, and the accountant with quality 11–13 (employed: the books must show an employment contract in the year, else `SIGNATURE_NOT_EMPLOYED`) or 21–22 (a CECCAR member, with membership number and fiscal code). Authorizing checks them (`SIGNATURE_*`) and that every owed document was supplied (`DOCUMENT_MISSING`), through the declaration framework's `authorization` hook.
- **The inactivity declaration (S1046):** a company that states it has carried out no activity since it was set up (`activity.none`, a person's fact with evidence) files S1046 instead of statements within 60 days (art. 36 alin. (2), OMF pct. 3.3); it is refused when the books show revenue or expense. Statements prepared from books with no revenue or expense ask the fact (`ACTIVITY_UNKNOWN`), never assume it. Structure A1.0.0 (an ≥ 2015), validator J1.2.0; only 2025 is written.
- **The half-year report** (`half-year.ts`, `ro.halfyear.report`): owed for 30 June 2025 by the entities whose net turnover of 2024 exceeded 1.000.000 € at the BNR rate of the year's close (OMF nr. 1.194/2025 art. 2, anexa pct. 16–17, by 18 August 2025); none for 2026 (no order, and ANAF lists no S1027–S1050 for 30 June 2026). The engine does not prepare it. It is an obligation of the closure (`SF.halfyear`, period `YYYY-06`, rule `obligation.halfyear@YYYY`, Y1): owed, UNSUPPORTED (`HALFYEAR_REPORT`, kept outside, explicitly assigned); not knowable, NEEDS_EVIDENCE with its question (`year.figures` for the previous year's net turnover, `evidence.missing` for the BNR rate); a year no order is read for, `RULE_GAP`. The calendar and its worklist items show it; the annual statements' detector no longer does.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/annual/agent.ts`): document types (the signed statements and documents, the shareholders' decision, the audit report, the shareholders' register, contracts, ANAF's statement of account), the three question kinds and `profile.fact` for `activity.none`, the `annual.input` detector (chapters, disclosures and documents a person owes), no template fields. `annual.ask {kind, year}` (agents may) opens the questions; the worklist's step is that.
- Not built yet: the statements of medium and large or audited entities (S1002, UNSUPPORTED). The inventory, impairments and provisions are [Year-end in full](#year-end-in-full-y1).

### Year-end in full (Y1)

The annual inventory, the year-end impairments and provisions, the cut-off of the year's last day, the reviewed year close with its lock, and the next year's opening checked against it. Core `src/ro/yearend` (`documents.ts`, `inventory.ts`, `treatments.ts`, `closing.ts`, `questions.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `agent.ts`), engine `modules/yearend.ts`. Sources: OMF nr. 2.861/2009 (the inventory's norms, its 2009 text as ANAF publishes it, hashed in the source register; the legislative portal's copy, registered as `OMF2861/2009-A`, shows no amendment since), OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 (its stored 2014 text: pct. 82–95, 325, 331–332, 335, 338, 351, 353, 369–388 and the functions of 151, 291, 428, 461, 475, 491, 658, 758) and the Codul fiscal in ANAF's consolidation (art. 25 alin. (4) lit. c), 26, 304–305 and norme pct. 78–79). Three parameters, all `extracted` with review specs: `ro.inventory.accounts`, `ro.impairment.accounts`, `ro.provisions.accounts`.

- **The inventory lists** are documents (`documents.record`, kind `inventory.list`, body `extracted-inventory-list-1`), each amount cited like the tax account's statement: the date (the year's last day for the annual inventory, pct. 4), the commission's appointing decision and members (pct. 6), and any of the sections: fixed assets by inventory number, found or missing, with the commission's value when below the books' and that of an asset found in surplus (pct. 34 alin. (2), 37–38); cash counted per cash desk and currency (pct. 29 alin. (3)); bank balances on the year-end statements (alin. (2)); partners' balances as confirmed, disputed or unanswered (pct. 28); stock by quantity. Their consistency (a number listed twice, a missing asset with a value, a confirmed balance without its amount, a commission appointed after the inventory) is the blocking `TOTALS` check; the company must be the list's entity. Identity: the date and the title. They are no booking's subject: the documents detector leaves them out.
- **`inventory.compare {date}`** (read): the lists of a date against the books on it: every card of the register in use (at its value less the straight-line depreciation due and the impairment held) found, missing, below its value or unlisted; each cash desk and bank used in the year counted or confirmed in its currency; each listed partner's confirmation against its balance per account and currency (only those listed: the norms ask for the balances that weigh); stock is compared with the stock register when the register explains every stock account (G1, `inventory.stock` books its differences), otherwise UNSUPPORTED (`INVENTORY_STOCK`).
- **`inventory.differences {date, documentIds, evidenceId, imputations, surplus}`** books the differences on the inventory's date (pct. 95 alin. (2)), citing the administrator's decision on the commission's proces-verbal (pct. 42–43; `INVENTORY_DECISION` acknowledged at approval):
  - a cash shortage imputed to the person found guilty, at exactly the amount missing: Dr 4282 (an employee, the line tagged with the person) or Dr 461 (another person, an open item) / Cr 5311; not imputed, Dr 6588; a surplus, Dr 5311 / Cr 7588. A cash desk in a currency is refused (`INVENTORY_CASH_CURRENCY`);
  - a missing fixed asset, once taken out of the register at the date (`assets.dispose`, then `assets.derecognize`; otherwise `INVENTORY_ASSET_DISPOSAL`), imputed at the replacement value (pct. 40 alin. (2): the cost of a similar asset with the non-recoverable taxes, VAT included): Dr 4282 or 461 / Cr 7581, without VAT collected (norme pct. 78 alin. (6) lit. a), pct. 79 alin. (10)). For a VAT payer it acknowledges `CAPITAL_GOODS_CESSATION`: a capital good in its adjustment period whose shortage is not a proven loss is adjusted with `vat.capitalGoods` (event `cessation`, cause `shortage`). Not imputed, for a profit payer it acknowledges `PROFIT_SHORTAGE` (art. 25 alin. (4) lit. c)): the fiscal value the profit tax deducts at the disposal is to be stated non-deductible;
  - a fixed asset found in surplus, registered on its card at the commission's value: Dr its account / Cr 4754 (the function of 475), acknowledging `SURPLUS_RELEASE`: releasing 4754 to revenue with the depreciation is not automated;
  - an impaired asset is `impairment.assets`'; bank and partner differences are investigated, never booked from a list; an imputation that matches no shortage is refused (`IMPUTATION_UNMATCHED`); nothing to book is `NOTHING_TO_BOOK`.
- **`impairment.receivables {yearEnd, evidenceId, round}`** (pct. 89, 332, 353): the company's written policy (`policy.impairment.receivables`: `individual`, every overdue receivable tested, or `overdue_90`/`180`/`270`/`365`) decides which customers are tested at the year's end; a tested customer's receivables (4111, open, opened by the year's end, at their year-end balance) need the person's answer (`receivable.collectibility`: collectible, doubtful, in litigation, in insolvency or in a bankruptcy declared, the share recoverable, guaranteed, affiliated, with the documents), asked as `RECEIVABLE_COLLECTIBILITY`, never assumed; without the policy, `POLICY_MISSING`. Each receivable's allowance is brought to its balance less the share recoverable, on 491 tagged per receivable (Dr 6814 / Cr 491); what is no longer needed (collected, recovered, not tested any more) is reversed (Dr 491 / Cr 7814). The receivable stays on 4111: 4118 is not held per partner in the engine's chart (pct. 331 is not followed; the gap is in the spec).
- **`impairment.assets {date, assets: [{assetId, value, evidenceId, irreversible, deductible}], evidenceId}`** (pct. 84–86, 95 alin. (3)): the net value at the date (value less the depreciation due, less the impairment held) above the value the commission or an authorized evaluator set is impaired on the asset's 29x (`ro.impairment.accounts`: 2903–2917 by its account; Dr 6813 / Cr 29x), reversed when the value rose or the asset left use (Dr 29x / Cr 7813). The asset stays at its entry value and is depreciated on it. An irreversible impairment (additional depreciation) is refused (`IMPAIRMENT_IRREVERSIBLE`).
- **`provisions.record {date, kind, reference, amount, evidenceId, deductible}`** (pct. 369–388): a provision brought to the best estimate of its obligation, per kind (litigation 1511, warranty 1512, restructuring 1514, pension 1515, tax 1516, termination 1517, bonus, leave and other 1518) and obligation (tagged `provision:<kind>:<reference>`): Dr 6812 / Cr 151x, reduced or cancelled Dr 151x / Cr 7812. Untaken leave and bonuses measured on the payroll records are a debt to the staff instead (Dr 641 / Cr 4281, pct. 338 alin. (2): the reading under review). Untaken leave reads the person's answer (`leave.untaken`: the days, the pay at each contract's daily average, the employer's contributions, whether measured on the payroll, with the computation; `LEAVE_UNTAKEN` until then, `LEAVE_AMOUNT` when it differs); at a year's end the other kinds must match the year's review of obligations (`provisions.review`; `PROVISION_AMOUNT`, `PROVISION_UNLISTED`).
- **The profit tax** (art. 26): each impairment and provision posting records in `explanation.details.profit` what the bridge reads of it: an impairment of receivables unpaid more than 270 days after their due date, not guaranteed, not affiliated, as the `receivables` base whose 30% is deductible (lit. c)); one in a bankruptcy declared, not guaranteed, not affiliated, 100% deductible (lit. j)); a fixed asset destroyed by a calamity or insured (lit. n)); a warranty provision within the contracts' rates (lit. b)). `yearend.profitDeterminations {year}` (read) turns the year's postings into `tax.profit`'s `determinations`, and the reversals of allowances never deducted into `nontaxable` ones (art. 23 lit. d)); everything else stays added back (rd. 29).
- **The review** (`yearend.review {financialYearId}`, read; the worklist's `yearend.review` items from the year's last month until the year is closed), every item with the step that moves it on:
  - the inventory: no list for the year's last day, an asset, cash desk or bank account the lists leave out (`inventory.lists`); differences no `inventory.differences` books, an impaired asset no `impairment.assets` books, a bank balance that differs from its statement, stock (UNSUPPORTED), differences in single entry (UNSUPPORTED) (`inventory.differences`, `inventory.stock`); a partner that disputed or did not reply (`inventory.partners`, a warning);
  - impairment: receivables overdue at the year's end without the policy, a tested customer without the answer, answers calling for an impairment no posting of the year's end books (`impairment.receivables`);
  - provisions: an employer (an employment contract in the year) without the untaken leave's answer, or whose debt or provision differs from it, a previous year's leave still held; a company without the review of its obligations, an obligation not booked at its estimate, a provision no longer listed (`provisions`);
  - the cut-off: accruals (408, 418) still open (a warning); deferrals (471, 472) whose balance is not the part of their schedule due after the year's end; every foreign-currency monetary item and treasury balance not at the closing rate (pct. 94, 325), and currency balances on 5x and 267 that `fx.revalue` does not reach (UNSUPPORTED); e-Factura invoices dated in the year not booked (unless disposed as needing no booking); bank movements of the year not explained (`cutoff`);
  - the result: for a profit payer the year's `tax.profit` (impairments and provisions come first, pct. 388 alin. (3)), then the year's revenue and expenses closed into 121 (`result`).
- **`fx.revalue`** revalues each foreign item at its balance on the revaluation date (version 2): a year-end revaluation posted in February, after January's entries, revalues the balance of 31 December, not today's.
- **The year close** is a reviewed action, `years.close.request {financialYearId}` (agents may request; a person approves, `actions.approve`, and `actions.execute` runs it). Its preview blocks on a year already closed, an earlier year with entries not closed, any month of the year with entries (and the year's last month) not closed (`MONTHS_OPEN`), and every blocking review item (`YEAR_END_*`); the review's warnings stay warnings. It records the itemized closing snapshot: every account's balance per currency, every open item's, every fixed asset in use (value, depreciation, impairment, and the fiscal basis version, recorded but not compared at the next opening) and what the next year carries (the loss vintages after the year's profit tax, the definitive pro-rata), with its hash. Executed, it locks the year: the database refuses any entry dated in it (`inv:closed_year`) and the reopening of its months (`inv:closed_year_month`; `periods.reopen.request` shows `YEAR_CLOSED`). `years.reopen.request {financialYearId, reason}` (people only) reopens it, refused once a later year is closed or the year's statements were submitted (`STATEMENTS_FILED`: a correction is then `correction.priorYear` on 1174, the allowed correction in the open year).
- **The next year's opening** (`years.opening {financialYearId}`, read): the previous year's recorded snapshot against the same snapshot recomputed now, per account, open item, asset and carried balance, and any opening entry (DESCHIDERE, not a storno) in a year whose previous year the books keep, which would count the balances twice (`ledger.general` and the database refuse one since; the check stays for books posted before). Every month of the next year is checked at its close: `YEAR_OPENING_DIFFERS` and `YEAR_OPENING_DUPLICATE` block. What the snapshot reads cannot change under the lock: entries are refused in the year, so are the register's cards and disposals dated by its end, and an asset's fiscal basis is not compared. The ledger is continuous, so `years.create` adds the year and nothing is copied: the loss vintages, the fiscal bases, the capital goods' register and the pro-rata are read from the books as they stand.
