The years of a kind follow one another without an overlap or a gap (`YEAR_OVERLAP`, `YEAR_GAP`; the database too, `inv:year_overlap`, `inv:year_contiguous`: a date in two financial years would be in none, and nothing could be posted there), and no financial year is added before an opening entry that stands (`YEAR_BEFORE_OPENING`, `inv:year_before_opening`).
- **The month close** of a financial year's last month warns `YEAR_END_REVIEW` with the blocking review items: the year close is the gate.
- **Single entry** (PFA, II, IF): the inventory's lists cover the register (the Registrul-inventar is `pfa.inventory`), and the review asks nothing else; differences are UNSUPPORTED; `inventory.differences`, `impairment.*` and `provisions.record` are `SINGLE_ENTRY_EXCLUDED`; the year closes and locks the same way.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/yearend/agent.ts`): eight document types (the lists, the commission's decision and proces-verbal, the partners' confirmations, the collectibility evidence, the evaluator's report, the leave computation, the provisions' evidence, the impairment policy), the three question kinds and `profile.fact` for the policy, the `yearend.review` detector's ten checks with their rules and steps, no template fields.
- **Review specs** (`reviews/specs/yearend.ts`): the imputed shortage's revenue (7581 or 7588), the impairment's base (the receivable with VAT or without), untaken leave measured on the payroll (a debt on 4281 or a provision on 1518), each with cases the engine computes.
- **Not built:** compensation of shortages with surpluses and the perishability allowances (pct. 40 alin. (3)–(5), 41; stock differences are G1's `inventory.stock`); recovering an imputation from the salary; releasing 4754 with depreciation; additional depreciation for an irreversible impairment; writing off receivables (654) and the VAT adjustment at a customer's bankruptcy (art. 287 lit. d)); moving doubtful receivables to 4118; a cash desk's difference in a currency; inventory differences in single entry.

### The stock register (G1)

Items kept by quantity and value in warehouses, from the entries that move them. Core `src/ro/stock` (`valuation.ts`, `treatments.ts`, `invoices.ts`, `count.ts`, `inventory.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `questions.ts`, `agent.ts`), `src/ro/saft/stock.ts`; engine `modules/stock.ts`, `modules/d406-stock.ts`. Sources: OMFP nr. 1.802/2014, its 2014 text and the portal's consolidation to 1 February 2022 (pct. 8 pct. 6, 75–76, 88, 95–96, 272–276, 283–292, the class 3 chart and the functions of 301–308, 371–378, 381–388, 39x, 401, 408, 4428, 60x, 681, 707, 781), OMF nr. 2.861/2009 (pct. 40–41), the Codul fiscal in ANAF's consolidation (art. 25 alin. (3) lit. d), (4) lit. c), 26 alin. (1), 304, norme pct. 7 alin. (5), pct. 78 alin. (6), (10)–(13)), OPANAF nr. 1.783/2021 (anexa nr. 4 pct. 9–10), ANAF's SAF-T guide, answers and structure, and OMFP nr. 2.634/2015 (its order and annexes 1 and 4 only: the forms' own norms and models, annexes 2–3 in MO 910 bis, were not obtained). Three parameters, `extracted` with review specs: `ro.stock.accounts`, `ro.stock.methods` and `ro.stock.returns`.

- **The kernel (`effect@2`).** An effect may carry `stockLayers` (the lots it creates: a FIFO or LIFO lot of one receipt, or an item's weighted-average pool in a warehouse, each on its item's stock account, with whether its quantity may go negative) and `stockMoves` (item, warehouse, account, layer, a signed quantity in thousandths of the item's unit, a signed value in bani, and the effect line it explains). An effect without them stays `effect@1`, keeps neither key and hashes as before; every existing proposal and effect hashes the same. The accounts the register explains by quantity are 301–303, 331–332, 341–347, 351–357, 361, 371 and 381 with their analytics (`stockAccountPattern`); price differences (308, 348, 358, 368, 378, 388), stock in transit (32x) and the adjustments (39x) hold values only. `validateEffect` checks, for `effect@2`: a created layer is on its item's stock account, in a known warehouse, and used; each move is on its layer's item, warehouse and account; the moves naming a line add up to its signed amount (debit positive); moves naming none move goods between warehouses and net to zero per account (value) and per account and item (quantity); no layer ends below zero unless it may, nor holds a value without a quantity. A stock-account line no move names is outside the register.
- **Materialized and guarded like lines.** `acc_stock_layers` (created by their entry) and `acc_stock_moves` (per entry, numbered) are written before the entry's header, which seals them (`inv:sealed_entry`); the entry guard checks them against the approved effect (`stock_layers_mismatch`, `stock_moves_mismatch`, an `effect@1` with none), each move on its layer and the layer on its item's stock account (`stock_move_layer`), the moves of a line against it (`stock_move_line`), the transfers (`stock_transfer`), and every layer the entry touches (`stock_layer_balance`). The catalogue (`acc_stock_items`: code, name, unit of the D406 list of UN/ECE codes, NC8, stock account, VAT category; `inv:stock_item_account`, `inv:stock_item_codes`) and the warehouses (`acc_warehouses`, with the keeper; `inv:warehouse_keeper`) are append-only.
- **Read sets.** A treatment reads positions (`ReadPlan.stock`: item and warehouse, the positions an entry touched, or the whole register, with the catalogue and each layer's flows in a range); a position's version is the number of moves posted on it, the whole register's all its moves. A move landing on a position read makes the proposal stale, at posting and in the database (`inv:stale_read`). A company that never moved stock reads nothing from the whole register, so what read it before hashes as it did.
- **Valuation** (`policy.stock.valuation`, a dated accounting-policy fact, pct. 96): `fifo` and `lifo` consume the lots received on or before the exit's date, oldest or newest first; `average` values an exit at the pool's weighted average after each receipt; `periodic` values it at the running average and `stock.periodCost {month}` brings the month's exits of each pool to the month's weighted average (opening stock and the month's entries), against the stock account's expense, on its last day. An exit reads what its layers held on its own date (the read `StockQuery.dated`: each layer's moves per day of the months still open, nothing being posted in a closed one; the plan need not know the date, an invoice's): one dated before moves already posted (a sale booked after a later receipt) takes what the position held then, at that date's average, and no more than it holds on any later day, so none goes below zero (`STOCK_INSUFFICIENT`; a FIFO lot emptied on the date and restored later by a storno is not available then). The month's entries and exits net their reversals and returns (`StockFlows`): a move enters or leaves by the line it explains, a debit or a credit of the stock account (a red storno of a receipt and goods given back to the supplier are negative entries, a customer's return a negative exit), a reversal's move by the move it mirrors (a black storno too), a transfer by its quantity's sign; a receipt reversed in the month is neither an entry nor an exit. LIFO is allowed (pct. 96 alin. (1) lit. c)), as the task's premise said otherwise. A position held by another method than the policy at the date is refused (`STOCK_METHOD_CHANGED`, pct. 287). `policy.stock.negative` `allow` lets an average pool go below zero (an exit valued at its average; one beyond the stock on its date at the average the pool holds now; the receipt that brings it back to zero or above values the units taken beyond the stock at its own unit cost, the difference corrected against the expense, `stock.receive` included, so what is left holds the receipt's cost); FIFO and LIFO lots never do (`STOCK_INSUFFICIENT`). `policy.stock.inventory` `permanent` keeps the register; `intermittent` (pct. 289–291, allowed to any entity) books purchases on 60x and sets the stock at each count (`stock.intermittent`), and the other treatments refuse (`STOCK_INTERMITTENT`). Without these facts a treatment asks for them (`POLICY_MISSING`, a `profile.fact` question).
- **Documents and treatments:**
  - **Purchases** (`invoices.recognize` with `stock: {warehouseId, lines: [{line, itemId, quantity, received?}]}`; the NIR): a purchase part may then be on the items' stock accounts (otherwise refused as before, `ACCOUNT`); each mapped invoice line's share of its part (its non-deductible VAT included; lines of the category no item names are charges spread over the items, acknowledged `STOCK_CHARGES`, pct. 8 pct. 6) enters the item's lot or pool. A line received short leaves out the missing share, claimed from the supplier on 461 until its credit note; one received in excess enters the excess at the invoice's unit cost, owed on 408 (`RECEIPT_DIFFERENCE`, ack). The invoice's quantities are as the input states them: the parsed e-Factura body keeps no quantities, so the reviewer checks them against the invoice. Differences on an invoice in a currency are refused.
  - **Goods without their invoice** (`stock.receive`, the NIR on the delivery note): in at the note's prices against 408 (pct. 284 alin. (2) lit. b)).
  - **Goods on a fiscal receipt** (`receipts.recognize` with `stock`, lines by their position from 1): as a purchase.
  - **Sales** (`invoices.recognize` sale with `stock`, or `sales.issue` with `stock: {warehouseId, lines: [{index, itemId}]}`, the line's unit the item's): each line's goods leave at the policy's value, Cr 371 / Dr 607. Only goods (371) and the company's products (341, 345, 346, G4: Cr 345 / Dr 711) are sold so (`STOCK_SALE_ACCOUNT`).
  - **Consumption** (`stock.consume`, the bon de consum): 30x and 381 onto their expense (601, 602x, 603, 608) or the slip's 6xx; goods are not consumed (`STOCK_CONSUME_GOODS`).
  - **Transfers** (`stock.transfer`): a memo entry with moves only; FIFO and LIFO lots keep their receipt date; not under the periodic average.
  - **Returns:** to the supplier on its credit note (`invoices.credit` with `stock`): out of the lots its invoice created, or the pool, at the credited value, valued on the note's date (6 November 2026). A lot or the pool must hold the goods on that date and on every later day (`STOCK_RETURN_CONSUMED`, with what was available; a pool `policy.stock.negative` allows below zero excepted, which gives the price): one dated when they had already left is refused though a later move brought the position back. A lot or pool the return empties on its date gives what it held then, the rest to the stock account's expense (Dr 3xx / Cr 60x, or the reverse), so the difference no longer falls on goods a later receipt brought in; one whose value on the date is less than the price gives its average. Out of a pool under the weighted average after each receipt, as `ro.stock.returns` reads it (`extracted`, review spec): `purchase`, the engine's, at the purchase price, the pool's average formed again on the stock left, which keeps the difference against the date's average. OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 has no rule for returns to suppliers (its only returns are a customer's, pct. 330, which correct the sale's accounts); pct. 95 alin. (1) takes goods out "la valoarea lor de intrare sau valoarea la care sunt înregistrate în contabilitate", the goods of the note's invoice entered at their purchase price and the function of 371 debits purchases at it (401), and a later discount corrects the stock still held (pct. 76 alin. (2)). The alternative, `average`, takes the date's average, the difference to the expense (pct. 96 alin. (1)–(2): the cost of fungible stock is the method's; pct. 285 alin. (4)). The periodic average takes the purchase price either way (a negative entry of the month, which `stock.periodCost` averages). A line with quantity 0 corrects the cost of what is still held, and one on goods no longer held is refused toward 609 (pct. 76). From the customer on the company's credit note: back at the cost the original sale took them out at (its moves' average for the item), Dr 371 / Cr 607 as a red storno (pct. 330); a return of an earlier year's sale is refused (`STOCK_RETURN_PRIOR_YEAR`: 418 and 408 at the balance date).
  - **The takeover** (`stock.opening`): a balance the ledger already holds outside the register (an opening, earlier bookings) taken in item by item at the inventory's values, debit with moves and credit outside, at most what the account holds outside (`STOCK_OPENING_EXCEEDS`).
  - **Reversals** (`ledger.reverse`) mirror the moves on the same layers and lines; a memo transfer reverses as a memo. Goods a storno takes out are valued on its date like a return to the supplier (`StockBook.reverse`, the read `dated`): FIFO and LIFO take the receipt's own lot, refused when it did not hold them on the date (`STOCK_INSUFFICIENT`; a consumption reversed after that date does not restore it then); a pool as `ro.stock.returns` reads it, a layer emptied on the date giving what it held then; the differences go to the expense on lines after the mirrored ones, their moves after the mirrored moves, so each mirrored move keeps the number of its original (`StockFlows`). Moves between warehouses stay exact mirrors, the destination holding them on the date.
- **The retail price method** (378, 4428; pct. 286 alin. (8), allowed in retail trade): built in G2 ([below](#retail-and-b2c-g2-z-reports-the-retail-price-method-b2c-e-factura)) as a value-only register of its own, beside this one. The method is a warehouse's (`retailMethodAt`): its own dated fact `policy.stock.retail.<warehouseId>` (`cost` or `retail`, with the policy as evidence; `profile.record` checks the warehouse exists), else the company's `policy.stock.retail`. A shop can so keep a depot by item at cost beside a warehouse at retail price, stocks of a different use (pct. 287 alin. (3); a different place alone does not justify it, alin. (4), which the person recording the fact answers for). The quantity treatments refuse a warehouse at retail price (`STOCK_RETAIL_METHOD`, naming it): its goods are kept globally, without quantities; a treatment over the register as a whole (the periodic costing, production's costing) names no warehouse, and the count refuses only the warehouses its lists name.
- **Counts:** `inventory.stock {date, documentIds, evidenceId, causes}` books the differences of the lists (each stock row by its catalogue code, unit and warehouse code; a surplus with the commission's value) against the register on the date: a shortage leaves at the policy's value onto the stock account's expense; its deducted VAT is adjusted (Dr 635 / Cr 4426, tax code ADJ-304, D300 rd. 34) at the lot's acquisition rate, or the count's for a pool (norme pct. 78 alin. (13)), unless the cause, with its proof, is a calamity, a theft shown, degraded goods destroyed, or a perishable or technological loss within the legal limits (pct. 78 alin. (10); the limits are not in the source register, acknowledged `STOCK_LOSS_LIMITS`); a shortage imputed is charged at the replacement value (4282 or 461 against 7581, no VAT collected, pct. 78 alin. (6)); an unimputable one is acknowledged non-deductible (`PROFIT_SHORTAGE`, art. 25 alin. (4) lit. c)). A surplus enters at the commission's value against the stock account's expense (`ro.stock.accounts` count, the reading under review). Compensating shortages with surpluses (OMF nr. 2.861/2009 pct. 40 alin. (3)–(5)) is not built. `inventory.compare` and the year-end review compare the stock with the register when the register explains every stock account, and `inventory.differences` leaves the stock to `inventory.stock`; stock outside the register stays `INVENTORY_STOCK` (UNSUPPORTED until taken over).
- **Write-downs** (`impairment.stock {date, evidenceId, items: [{itemId, value}]}`, pct. 88, 292): each item's allowance on its 39x (391, 3921, 3922, 397, 398), tagged by item, brought to its cost less the net realizable value (Dr 6814 / Cr 39x), released when the value rose or the goods left (Dr 39x / Cr 7814, pct. 95 alin. (3)); non-deductible for the profit tax (art. 26 names no stock).
- **The register's reads:** `stock.register {date?, itemId?, warehouseId?, layers}` (each position's quantity and value, and per stock account the register, the ledger and what the ledger holds outside the register), `stock.card {itemId, warehouseId, from?, to?}` (the fișa de magazie), `stock.items.*`, `stock.warehouses.*`.
- **The month close** blocks `STOCK_LEDGER_DIFFERS` when a company keeping the register holds a stock account's balance outside it (margin-scheme items apart), and `STOCK_PERIOD_COST_MISSING` when a periodic pool's exits of the month are not at the month's average and no `stock.periodCost` stands, read as the costing reads them (a receipt reversed, or exits already at the average, ask for none, the costing having nothing to book). The close of a 100,000-line year with 10,080 stock moves stays under a second a month.
- **D406** ([Declarations](#declarations)): the on-demand stock file (`declarations.prepare` D406, period `2026-03-C` or `2026-Q1-C`, HeaderComment C): the stock accounts with their balances, UOMTable, MovementTypeTable, Products (NC8 or 0, the valuation method), PhysicalStock per warehouse, product and lot (FIFO, LIFO) or pool with its opening and closing quantity and value, the company as the owner, and MovementOfGoods: each entry's moves as a movement of the nomenclator's type (10 purchase, 30 sale, 40 a customer's return, 50 a return to the supplier, 60 a price credited or a return's difference, 70 consumption, 80 transfer, 110 and 120 a count's surplus and shortage, 101 the periodic costing, 180 otherwise, a storno's difference included), the partner's code on purchases and sales and the company's on both sides otherwise. ValuationMethod is `FIFO`, `LIFO` or `CMP` (ANAF's guide, sec. 2.9: "metoda de evaluare (FIFO, LIFO, CMP)"); until 6 November 2026 a weighted-average company's file wrote the structure's `Cost mediu`, which exceeds SAFcodeType's 9 characters, and ANAF's validator refused it. It passes ANAF's SAF-T validator (FIFO, and the weighted average with returns and a storno valued on their date, `d406-stock.integration.test.ts`). MovementOfGoods, PhysicalStock, MovementTypeTable and Owners are not in the monthly file (the structure's column T: N/A), so the monthly D406 is unchanged.
- **Closure:** `activity.goods` `yes` is covered once `policy.stock.valuation`, `policy.stock.inventory` and `policy.stock.negative` are recorded (`FACT_UNKNOWN` until then), the first and the last for every warehouse kept at cost: a company at retail price whose own facts keep warehouses at cost (`policy.stock.retail.<warehouseId>` `cost`) is asked once for all of them, on the worklist (`closure.need`), rather than by each treatment on them (`POLICY_MISSING`); `ro-closure@9`; `GOODS_STOCK` remains only for class 3 lines the register does not explain (an `effect@1` booking, a price difference), opening balances and takeovers apart. A dealer's margin-scheme items keep their per-document register (V4) and coexist with the stock register.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/stock/agent.ts`): nine document types (the policies, the supplier's invoice, the NIR, the delivery note, the consumption and transfer slips, the inventory list, a shortage's proof, the net realizable value), the question kind `stock.item` (which catalogue item a supplier's product is, and the units per invoice unit, held until changed) and `profile.fact` for the policies, the detectors (`close.finding`, `question.needed`, `yearend.review`, `document.unproposed`), no template fields.
- **Review specs** (`reviews/specs/stock.ts`): a count's surplus (the stock account's expense or 7588), the periodic average's period (the month or the quarter), and goods back to the supplier out of a weighted-average pool (`ro.stock.returns`: the purchase price or the date's average; its cases are stornos after an exit and a later receipt, before any exit, and one emptying the pool), each with cases the engine computes.
- **Not built:** stock held by third parties (35x) and biological assets (361), kept by quantity but booked by no treatment (products and work in progress are production's, G4, [below](#production-g4-bills-of-materials-work-orders-work-in-progress-the-standard-and-the-actual-cost)); compensating shortages with surpluses; the legal perishability limits; invoice quantities verified against the e-Factura XML; a supplier's or a customer's return across years; transfers under the periodic average; a change of output method; margin-scheme items moved into the register.

### Retail and B2C (G2): Z reports, the retail price method, B2C e-Factura

A shop's sales through its fiscal cash register, the goods it keeps at sale price, and its invoices to individuals. Core `src/ro/retail` (`documents.ts`, `zreport.ts`, `invoiced.ts`, `retail.ts`, `accounts.ts`, `b2c.ts`, `parameters.ts`, `questions.ts`, `agent.ts`), sources `src/ro/sources/retail.ts`; engine `modules/retail.ts`, with `modules/sales.ts`, `modules/d394.ts`, `modules/d406.ts`, `kernel/work.ts`. Sources: OUG nr. 28/1999 (art. 1 alin. (8), 3 alin. (3)–(5), 3^1, 4 alin. (5)–(9), 5 alin. (3), 6, in the portal's text, which stops at 2018), OPANAF nr. 146/2018 (anexa nr. 2 secțiunile II.7, II.12–II.14) and ANAF's A4203 structure and schema (27.09.2018), OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 (pct. 286 alin. (4)–(8), 291 alin. (5), 302 alin. (2), the function of 532), the Codul fiscal's norms (title VII pct. 97 alin. (1)), ANAF's SAF-T answers (the Z report in the General Ledger), D394's structure (op2, rezumat2, informatii) and OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 anexa nr. 2 (lit. G), OUG nr. 120/2021 as consolidated to 01.06.2026 (art. 10 alin. (7), 10^1) and ANAF's guide to the invoice codes 2.9 (BT-47). Four parameters, `extracted`, with three review specs (`reviews/specs/retail.ts`): `ro.retail.zreport`, `ro.stock.retail.coefficient`, `ro.efactura.b2c`, `ro.deadline.efactura`.

- **The Z report** (raportul fiscal de închidere zilnică) is a document of kind `zreport`, authority `extracted` or `amef`:
  - **From a photo or a PDF:** an agent reads it with `documents.record` and cites each value (OUG nr. 28/1999 art. 4 alin. (5)–(9)): the seller (the company), the register's unique number, the report's number, date and time, the receipts, each rate's total with VAT and its VAT, exempt operations, taxes outside the base, the totals, receipts with the buyer's code, cancellations, discounts, surcharges, service sums, each payment method, the electronic journal's identifier, the fiscal memory's running total and, where the register prints them, its articles (code, name, quantity, unit, value, rate). The totals must agree (`TOTALS`: rates against the total, the VAT included at each rate within a ban per rate, payments against the total).
  - **From the register's export** (`retail.importZ`, the A4203 file of OPANAF nr. 146/2018, plain or signed PKCS#7, whose signature is unwrapped and not verified): the `rB` section's identifier (the register's 10 characters, date and time, report number), totals, payments by type (`pl`) and rates (`coteZ`). The register must be recorded and the file's company is the company. No articles: the export has none.
  - **Identity:** register, number and date; a second photo of the same report joins the first one's group.
- **Booking** (`retail.zReport {documentId, cash, card?, tickets?, account | allocations, stock? | goods?}`, one entry per report): Cr 70x by rate (707 goods, 704 services, or allocations by rate), Cr 4427 by rate with a tax entry `S-<rate>` (`documentType: zreport`, the register, number and receipts); Dr 5311 (the cash desk's analytic) the cash, Dr 5125 the card payments (sums in transit, pct. 302 alin. (2)), tagged by the report, Dr 5328 meal and value tickets (`ro.retail.zreport` tickets, acknowledged `ZREPORT_TICKETS`: 532 holds tickets received, the reading under review). The goods leave stock:
  - at cost, by the G1 register: each article line mapped to its catalogue item (`stock.lines: [{line, itemId}]`, the quantity from the report), out at the policy's value, Cr 371 / Dr 607; a report with only totals asks which goods left (`retail.goods`, the question's answer the items and quantities with the evidence, e.g. the shop's sales report) and books them once answered (`ZREPORT_GOODS` until then);
  - at sale price (the retail method, `goods: {warehouseId}`): Cr 371.<warehouse>-<rate> / Dr 607 at each rate's value with VAT, discharged monthly (below). Which of the two is the warehouse's method (`policy.stock.retail.<warehouseId>`, else the company's); the other is refused (`RETAIL_POLICY`).
  - Refused, never approximated: a currency other than lei; a company under VAT on collection (`ZREPORT_CASH_VAT`, UNSUPPORTED: the receipts' VAT is chargeable when paid, a rule the engine does not apply to Z reports yet); exempt operations (`ZREPORT_EXEMPT`) and taxes outside the base (`ZREPORT_OTHER_TAXES`, the SGR deposit among them); cash advances, vouchers, credit, modern and other methods (`ZREPORT_PAYMENT`, UNSUPPORTED in `ro.retail.zreport`); the intermittent inventory (`ZREPORT_INTERMITTENT`, info). A reduced rate is acknowledged (`REDUCED_RATE`).
- **The register's checks** at the month close (`retail.zreports`): a report of a register not recorded (`ZREPORT_REGISTER`, warn); a gap in a register's numbering since its first report (`ZREPORT_GAP`, block); a number at two dates (`ZREPORT_DUPLICATE`, block); a running total that does not follow from the previous report's plus the day's total (`ZREPORT_CUMULATIVE`, block, OUG nr. 28/1999 art. 3 alin. (3): the fiscal memory accumulates); a day since the register's start without a report (`ZREPORT_DAY`, warn: the shop may have been closed, which a person confirms with `work.dispose`); a report not booked (`ZREPORT_UNBOOKED`, block). A superseded extraction stands for its successor. Card sums in transit for more than a month warn (`CARDS_IN_TRANSIT`, `retail.cards`).
- **Card settlements** (`bank.settle` with `cards: {account, settles: [{documentId, amount}], fee, feeAccount}`): the acquirer's payment settles each report's card sum on 5125 (Cr 5125, tagged by the report) and its fee onto 627 (Dr 627); more than a report holds is refused (`CARDS_EXCESS`); only a receipt in lei (`CARDS`).
- **An invoice for a fiscal receipt** (`sales.issue` with `receipt: {zReportId, receipts}`, norme pct. 97 alin. (1): "conform bon fiscal nr. … din …" stands for the lines): one sale with the Z report. Its recognition is `retail.receiptInvoice`, a memo entry without lines (the Z report booked the revenue, VAT and payment) with two tax entries: the invoice's (attributes `fiscalReceipt`) and the same amounts taken out of the Z report (`invoiced`), so D300 counts the VAT once, D394 lists the invoice among the supplies and takes it out of op2 (OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 anexa nr. 2 lit. G), and the SAF-T GL keeps the Z report's entry. Refused: a receipt of another month than the invoice (`RECEIPT_INVOICE_PERIOD`, UNSUPPORTED: which return corrects it is not read), an invoice dated before the report (`RECEIPT_INVOICE_DATE`), or for more than the report holds at a rate (`RECEIPT_INVOICE_AMOUNT`). D406 refuses a period with one (`D406_RECEIPT_INVOICE`): ANAF's answer reports it in SalesInvoices with type 751 and code 310327, which the writer does not produce.
- **B2C e-Factura** (OUG nr. 120/2021 art. 10^1 alin. (2)–(4), `ro.efactura.b2c`): `sales.issue` to an individual (`ro_individual`) needs `b2c: {identification: 'cnp' | 'none'}` (`B2C_IDENTIFICATION` without it): with `cnp`, the CNP recorded with `parties.recordCnp` (`B2C_CNP` without it), opened from its seal for the XML only and masked in `sales.preview`; with `none`, 13 zeros (`0000000000000`) as the buyer's legal identifier, BT-47 (the CIUS-RO schematron was not obtained: whether it checks the field is not known). A buyer abroad (`B2C_COUNTRY`: `invoices.euConsumer` and V4) or with a VAT code (`B2C_VAT_ID`) is refused. The CNP is never logged or kept unsealed; only the invoice's XML, its evidence, carries it. An invoice for a fiscal receipt is transmitted as the parameter reads it (`receiptInvoices: transmit`, the reading under review: the exemption of art. 10 alin. (1) names simplified invoices, not the invoices issued for receipts).
- **The e-Factura deadline** (`ro.deadline.efactura`, 5 working days, art. 10 alin. (7), 10^1 alin. (2^1)): every issued invoice to a buyer in Romania without a live submission (none, or the last rejected or absent) is on the worklist (`efactura.due`, warn until its day, block after, the B2C invoices named), with `spv.submit` as its step. The latest date of art. 319 alin. (16) is not counted.
- **The retail price method** (`policy.stock.retail` `retail`, or a warehouse's own `policy.stock.retail.<warehouseId>` `retail`, pct. 286 alin. (8): goods in retail trade at their sale price with VAT; a warehouse at cost beside it keeps the stock register's): a warehouse's goods on analytics of 371 by VAT rate (`371.<code>-<rate>`), the markup on `378.<code>` and the VAT included on `4428.<code>-<rate>`, created by `retail.setup {warehouseId, rates}`. Those accounts are of a control kind of their own (`retail`), kept apart from VAT on collection's 4428 (tax-controlled, with its tax entries): every line on them carries the warehouse's tag (`retail:<warehouseId>`) and only the method's treatments write them (`RETAIL_ACCOUNT`; `inv:retail_line` in the database), so neither can reach the other. The value is kept without quantities (the global-value method; the intermittent inventory is not used in retail with it, pct. 291 alin. (5), `RETAIL_INTERMITTENT`). Treatments:
  - **The reception at sale price** (`invoices.recognize` with `retail: {warehouseId, evidenceId, prices: [{account, sale}]}`, the NIR): the cost on the goods' account as the invoice books it (its non-deductible VAT included), then Dr 371 / Cr 4428 the VAT the sale price includes and Dr 371 / Cr 378 the markup; a sale price below the cost is refused (`RETAIL_MARKUP_NEGATIVE`).
  - **The takeover** (`retail.opening`), **a change of prices** (`retail.reprice {date, warehouseId, evidenceId, changes: [{rate, amount}]}`, the markup recalculated, pct. 286 alin. (8); a lower price a red storno of the debit), the **exits** (the Z reports' goods, the count's shortages).
  - **The month's discharge** (`retail.discharge {month, warehouseId}`): the exits at sale price (the credits of the goods' accounts, a reversal counted on the side of what it reverses, `TurnoverQuery.sides`: a black storno of a reception is a negative entry, never an exit, and of a Z report a negative exit; the close's `retail.method` check reads them the same way), their VAT included (Dr 4428 / Cr 607) and their markup at the coefficient K (Dr 378 / Cr 607), so that 607 holds the cost of the goods sold. K = (opening markup + markups entered) ÷ (opening goods + goods entered − the VAT included in both), cumulated from the financial year's start (`ro.stock.retail.coefficient` `year`, pct. 286 alin. (4) and note *2), applied to the year's exits without VAT to the month's end less the markup the year's earlier months discharged, as `production.differences` applies its coefficient: 378 keeps K × the stock without VAT (alin. (7)), and a month may give markup back (a red storno); the month's own coefficient on the month's exits is the review spec's alternative. A year without exits needs no coefficient (what earlier months took is given back, if anything). A discharge that no longer covers the month's exits is stale (`RETAIL_DISCHARGE_STALE`); none is `RETAIL_DISCHARGE_MISSING` (both block the close).
  - **The count at sale price** (`retail.count {date, warehouseId, evidenceId, rows: [{rate, counted}], cause}`): a shortage leaves at sale price (Cr 371 / Dr 607, its VAT and markup with the month's discharge); unimputable, its deducted VAT is adjusted (Dr 635 / Cr 4426, ADJ-304, D300 rd. 34) on its cost, the sale price less the VAT included and the markup at K (art. 304, norme pct. 78), acknowledged non-deductible (`PROFIT_SHORTAGE`); a calamity, a theft shown, degraded goods destroyed or a perishable loss within the limits keep the VAT, with their proof (`STOCK_CAUSE_PROOF`, `STOCK_LOSS_LIMITS`). A surplus enters at sale price, its VAT included and markup at K, the cost against 607. Without a cause the shortage blocks (`RETAIL_CAUSE`); an imputation is not among the causes (not built). The financial year's last month needs a count of each warehouse holding goods (`RETAIL_COUNT_MISSING`, block; OMF nr. 2.861/2009 pct. 1–2, 4).
  - **Reads:** `retail.position {warehouseId, date}` (the goods by rate, the VAT included, the markup and the cost they imply). The year-end inventory and the stock register leave the retail accounts to these treatments.
- **Fiscal cash registers (AMEF), tracked, not integrated:** `retail.registers.record` (owners only: the register's unique number, fiscal series, location, warehouse, the day it entered use, the fiscalization record) and `retail.registers.event` (ANAF's registration, the service contract, the fiscal memory or journal replaced, a failure and its repair, the register closed), append-only (`acc_cash_registers`, `acc_cash_register_events`; `inv:cash_register_identity`, `inv:cash_register_event`: events numbered without gaps, from the register's first day, nothing after its closing). The worklist lists a register whose ANAF registration (art. 3^1) or service contract is not recorded (`retail.register`, warn, external obligations). The engine never talks to a register or to ANAF's register service.
- **Returns:** the Z reports' tax entries are supplies at their rates: D300 rd. 9–10 (with the invoices); D394 op2 I1 by month (the registers, the receipts, the total, each rate's base and VAT, the receipts for which invoices were issued taken out), rezumat2's `baza_incasari_i1` and `tva_incasari_i1`, and `informatii`'s `nr_BF_i1`, `incasari_i1`, both passing DUKIntegrator; D406's General Ledger with the codes 3803xx of art. 319 alin. (10) by rate (`d406ZReportCode`).
- **Closure:** a retail company (`activity.goods` with `policy.stock.retail`) is covered once its policies are recorded, and the cost method's for each warehouse it keeps at cost by its own fact (asked once, [above](#the-stock-register-g1)); the retail method under the intermittent inventory, the company's or a warehouse's, is UNSUPPORTED (`RETAIL_INTERMITTENT`).
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/retail/agent.ts`): eight document types (the Z report, the register's article report, the acquirer's statement, the NIR at sale price, the price change, the inventory list at sale price, the register's records, the retail policy), the question kinds `retail.goods` and `profile.fact`, the detectors (`close.finding`, `question.needed`, `document.unproposed` proposing `retail.zReport` for each report, `efactura.due`, `retail.register`), no template fields.
- **Review specs** (`reviews/specs/retail.ts`): the coefficient's period (the year cumulated or the month), the tickets' account (5328 or 461), the receipt invoices' transmission (transmitted or exempt), each with cases the engine computes.
- **Not built:** exempt operations and taxes outside the base on a Z report; VAT on collection for receipts; payment methods other than cash, card and tickets; a Z report split over several accounts at one rate in D406 (`D406_SPLIT`); D406 for invoices issued for receipts; an invoice for an earlier month's receipt; imputations, returns from customers and supplier credit notes at retail price; transfers between warehouses at retail price; the register's signature; op2 I2 (activities exempt from registers); integration with the registers or ANAF's AMEF service. HG nr. 479/2003 (the norms of OUG nr. 28/1999), the OUG's text after 2018, OPANAF nr. 4.156/2017 (the register's connection) and the CIUS-RO schematron (mfinante.gov.ro refused the connection) are to be downloaded by hand.

### Production (G4): bills of materials, work orders, work in progress, the standard and the actual cost

A manufacturer's products made on work orders (the job-order method), costed from what each order consumed, its share of the month's direct labour and production overheads, and kept in the stock register beside the materials and goods. Core `src/ro/production` (`parameters.ts`, `costing.ts`, `treatments.ts`, `questions.ts`, `agent.ts`), sources `src/ro/sources/production.ts`; engine `modules/production.ts`, with `kernel/state.ts` (the `production` read), `modules/stock.ts`, `modules/d406-stock.ts`, `modules/determinations.ts`. Source: OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 as the legislative portal consolidates it (the owner's copy, to 1 February 2022; SHA-256 070c826a…), which carries the 2015 amendment of the functions of 345, 347, 348 and 711 (OMFP nr. 773/2015 art. 9: every price difference of a product is booked against 711, none against 345, which the 2014 text still shows): pct. 8 pct. 7, 79, 286 alin. (1)–(8), 288, 330 alin. (2), 435 alin. (1) lit. b), 593 alin. (4) and the functions of 331–332, 341–346, 348 and 711–712. Three parameters, `extracted`, each with a review spec (`reviews/specs/production.ts`): `ro.production.accounts`, `ro.production.costing`, `ro.production.coefficient`.

- **The policy** (`policy.production.cost`, a dated accounting-policy fact): `standard` (pct. 286 alin. (1)–(2): products at the standard cost, their differences from the production cost on 348) or `actual` (the production cost of pct. 8 pct. 7). Without it every production treatment asks (`POLICY_MISSING`, `profile.fact`); the stock policies (`policy.stock.*`) value the products and materials as they do goods. The intermittent inventory is not built for products (the treatments refuse, `STOCK_INTERMITTENT`; the closure says `PRODUCTION_INTERMITTENT`): the functions of 345 and 711 describe the perpetual one, and only the work in progress is set by a count.
- **Bills of materials** (`production.boms.record`, append-only `acc_boms` and `acc_bom_lines`): a version per product (an item on 341 or 345), numbered from 1 and dated, each valid after the one before: the materials (items on 301, 302x, 303, 381) and their quantities for an output quantity, the labour and machine hours, and the standard cost of the output (the normal levels of pct. 286 alin. (2), with the technical sheet or the calculation as evidence). A version takes no line once an order is costed on it (`inv:bom_sealed`); a revision is the next version. `production.boms.list`.
- **Work orders** (`production.orders.open`, `.close`, `.list`; `acc_work_orders`, `acc_work_order_events`): the product, the BOM version valid on the opening day (`inv:work_order_bom`), the quantity launched and the launching document; the close is final and dated no earlier than the order's last consumption or hand-over (`ORDER_LATER`), nothing after it (`PRODUCTION_ORDER_CLOSED`). Every production posting tags its lines with the order (`production:order:<id>`): the cost sheet is kept outside the ledger, as class 9 is optional (pct. 593 alin. (4)), and read with `production.costSheet {orderId}` month by month.
- **The catalogue:** products (341, 345, 346) and work in progress (331) are items of the stock register too (`stock.items.create`), by quantity and value in warehouses, like goods. They enter only from production; `stock.receive`, `stock.consume` and purchase invoices refuse them (`STOCK_KIND_UNSUPPORTED`). Sales, customer returns (pct. 330 alin. (2): 7015, 711, 345), transfers, the takeover of an opening balance (`stock.opening`, products only) and the counts take them, each exit onto 711 (`ro.stock.accounts` now names 711 as the expense of 331, 341, 345 and 346, and 393, 3941, 3945, 3946 as their allowances).
- **Treatments:**
  - **Materials given to an order** (`production.consume {orderId, date, warehouseId, reference, evidenceId, lines}`, the bon de consum): out of the warehouse at the stock policy's value onto each stock account's expense (601, 602x, 603, 608), both sides tagged with the order. A material its BOM does not name is acknowledged (`PRODUCTION_OFF_BOM`); goods and semi-finished products are refused (`PRODUCTION_MATERIAL_ACCOUNT`: semi-finished products consumed in the unit pass through 301, 302 first, the function of 341, not built).
  - **Products handed over** (`production.output {orderId, date, warehouseId, reference, evidenceId, lines}`, the bon de predare): the order's product at its registration price, the standard cost of the order's BOM version (Dr 345 or 341 / Cr 711), into the warehouse's FIFO lot or pool; a residual product (346) at the net realizable value the commission sets, which the costing deducts from the order's cost (pct. 8 pct. 7: secondary products). Another product is refused (`PRODUCTION_JOINT_PRODUCT`: joint products allocated by relative sales value are not built); a BOM without a standard cost (`PRODUCTION_STANDARD_MISSING`); under the actual cost the order's own product (`PRODUCTION_OUTPUT_ACTUAL`: it enters with the costing of the month the order closes).
  - **The month's costing** (`production.cost {month, labour, overheads, machineHours?, wip?, outputs?, abandoned?}`, on the month's last day; subject `production:cost:<month>`): each order active in the month gathers its tagged materials and its share of:
    - **direct labour:** `hours`, by the timesheet's hours of each contract on each order at the contract's cost in the month's payroll run (its salary on 641 and, in the reading, the employer's contributions: CAM on 646 and those borne in the employee's name); the hours on no order stay an expense. The run must be the month's, valid and posted (`PRODUCTION_PAYROLL_RUN`, `PRODUCTION_PAYROLL_UNPOSTED`). Or `stated`, a key in percent of the month's 641 and 646 a person states; or `none`, acknowledged when salaries were booked (`PRODUCTION_LABOUR_NONE`);
    - **production overheads:** pools of expense accounts (groups 60–64 and 6811, without 607, 609 and the labour accounts, `PRODUCTION_OVERHEAD_ACCOUNT`; no account in two pools, none holding the month's direct materials), each allocated on a key (machine hours from the machines' log, labour hours from the timesheet, the labour cost or the materials). Variable overheads are allocated in full; fixed ones at the rate of the normal capacity (pct. 8 pct. 7, pct. 79 alin. (2)): a month below it absorbs `pool × actual ÷ normal` and the rest stays an expense of the period, a month above it absorbs the pool, no more, so products are not valued above their cost; the actual level stands for the normal one only when a person states it approximates it (`approximates`). A fixed pool needs its capacity (`PRODUCTION_CAPACITY`) on an hours key (`PRODUCTION_CAPACITY_KEY`). Administrative and selling costs are what no pool names;
    - **abnormal waste:** a closed order's consumption of each material above its BOM's norm for the products it delivered over its life is asked of a person (`production.excess`, `PRODUCTION_EXCESS`): an abnormal loss stays an expense (pct. 79), a normal one is part of the cost. The engine computes the excess and never decides it.
  - It books: for an order open at the month's end, its cost less its products at the registration price (`ro.production.costing` `accumulated`, the reading under review; pct. 288), counted in the work-in-progress list (`wip.orders`: the order, a 331 item, the warehouse, the units and their completion, with the count's evidence; `PRODUCTION_WIP_UNLISTED` without it, `PRODUCTION_WIP_NEGATIVE` when the products exceed the cost): Dr 331 / Cr 711, into the register. For an order closed in the month at the standard cost, the difference between its cost and its products at standard: Dr 348 / Cr 711 when unfavourable, Dr 711 / Cr 348 when favourable (the amended function of 348); at the actual cost, its products at that cost, the quantities of `outputs` (Dr 345 / Cr 711, split by quantity), partial deliveries of an open order refused (`PRODUCTION_OUTPUT_OPEN`). An order closed without products is abandoned: its costs stay expenses (`PRODUCTION_ABANDONED`, acknowledged); at the actual cost only once a person states it (`abandoned`; `PRODUCTION_OUTPUTS_MISSING` otherwise), its products being the costing's own input. The labour and the overheads stay on their expense accounts: 711, the variation of stocks (pct. 435 alin. (1) lit. b)), carries the production's cost. The explanation is the month's cost sheet: each order's opening, materials, labour, fixed and variable overheads, residual, abnormal, cost, products, work in progress or difference; the labour and overheads left unallocated. A month with nothing to book posts a memo entry with the cost sheet.
  - **The work in progress taken back** (`production.wipReverse {month}`, on the month's first day; the function of 331: "scăderea din gestiune … la începutul perioadei următoare"): every 331 position from before the month, Dr 711 / Cr 331. The costing refuses a month whose opening work in progress was not taken back (`PRODUCTION_WIP_REVERSAL`); the next month's costing carries each order's cost from the work in progress booked on its order at the previous month's end.
  - **The price differences on the exits** (`production.differences {month}`, at the standard cost, after the costing): for each product account (341, 345), the coefficient of pct. 286 alin. (4) on the exits at registration price (sold, found missing; the credits of the account and its entries the debits, a reversal on the side of what it reverses, so a sale reversed in black nets out): `ro.production.coefficient` `year` (the reading under review: the differences at the year's start with those of the year's entries, over the stock at the year's start with the year's entries, applied to the year's exits less what the year's earlier months took, so the differences follow the goods exactly) or `month`. Unfavourable Dr 711 / Cr 348, favourable Dr 348 / Cr 711, tagged by account; what stays on 348 is the stock's (alin. (6)). Under the actual cost, `PRODUCTION_NOT_STANDARD`.
- **Sales** (`sales.issue` or `invoices.recognize` with `stock`, G1's path): products leave at their registration price, Cr 345 / Dr 711 (the function of 711: "prețul de înregistrare al produselor vândute"), their differences following at the month's end; the revenue on 701x. A customer's return comes back at what the sale took them out at, red storno (pct. 330 alin. (2)).
- **The month close** (`production.register`): `PRODUCTION_WIP_REVERSAL_MISSING` (331 held on the first day from before the month, with work in progress booked on an order at the previous month's end, and no `production.wipReverse`), `PRODUCTION_COST_MISSING` (production postings of the month, or work in progress carried into it, without a costing that stands), `PRODUCTION_DIFFERENCES_MISSING` (products left 341 or 345, net of their reversals, while 348 holds lines, without the month's spread): each blocks, and the worklist proposes the treatment. The register's `STOCK_LEDGER_DIFFERS` covers products and work in progress like any stock.
- **The year's end:** products and work in progress are counted with the rest by the inventory's lists (OMF nr. 2.861/2009; the work in progress items and their units, as `wip.orders` counted them on the year's last day) and compared with the register (`inventory.compare`); `inventory.stock` books their differences onto 711 (the functions of 345 and 711: "lipsurile la inventar (711)"; a shortage of a product the company made adjusts the VAT deducted on its inputs on its cost at its sale category's rate, acknowledged `PRODUCT_SHORTAGE_VAT`), and `impairment.stock` writes them down to their net realizable value on 393 and 3941–3946 (pct. 292).
- **D406** (the on-demand stock file): materials given to an order are movements of type 70, products handed over and the work in progress the costing counts of type 20 (Producție), its reversal 180, products sold 30; ProductType `Produse finite`, `Semifabricate`, `Produse reziduale`, `Productie in curs` (ANAF's validator takes 18 characters). The GL accounts 331, 345, 348 and 711 are the chart's. It passes ANAF's SAF-T validator.
- **Closure:** a company whose books show production postings (`productionMonths`) needs `policy.production.cost` (`FACT_UNKNOWN`), is UNSUPPORTED under the intermittent inventory (`PRODUCTION_INTERMITTENT`), and is otherwise covered; the products' price differences (348, tagged) no longer count as stock outside the register (`GOODS_STOCK`).
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/production/agent.ts`): ten document types (the policy, the technical sheet and standard cost, the work order, the consumption slip, the hand-over slip, the timesheet, the machines' log, the normal capacity, the work in progress count, the loss record), the question kinds `production.excess` (subject `workOrder`) and `profile.fact`, the detectors (`close.finding` with the three findings, `question.needed`, `yearend.review`), no template fields.
- **Review specs** (`reviews/specs/production.ts`): the cost of direct labour (with the employer's contributions, or the salary alone), the work in progress of a job order (its accumulated cost less its products, or the units counted times their completion at the standard cost), the coefficient's period (the year cumulated or the month), each with cases the engine computes.
- **Not built** (refused or UNSUPPORTED, never approximated): the intermittent inventory for products; services in progress (332, 712) and agricultural products (347), whose items the catalogue refuses (`UNSUPPORTED`), and biological assets; semi-finished products consumed into another order (multi-phase production through 301, 302), and materials returned from an order; joint products; partial deliveries under the actual cost; indirect labour as a fixed overhead (only through the stated key); borrowing costs of long-cycle assets (pct. 80); the standard cost's coefficient by group or category of stock (pct. 286 alin. (5)); a change of cost method within the year; work in progress taken over without its orders (a company starting the engine with production in progress); class 9 cost accounting.

### VAT on collection (TVA la încasare)

The company's regime is the profile axis `vat.cash`. The ceiling is the cited parameter `ro.vat.cash.ceiling`: 5,000,000 lei from 1 March to 31 December 2026, 5,500,000 lei from 2027; earlier dates are a rule gap.
- **Deferral at recognition** (Codul fiscal art. 282 alin. (3)–(7), art. 297 alin. (2)–(3)):
  - The VAT of an invoice under the system goes on 4428, with tax entries that are not yet chargeable (no exigibility), and are marked `cashVat` (`own`, `supplier`, `own+supplier`).
  - For the company's own regime, the engine reads `vat.cash` on the earlier of the invoice's issue date and tax point.
  - **Sales:** a sale to an affiliated buyer (input `affiliated`) stays under the general rules, as alin. (6) says. The invoicer writes the mention "TVA la încasare" (BT-22) on invoices under the system.
  - **Purchases:** the deduction waits for payment if the company is in the system, the invoice carries the mention, or the supplier's ANAF snapshot shows it in the register. Only the deductible VAT waits.
  - Foreign currency and advances under the system are refused.
- **Release at settlement:**
  - `bank.settle` and `cash.record` read each settled invoice's history.
  - They release, on the statement or receipt date and in that date's period, the share of VAT now collected (Dr 4428 / Cr 4427) or paid (Dr 4426 / Cr 4428).
  - The release is rounded cumulatively, so a full settlement releases exactly what is left.
  - **Credit notes** (alin. (10)) first reduce what is deferred, and the excess reduces chargeable VAT at the note's date.
  - An offset (`items.offset`) collects and pays, and releases the VAT the same way. Settling a deferred invoice through the general journal (cession, payment in kind) is refused.
- **Returns:**
  - **D300:** releases go to rows 9–10 and 24–25. The informative fields A, A1, B and B1 hold what is still deferred.
  - **D394:** invoices under the system are listed in full in their period (L, A, AI), with the VAT collected and deducted from the releases.
  - **D406:** codes 310350/310354 for sales, 301305/301306, 341306 and 351305/351306 for purchases, and releases as 380106/380107 on the bank or cash line. All three pass ANAF's validators.
- **Month close:** warnings for deferred VAT on settled invoices (`CASH_VAT_UNRELEASED`), the ceiling exceeded (`CASH_VAT_CEILING`: the bases of the year's chargeable supplies, never the VAT the company self-assesses as a buyer, `RC-…`, `AIC-…`, `IMP-DEF-…`, `TI-A-…`), and leaving the system (`CASH_VAT_EXIT`).
- **Not covered:**
  - eligibility (alin. (3^1)–(5)) is checked by the yearly determination of `vat.cash`, not by the treatments;
  - cession and payment in kind are not treatments yet;
  - one 4428 account holds both sales and purchase deferrals;
  - e-TVA's comparison will differ for a company in the system.

### `deferrals.defer` and `deferrals.release`: amounts for later months (471, 472)

- **`deferrals.defer`** moves an expense (6xx) or revenue (70x) already booked, such as a yearly subscription or insurance, to 471 or 472.
  - The amount becomes an open item of the partner, with a schedule of equal monthly releases over `months` from the month `from`; the remainder goes to the first months.
  - Its evidence shows the period covered. Releases start in the deferral's month or later.
  - When the documents don't say which months the amount covers, the input leaves out `from` and `months`: the proposal is blocked with `DEFERRAL_SCHEDULE`, whose `details.question` asks a person `deferral.schedule` about `deferral` `<evidenceId>:<account>`. It is revised with the answer's months and the question as its basis.
  - Lines: Dr 471 / Cr expense, or Dr revenue / Cr 472.
- **`deferrals.release`** books a month's share on its last day, back to the account the amount came from.
  - It releases what each open deferral's schedule has due by then, less what was already released (the schedule's total less the item's balance). A missed month is caught up, and nothing is released twice.
  - With nothing due, it is refused (`NOTHING_TO_RELEASE`). A month with a release due does not close.
- Schedules are read with their open items (an open-item query with `schedules`). They never change, so the item's version covers them.

### `items.offset`: offsetting a partner's debts (compensare)

- What a partner owes the company and what the company owes it cancel each other for the same amount, in lei, on the date of the offset document, with no money moving.
- Inputs: debit items (receivables, advances paid, debtors) against credit items (payables, advances received, creditors), all of one partner.
- An invoice under VAT on collection counts as collected or paid for the amount offset, and releases its VAT (norme pct. 26 alin. (10)).
- Not covered yet: offsets between more than two parties, and foreign-currency items.

### `accruals.record` and `accruals.settle`: invoices not yet received or issued (408, 418)

- **`accruals.record`** books a service in its month before its invoice: received from a supplier (Dr expense / Cr 408) or rendered to a customer (Dr 418 / Cr revenue).
  - The amount is net of VAT and is an open item of the partner, justified by a contract, report or timesheet.
  - The accrual keeps the account it was booked on as a one-row schedule.
- **`accruals.settle`** reverses the accrual on the invoice's day, once the invoice is booked. The invoice must be the same partner's, and the open balance is reversed in full. The difference between the invoice and the estimate falls in the invoice's month.
- **VAT:** it comes from the invoice, at the invoice's tax point. Putting VAT not yet chargeable on 4428 at the accrual is a policy the engine doesn't apply. Whether the reversal method suits the company is for the expert accountant to confirm.

### Loans and leases: `loans.*`, `leases.commence`

- **Accounts, as OMFP 1802/2014 places them:**
  - bank credits on 1621 (long term) or 5191 (short term);
  - loans from affiliated or associated companies on 1661/1663;
  - other lenders and lease liabilities on 167;
  - associates on 4551.

  The loan accounts are controlled as `loan.payable` and their interest accounts (1682, 1685–1687, 5198, 4558) as `loan.interest`, for companies created from now on.
- **A loan is an open item of the lender in its own currency, with one schedule:**
  - principal rows on the loan account, adding up to the amount borrowed;
  - interest rows on the interest account, or on 666 for a lease.
- **Treatments:**
  - **`loans.receive`** explains the bank movement that brings the loan in. The schedule is given as rows or as terms (annuity or linear, 30/360).
  - **`loans.schedule`** attaches or revises a schedule on an existing loan, for example an associate's 4551 money or an opening balance. Rows already due stay unchanged, and so does the principal.
  - **`loans.accrue`** books month-end interest (Dr 666 / Cr interest account). Each period's interest is spread over its days. The accrual is cumulative, so it never books twice and catches up a missed month.
  - **`loans.withhold`** withholds the tax on interest paid to an individual (Codul fiscal art. 97 alin. (2), 10%, parameter `ro.interest.tax_rate`): Dr interest / Cr 446. It is declared in D100 (code 605) and D205 (tip 09).
  - **Repayments** go through `bank.settle`.
- **Finance lease (lessee):**
  - **`leases.commence`** brings a registered asset in against 167 at the rate of the financing date (pct. 321). The contract is either settled in lei at the euro rate, or in its currency.
  - **Instalments** are the lessor's invoices, recognized with an allocation on 167 (`lease`, `principal`, `vatExpense`).
    - The lessor is held on 404, and 167 is released at its carrying amount.
    - The exchange difference goes to 668/768 (lei-indexed) or 6651/7651.
    - VAT comes from each invoice, a service taxed at each payment date (art. 271 alin. (3) lit. a), art. 281 alin. (8)). The 50% vehicle limit and VAT on collection apply as usual.
    - **D406:** one VAT rate spans the principal (167) and the interest (666). The principal's line is the base of its part, and the lines it brings (the VAT it does not deduct on its `vatExpense`, the exchange difference on 668/768 or 6651/7651) carry no VAT. The principal leaves 167 at its carrying amount, not at the invoice's rate, so its line is written in lei. Each invoice line goes to the part whose amount it makes up, the only grouping that adds up (otherwise `D406_SPLIT`), as a service (art. 271 alin. (3) lit. a)). Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator.
- **Operating lease:** the lessor's invoices on 612, through `invoices.recognize`.
- **`fx.revalue`** revalues foreign-currency loans and their interest.
- **`loans.list`** shows each loan with its balance, schedule and the part due within 12 months. The reclassification of pct. 360 is presentation only, so it's not booked.
- **Month close:**
  - interest not accrued for an open loan blocks (`LOAN_INTEREST_DUE`; not on single-entry books);
  - a lease instalment due (`LEASE_INSTALMENT_DUE`) or an overdue loan (`LOAN_OVERDUE`) is a warning.
- **Not covered yet:**
  - withholding on interest in a currency (a non-resident lender's in lei is `nonresidents.withhold`'s);
  - revising a lease's schedule;
  - revolving credit lines and overdrafts;
  - the off-balance records of future interest and of operating leases.

### `fx.revalue`: month-end revaluation

- **Input:** a month-end date and a round number (a later round re-revalues after further entries).
- Revalues monetary items (receivables, payables, accruals, unapplied cash, loans, a credit note's refund in a currency on 462 or 461, and taxes owed or claimed in a currency: the Union scheme's VAT owed in euro on 4423, Codul fiscal art. 315 alin. (12)–(13), a liability of a fixed or determinable amount of euro, OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 315, 325) and foreign bank or cash balances at the selected rate, each item at its balance on the date (version 2, Y1: a revaluation posted after later entries revalues the date's balance). Each difference is booked on the item or account with a zero currency amount, against 7651 or 6651.
- Advances and deferrals are not monetary and are not revalued.

## Documents from PDFs and photos

An agent reads a PDF or a photo (`evidence.read`: text by line, or the image for its own vision model) and records what it read with `documents.record`: an invoice or credit note in the UBL parser's shape (`extracted-invoice-1`), a fiscal receipt (`extracted-receipt-1`), a contract (`extracted-contract-1`), or ANAF's decisions and statement of the account (`extracted-tax-decision-1`, `extracted-tax-statement-1`, [The tax account](#the-tax-account-t2)), each field citing `{ page, line, text }` of the file's text layer. The body and the checks are pure (`xfactura-core/src/documents`); the wire contract is in [AGENT_API.md](AGENT_API.md#documents-from-pdfs-and-photos).

- **Authority.** Every document records where its content comes from: `efactura` (the SPV's XML), `issued` (`sales.issue`), `ubl_upload`, `invoicer`, or `extracted`. Extracted documents keep their file and part in their source key (`extract:<file sha256>:<part>`), so they never take the place of a structured source: the same invoice from e-Factura is a document of its own.
- **Checks**, kept with the document (`checks_json`) and its citations with their outcomes (`citations_json`):
  - refused outright: a document the company is not exactly one party of, on the side its kind says (`COUNTERPARTY`); a body that does not parse. Recording a part of a file again with other content is a conflict (`DOCUMENT_CONFLICT`).
  - blocking a treatment that reads it (`SOURCE_INCONSISTENT`): `TOTALS` (lines to net, net and VAT to gross, gross to payable, the VAT breakdown to the totals) and `VAT_CATEGORIES` (each category's VAT is its base at its rate, a receipt's its gross at its rate, within half a ban per line and at least one ban). An extraction whose amounts do not add up must be corrected, not acknowledged, as the treatment books from them.
  - to acknowledge (`SOURCE_EXTRACTED`, naming the failed and unverified ones): `VAT_RATE` (a standard-rated category at a rate in force on the tax point; unverified on a rule gap), `TAX_IDS` (control digits), `IBAN`, `DATES` (not in the future or older than ten years, the tax point within a year of issue, the due date not before it) and `CITATIONS` (each citation's line exists, carries the cited words and shows the field's value; without a text layer, unverified, never passed; the key fields must be cited).
  - Every proposal reading an extracted document carries `SOURCE_EXTRACTED`, even when every check passed: a person confirms what a model read. A posting policy posts it only when it acknowledges the code.
- **Receipts** are booked with `receipts.recognize`, with the VAT they deduct as simplified invoices ([above](#receiptsrecognize-a-fiscal-receipt-v3)).

### Copies and groups

- **Identity.** A document's `identity_key` hashes its supplier's tax id (without RO), its number normalized (compatibility forms folded, no spaces, capitals), its issue date and its type; for a number already in that form it is the hash `recordInvoice` has always deduplicated e-Factura on. A receipt is identified by seller, register and number, only when the register is known (a chain's stores share a CUI and restart their numbers); a contract by the other party, number and date, when numbered.
- **Groups.** A document recorded with the identity of an earlier one joins that one's group by a `same_as` link to the group's root, the earliest recorded. Near copies whose identities differ (same partner and gross amount, dates at most 3 days apart: a date or number read differently) are joined only by a person, `documents.link` (`same_as_confirmed`), and only while the joining document has no proposal.
- **Booked once.** `Proposals.propose` takes the group's root as the subject of any `document:<id>` proposal, so the same invoice cannot be proposed twice from two copies (`SUBJECT_EXISTS`); a revision may move between copies, keeping the subject.
- **The better source.** A proposal reading a copy other than an e-Factura one, in a group that holds an e-Factura copy, carries `BETTER_SOURCE` (to acknowledge), naming it: revise onto it. Groups and supersessions are in the read set (`documentSource`), so a copy arriving makes an open proposal stale.
- **Corrections.** `documents.supersede` records a corrected extraction of an extracted document that no posted entry recognizes (directly or through its group's subject) and links it (`supersedes`); a proposal reading the superseded one is blocked (`DOCUMENT_SUPERSEDED`). After posting, the correction is a reversal and a new proposal.
- **Database guards:**
  - `document_authority`: every new document records its authority;
  - `extracted_source`: an extracted document has its evidence and an `extract:<that file's hash>:<part>` source key, which no other document uses;
  - `same_as_root`: a document joins a group only while in none and without members, only through the root, and a `same_as` link only into a group holding its identity;
  - `supersede`: only an extracted document is superseded, by an extracted one of its kind, once, while no posted entry recognizes it or its group;
  - `identity_taken`: no proposal names a document while another document of its identity or group has an open or posted proposal.
- **Migration.** Existing documents gained their authority from their origin. An e-Factura document's identity was derived from its `invoice:` source key when its number is already in normal form; issued and invoicer documents, and numbers needing normalization, have no identity and join no group.

## Issuing invoices

The company's own invoices are issued through the books, so the number, the XML and the recognition come from one command.

- **Details.** The company's invoicing details (`sales.seller.record`, owners only) must carry its CUI; a partner's details (`parties.recordDetails`) must match the identifier it was created with. Both are dated versions, and each issued invoice records the versions it used.
- **Series.** `sales.series.create` fixes a code, a prefix, a counter width, the first number, the document types (380, 381) and the default revenue account. Numbers are the prefix and the padded counter, for example `XF0001`.
- **Issue.** `sales.issue` is human-only. It takes the next number at the moment of issue, writes the e-Factura with the core writer, keeps the XML as evidence and the document as an issued sale, then proposes its recognition (`invoices.recognize`, or `invoices.credit` for a credit note). A posting policy can post that recognition at once. A refusal after issue leaves the invoice issued and reports why.
- **Refusals.** `sales.preview` shows the number and every problem without issuing. An issue is refused, with no number used, when:
  - a party's details are missing;
  - the buyer is an individual and how they identified is not stated or not recorded (`B2C_IDENTIFICATION`, `B2C_CNP`; G2, [above](#retail-and-b2c-g2-z-reports-the-retail-price-method-b2c-e-factura): the CNP sealed, or 13 zeros), or is abroad (`B2C_COUNTRY`; a consumer of another Member State's invoice, issued outside the engine, is booked with `invoices.euConsumer`, [V4](#sales-to-consumers-in-other-member-states-and-the-one-stop-shop-v4-invoiceseuconsumer-d398));
  - the company's VAT status on the tax point is unknown or contradicts its details;
  - a standard-rated line is at neither the standard nor the reduced rate the register gives for the tax point, a reduced-rate line has no `reducedRateBasis` (the supply of art. 291 alin. (2) it is, kept with the issue), or the rate is a rule gap (for example after 2026-12-31);
  - the invoice fails the e-Factura checks (Romanian county and Bucharest sector, lei VAT for foreign currency, the corrected invoice of a credit note);
  - a credit note is in another currency than its invoice (`CURRENCY`), or, in a currency, states a VAT in lei other than its VAT at the rate its invoice's base was determined at (`VAT_FX`); without one, the issue computes it ([`invoices.credit`](#invoicescredit-credit-note-against-the-original));
  - the date is earlier than the series' last issue, or the number already appears on a sales document, imported or issued.
- **Database guards.** Numbers in a series are consecutive from the first number, never go back in date, match the document's type, number and date, are never reused across sales documents, and cite recorded party details.

## Importing the invoicer's history

`invoices.importInvoicer` reads the browser invoicer's JSON export (adapter `xfactura-web-export-1`), kept as evidence, and gives every record one outcome:

| Outcome | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `imported` | A sales document now exists, known only from the export. |
| `duplicate` | The same content as an imported record with the same number. |
| `conflict` | Records share a number but differ; none of them is imported. |
| `invalid` | The record cannot be an e-Factura (for example a Bucharest address without its sector). |
| `not_seller` | The company is not the seller. |
| `existing` | An earlier import already holds the record; importing again adds nothing. |

Documents are what the export states, converted as the invoicer now writes them; the export, not regenerated XML, is their evidence. The SPV archives are the authority on what was issued: syncing the inbox confirms or contradicts each document (`spv.messages`). Individuals are recorded by the keyed hash of their CNP, without invoicing details, and the export, which names them by CNP, is `personal` data.

## Payroll

Payroll data is personal: employees' CNPs are encrypted at rest (AES-256-GCM under a key derived from the server secret, bound to the company) and never returned by a view; an employee's party uses the CNP's keyed hash as its identity (an HMAC under a per-company key derived from the server secret, never the CNP or its plain hash), the same as a buyer who is this person. Files that carry payroll or CNPs (a D112, an employment contract a payroll record cites) reach only those who may read those classes ([AGENT_API.md](AGENT_API.md#personal-data)). Payroll operations are not open to agents.

- `payroll.employees.record` records an employee (name, CNP; the birth date comes from the CNP).
- `payroll.contracts.record` records a contract's terms, or a new version of them from a date: base salary, daily hours, main job (funcția de bază), COR code, dependents, children in school, union dues, and the reason the part-time minimum does not apply. `kind: mandate` records an administrator's administration or mandate contract instead of an employment contract, the same in every version ([The administrator's mandate](#the-administrators-mandate-w1)).
- `payroll.runs.prepare` computes a month from the contracts in force and what differs this month (active days, the month's amounts, leave days, the 26th-birthday decision). The month's working days are the weekdays less the public holidays of Codul muncii art. 139 (Easter, Good Friday and Pentecost by the Orthodox calendar), unless given. A contract that starts, ends or changes during the month needs its days and amounts; the run keeps every line with its inputs, the calculation's steps and their clauses, or the problems that stopped it. Computing the month again supersedes the earlier run.

The calculation (`xfactura-core/src/ro/payroll`) follows the Fiscal Code articles it cites, read in ANAF's consolidated text:
- CAS 25% on salary in money, CASS 10% also on meal and holiday vouchers, tax 10% on the net income less the personal deduction and union dues at the main job, CAM 2.25% on salary in money;
- the personal deduction, in whole lei as D112 declares it, by the table of art. 77 (percentages of the minimum wage by 50-lei bands up to 2,000 lei above it and by dependents), 15% of the minimum wage under 26, 100 lei per child in school, limited to the net income and not reduced for part time;
- CAS and CASS at least on the minimum wage less the minimum-wage relief, for the contract's active working days and in whole lei, as ANAF's D112 structure computes the limit (4,325 − 200 lei from July 2026), the difference borne by the employer, except in the cases of art. 146 alin. (5^7);
- the tax base rounded to whole lei with 50 bani and less dropped (the methodological norms), contributions and tax half up (OUG nr. 59/2005 art. 1 alin. (1), read on the legislative portal; the procedure code has no rounding article); a contribution below one leu (CAS, CASS, CAM, the employer's part of the minimum) is one leu, as D112 declares it (its structure, p. 5: „valorile subunitare ale contribuției se întregesc la 1 leu”, `ANAF/D112-0726:rotunjiri`), while the tax keeps the plain rounding.

The minimum wage is 4,050 lei from 2025 (HG 1506/2024 art. 1) and 4,325 lei from July 2026 (HG 146/2026 art. 1). The minimum-wage relief (OUG 156/2024 art. LXVI for 2025, OUG 89/2025 art. III for 2026) leaves 300 lei a month (200 lei from July 2026) out of every base for a full-time contract at the main job whose base salary is the minimum wage, not lowered since the ordinance, with salary in money (vouchers excluded) up to 4,300 lei (4,600 lei from July 2026). For part of a month the ordinance lists what reduces the amount but not how, so such a month is stopped for the expert. The Fiscal Code values apply from 2026-01-01, the snapshot's date; earlier months are a rule gap.

Annual leave: a run entry gives the leave days; without the month's amounts, the run pays the worked days at the base salary and the leave at the daily average of the contract's previous three posted months (salary and leave pay, without bonuses), at least the base salary for the leave days, both in whole lei (Codul muncii art. 150, as consolidated on 27.04.2026). The computation is kept with the line.

Sick leave (OUG 158/2005 as consolidated on 1 July 2026, with OUG 91/2025 art. II as amended by Legea 64/2026, which the consolidation's notes quote; the owner's copy is stored as `OUG158/2005-A`):

- **Input:** a run entry lists the month's medical certificates (series, number, indemnity code, issue date, first and last day, the certificate it continues, hospitalization, where it was prescribed, and the diagnosis code, which is stored sealed like the CNP; for codes 09, 91 and 17 the child's or patient's CNP, sealed the same way; for 06 the emergency's code, for 05 and 51 the infectious disease's). A certificate covers days of one month. The codes computed are those of `ro.sick.codes` ([The other medical-leave codes](#the-other-medical-leave-codes-w1)); any other code stops the line.
- **Base:** the six most recent of the twelve months before the episode, from the posted runs: each month's CAM base capped at 12 minimum wages, over its active days. When the payroll holds fewer than six, the base comes from the employee's stage certificate (`sickBase`). The daily average is truncated to the ban, as the norms' worked example does (the norms, Ordin 15/2018/1.311/2017 as amended in June 2026, were read only in a secondary source).
- **Amount:** the daily average × the certificate's percentage (55% up to 7 days, 65% for 8–14, 75% over 15), for its working days. For certificates issued from 1 February 2026 to 31 December 2027, the episode's first working day is not paid, unless the patient is hospitalized. The employer pays the next five working days and FNUASS the rest. A certificate issued in January 2026 loses no day: the employer pays the first five (art. 12 lit. A; `ro.sick.days` and `ro.sick.codes` have a version for January 2026, the other sick-leave keys hold from 1 January 2026, the consolidation's amendment notes showing no change of the articles read before 1 July 2026, `OUG158/2005-A:modificari-2026`). Certificates issued before 2026 are a rule gap. A continuation keeps the episode's base, loses no further day and carries the employer's days over. Each part is rounded to whole lei.
- **What stops the line:** a 15-day certificate (the law's tiers skip it); a continuation whose episode reaches another percentage than its own certificate (art. 17 alin. (1^2) recalculates the earlier month from July 2026, not built); a continuation that does not follow on; sick amounts also given by hand.
- **Contributions:** both parts are salary income for the tax and in the CAS and CASS bases (code 01); the fund's part is not in the CAM base (Codul fiscal art. 76 alin. (1), 139 alin. (1) lit. o), 157 alin. (1) lit. v), 220^5). Sick pay does not count towards the minimum contribution, which is for the active days.
- **Booking:** the contract is suspended on the certificates' working days. The employer's part is on 641; the fund's part is a claim on FNUASS (Dr 4382), recovered on the employer's request (art. 38), its receipt booked by a journal entry.
- **D112:**
  - An employee with certificates is declared in section B (the days worked and those on leave, the indemnities by payer, the bases) together with section D, one record per certificate. ANAF's validator refuses section D next to section A.
  - The employer's totals are in C1, and the medical leave by percentage and the amount to recover from FNUASS are in C2.
  - Still refused: sick pay entered by hand, a certificate without its place or diagnosis, an employee on the minimum-wage relief, and a part-time employee owing the minimum contribution in a month with sick leave.

CAM is posted on the employer's total base, rounded once, as D112 declares it (one below a leu is a leu).

### Benefits in kind (W1)

A run entry lists the month's benefits in kind (`benefits`), each with the document its value comes from (norme pct. 14 alin. (3)), in the order the employer sets for the 33% cap (art. 76 alin. (4^2)). `evaluateBenefits` (`xfactura-core/src/ro/payroll/benefits.ts`) values them as the methodological norms do (pct. 12 alin. (4)–(9)) and splits what is income from what the law leaves out, by the parameters `ro.payroll.benefit.car` and `ro.payroll.benefit.limits`, read in ANAF's consolidated Codul fiscal:

| Benefit | Income | Left out |
|---|---|---|
| A car used privately (`car`) | 1.7% of its input value a month, or the rent when it is rented (pct. 12 alin. (5) lit. a)), by the private kilometres for mixed use (alin. (6) lit. a)), commuting not counted (alin. (9)) | a passenger car of art. 25 alin. (3) lit. l) not used only for the business: lit. ț) under profit tax, lit. ț^1) at a micro-enterprise (the regime from `income_tax`; unknown, the line stops) |
| Housing (`housing`) | the rent or value above what is left out | 20% of the minimum wage when the employee declared the conditions of art. 76 alin. (4^1) lit. c), within the 33% |
| Gifts (`gift`) | everything at another occasion; above 300 lei per person at Easter, Christmas and similar holidays, 8 March (employees) and 1 June (children) | up to 300 lei per person and occasion (art. 76 alin. (4) lit. a)) |
| Insurance (`insurance`) | other premiums, when paid (alin. (3) lit. g)) | voluntary health insurance and medical subscriptions up to 400 euro a year (alin. (4^1) lit. f)), at the BNR rate of the month's last day (more than 7 days old: the line stops), within the 33%, the year's earlier months read from the posted runs |
| Anything else (`other`) | the value a person states, with its basis (vouchers above the legal limit) | — |
