What is income is in every base: the tax, CAS (art. 139 alin. (1) lit. a)), CASS (art. 157 alin. (1) lit. a), u)) and CAM (art. 220^4 alin. (1) lit. a), n)); what is left out is in none (art. 142 lit. b), s), aa^1), 157 alin. (2), 220^4 alin. (2)). Benefits are not paid in money: the net is the money salary less everything withheld (norme pct. 16 alin. (1)). With the minimum-wage relief a month with benefits stops (`RELIEF_BENEFIT`): OUG nr. 89/2025 art. III does not say whether they count towards the 4,600 lei ceiling. D112 declares the taxable ones in E3_44 (any other) and E3_58 (insurance), within E3_60, and those left out in E3_86 (gifts, within E3_69, A_sal2 = E3_8 − E3_69), E3_78 (housing) and E3_71 (health insurance), within E3_90. Vouchers are checked against Legea nr. 165/2018 (consolidated on 1 December 2025, `ro.payroll.voucher.limits`): a meal voucher of at most 45 lei, one per day worked (art. 12 alin. (2), art. 14; the days worked are the active days less annual leave), and holiday vouchers of at most 6 minimum wages a year (art. 24 alin. (3), counting the year's earlier posted months). What exceeds them is not granted "potrivit legii" (art. 76 alin. (3) lit. h), art. 142 lit. r)): `voucherExcess` turns it into a benefit of kind `other` by itself, in every base and in E3_44, and the vouchers keep only what is within. The 45 lei held through September 2026 (Legea nr. 201/2025 art. II); from October 2026 a joint order may index it (art. 32), which the register does not hold, so 45 lei is only its floor and an excess over it stops the line (`VOUCHER_INDEXATION`). Whether the excess is in the CAS and CAM bases or taxed like the vouchers is the review spec's question.

### The other medical-leave codes (W1)

`ro.sick.codes` says, per indemnity code, who pays, whether it is taxed, whether it is in the CASS base and whether the first day is reduced; `ro.sick.code.percent` the percentage of the codes other than 01, applied to the base of art. 10. Both are read in the consolidated OUG nr. 158/2005 (who bears each indemnity and its percentage; OUG nr. 91/2025 art. II in its notes), Legea nr. 346/2002 for 02–04, the D112 structure (Nomenclatoarele 9–10, D_20 = 0 for the codes FNUASS alone bears, its own list of OUG nr. 91/2025's exceptions) and the Codul fiscal (art. 62 lit. c), art. 155 alin. (1) lit. i)).

| Codes | Payer | Tax | CASS | Day reduced | Percentage |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 01 ordinary illness | employer 5 days, FNUASS after | yes | yes | yes | 55/65/75% by length (art. 17 alin. (1)) |
| 02–04 work accidents, occupational diseases | employer 3 days, the state social insurance budget after (Legea nr. 346/2002 art. 34) | yes | no | no | 80%, 100% for an emergency (art. 33) |
| 13 cardiovascular | employer, FNUASS | yes | no | yes | 75% (art. 17 alin. (1^1)) |
| 05, 06, 12, 14, 16 (infectious group A, emergencies, TB, cancer, burns) | employer, FNUASS | yes | no | yes | 100% (art. 17 alin. (2)) |
| 51 infectious with isolation | FNUASS (art. 12 lit. B lit. c)) | yes | no | no | 100% (art. 20 alin. (3)) |
| 07 quarantine | FNUASS (art. 22 alin. (1)) | yes | yes | yes | 100% (art. 20 alin. (3)) |
| 08 maternity | FNUASS (art. 25 alin. (2)) | no | no | no | 85% (art. 25 alin. (1)) |
| 09, 91 child care | FNUASS (art. 26 alin. (2)) | no | no | yes | 85% (art. 30 alin. (1)) |
| 15 maternal risk | FNUASS (art. 31 alin. (2)) | no | no | no | 75% (art. 31 alin. (4)) |
| 17 care of an oncological patient | FNUASS (art. 30^3 alin. (2)) | no | no | no | 85% (art. 30^3 alin. (1)) |

Isolation (51) keeps its first day as ANAF's D112 structure lists it among the exceptions, although OUG nr. 91/2025 art. II alin. (4)–(5) as stored does not: the `ro.sick.codes` review spec asks which applies. Quarantine on return from a personal trip to an epidemic zone is borne by FNUASS for 5 days only (art. 22 alin. (1^1)), a cause the engine does not know.

A code whose percentage is not in the register stops the line (`SICK_PERCENT`). Codes 10 (the reduced working time's own formula), 11 (not paid by FNUASS) and 92 (100% from FNUASS, art. 30 alin. (2), but its tax and CASS are not in the register) are refused (`SICK_CODE`), saying why.

Work accidents and occupational diseases (02–04, Legea nr. 346/2002 as consolidated on 11.11.2024) are computed once the territorial pension house confirmed them (`accidentConfirmed` on the certificate); an unconfirmed one stops the line (`SICK_ACCIDENT_UNCONFIRMED`): the D112 structure declares it in section D, and art. 33 alin. (4)–(5) recovers the amounts later, without the stored texts saying how it is paid meanwhile. The base is the monthly average of the gross salary income at the employer in the six posted months before (art. 19 alin. (1)–(2), uncapped; without them, `sickBase` gives the average), over the working days of each month of leave (alin. (4)), kept for the episode's continuations; 80% of it, 100% with an emergency code (art. 33 alin. (1), (3), `ro.sick.accident`), no day reduced (OUG nr. 91/2025 art. II is about OUG nr. 158/2005's indemnities). The employer bears the first 3 working days and the state social insurance budget the rest (art. 34), which the employer pays and recovers from the pension house (art. 19 alin. (6)–(7)), booked on 4382 with FNUASS's part. The indemnity is taxed (Codul fiscal art. 76 alin. (1)), outside the CASS base (art. 155 alin. (1) lit. i)) and the CAS base (the budget's part, art. 141 lit. a) and norme pct. 41; the employer's, as D112 computes B4_7); the budget's part is outside CAM's. D112 declares it in B3 (B3_8 its working days, B3_4 and B3_9 the budget's days and amount, B3_10 the employer's, B3_11 their sum, B3_1 = B3_6 + B3_8), C3 rd. 1, C1_7 (to recover from the pension houses) and the salary fund (B_brutSalarii adds C3_13 − C3_14), not in section D; ANAF's validator accepts it. Which six months a continuation in a later month uses is the `ro.sick.accident` review spec's question; severe burns (art. 36^1, 100%) are not recognized. All indemnities are in the CAS base; what FNUASS bears is in no CAM base. The CAS of an untaxed indemnity is not deducted from the taxable income (`cas.taxable`), and the minimum contribution compares only the indemnities that are in its base. D112 declares each certificate in section D (D_28 only for 01, D_20 0 for FNUASS's codes, D_23 RM for maternal risk, D_8/D_8a/D_11/D_12 as the code asks), and the employer's C2 by row: incapacity (1, isolation also 1.1), prevention and quarantine (2, 2.2), maternity (3), child care (4), oncological care (4.1), maternal risk (5), and C2_T6 adds what FNUASS bears. The untaxed indemnities are out of E3_8 and of the unemployment base. Still refused: the minimum-wage relief in a month with sick leave and the part-time minimum contribution with sick leave (the D112 structure computes the limit over the days worked and those of medical leave, B2_5P, and subtracts the indemnities' CAS too, B4_8D, where the payroll follows art. 146 alin. (5^6)'s active days: expert questions).

### Corrections of a posted month (W1)

A posted month is computed again only as a correction (`payroll.runs.prepare` with `correction: { reason, evidenceId }`): `error`, an error found after posting or filing, and `retroactive`, a change of the contract's terms recorded later with effect in the month (`ro.payroll.correction.retroactive`: the month is recomputed and its D112 rectified, an interpretation awaiting review against art. 78 alin. (3)'s taxation at payment). The new run is the next round of the month and corrects the latest posted one; `payroll.recognize` books the difference. Amounts owed for earlier months by law or by a court's decision (art. 146 alin. (9)–(9^1), art. 78 alin. (3)–(4), D112 tip_rec 2) are refused (`CORRECTION_LEGAL`). The month's run is from then on the latest standing round: D112, the sick-leave base, the leave average and the garnishments read it.

The month's D112 is then rectifying (d_rec 1, OPANAF nr. 605/2026 anexa nr. 6 cap. II pct. 2: filled in whole, with every insured person): the option `rectifying` is required for a month with a correction round, derived when the engine recorded the month's D112 as submitted or accepted, and refused `false` then. The worklist lists a month corrected after its D112 was filed (`payroll.due`, subject `D112:<month>`).

### Garnishments (W1)

`payroll.garnishments.record` records an order the employer received (the creditor as a partner, the kind of debt, the amount to recover, the monthly amount or the share of the net pay the order states, the document), `payroll.garnishments.release` its release from a date, `payroll.garnishments.list` what was withheld and what remains. The run takes from each contract's net pay, by `garnish` (`xfactura-core/src/ro/payroll/garnishment.ts`) and `ro.garnishment.limits`:
- in the Labour Code's order (art. 169 alin. (3): maintenance, the state's contributions and taxes, damages to public property, other debts), the orders of one rank as received;
- each what its order states, never more than its debt still owed (from the posted runs);
- maintenance up to half of the net, the other debts together up to a third, all together up to half (Codul de procedură civilă art. 729, as consolidated on 14.12.2025: the same limits as art. 728 of the 2013 text read before; the third as one limit for all the other debts is an interpretation awaiting review), and deductions together never more than half of the net salary (Codul muncii art. 169 alin. (4), as consolidated on 27.04.2026).

Refused: a net below the net minimum wage (art. 729 alin. (3): only the part above half of it may be taken; the code defines neither that minimum nor whether the limits apply to the whole net or to that part, so the month stops, `GARNISHMENT_LOW_INCOME`; the engine's figure is the net of a full-time minimum-wage contract at the main job without dependents), and a month with health-insurance indemnities (alin. (4)–(7), `GARNISHMENT_INDEMNITY`). The withheld amount is owed to the creditor on 427; ANAF's orders are paid within 3 working days (Codul de procedură fiscală art. 236 alin. (9) lit. a)), a due date the engine does not set.

### The administrator's mandate (W1)

A contract of `kind: mandate` is the remuneration of an administrator under an administration or mandate contract, without an employment contract: salary income (art. 76 alin. (2) lit. o)) in the CAS (art. 139 alin. (1) lit. d), whatever else the person is), CASS (art. 157 alin. (1) lit. e)) and CAM (art. 220^4 alin. (1) lit. d)) bases; no minimum wage, no minimum-wage relief and no minimum contribution (art. 146 alin. (5^6) is for employment contracts). The tax is under art. 78 alin. (2) lit. b) (10% of the gross less the contributions), with the personal deduction only when the person declares the main job there (`mainJob`; `ro.payroll.mandate.deduction`, norme pct. 14 alin. (9), an interpretation awaiting review). Annual and sick leave are refused for a mandate (`MANDATE_LEAVE`). D112 declares the person as insured type 6 (Nomenclator 5), in section A, insured for medical leave and not for unemployment, without hours worked, E1 or E2 by `mainJob`. A mandate is not registered in REGES-ONLINE.

The obligation closure counts a mandate's months as payroll months, so payroll taxes in them no longer raise `PAYROLL_WITHOUT_EMPLOYEES`. The micro-enterprise's employee condition counts employment contracts only (a mandate is never a full-time employee); a mandate paid at least the minimum wage meets it through art. 51 alin. (4) lit. b), which the owner states in `micro.conditions` (`mandate`), as before: the determinations module's reading is consistent with the text, and the books do not override the answer.

### REGES-ONLINE, an external obligation (W1)

The register of employees is REGES-ONLINE (HG nr. 295/2025 as consolidated with OUG nr. 46/2025, which moved Revisal's end to 31 December 2025; OMMFTSS nr. 1107/2025). The engine never transmits to it. `payroll.reges.list` lists, per employment contract, what the register needs, with its deadline (`regesDeadline`, HG nr. 295/2025 art. 5):
- the contract by the day before its start (art. 5 alin. (1) lit. a), Codul muncii art. 16 alin. (2), `ro.deadline.reges.start`);
- a change of the occupation, the working time or the end date of a fixed-term contract by the day before it takes effect (alin. (3)), of the salary within 20 working days of it (alin. (4)), the earliest when several change together (`ro.deadline.reges.change`);
- the end at the latest on its date (alin. (1) lit. f), `ro.deadline.reges.end`); an end date given to an open-ended contract is its end, not a change of its duration;
- a changed start date is a correction (alin. (8)), due when the employer learns of the error: no deadline, it blocks.

A change ordered by a court has 10 working days from when the employer learned of it, which the engine does not know; whether the salary's 20 working days run from the date the new salary applies or from the addendum is the review spec's question. Suspensions, transfers and detachments are not tracked. `payroll.reges.record` records a transmission with its receipt; the worklist lists each one not recorded (`payroll.due`), blocking once late or without a deadline. Events before the register replaced Revisal (`ro.deadline.reges.start`'s `since`, 1 January 2026) are not listed: past the rules' review horizon (a new year before its rollover) `since` is read in the latest month the register reviews, since that day is past, so a contract begun in 2025 stays off the worklist in January 2027 as in 2026.

## Declarations

Tax returns are artifacts of the books:

1. `declarations.prepare` reads what the return is computed from (for example the VAT tax period) and writes its XML in ANAF's format for the reporting period. A period with no pinned format is a `FORMAT_GAP`. The XML is kept as evidence with the hash of the inputs read.
2. ANAF's own validator (DUKIntegrator with the declaration's package, pinned by `bun run validators:fetch`) runs on the exact XML: `validated_official`, or `validation_failed` with ANAF's messages. Each call starts DUKIntegrator with a config folder of its own (`-c`, `adapters/declaration-validator.ts`): the kit's config.properties with `maxWaitBeforeExitMillis=0`, in the temporary directory, so the JVM halts as soon as its result is written instead of a second later; the kits (`ACCOUNTING_ANAF_VALIDATOR_DIR`, `ACCOUNTING_ANAF_SAFT_VALIDATOR_DIR`) are only read and may be mounted read-only.
3. `declarations.authorize.request` is a reviewed action on the exact XML. Its preview recomputes the inputs; if the books changed since preparation, or the return is not validated, it blocks. A kind may add what authorizing it needs (`authorization`): the annual statements' signature roles and accompanying documents. Executing it locks the tax period: from then on, an entry adding tax to that period is refused, by the proposal and by the database.
4. `declarations.pdf` produces ANAF's PDF from the authorized XML, with the XML attached.
5. `declarations.importSigned` takes the PDF the company signed. It must carry, as an attachment, exactly the authorized XML (by hash) and a signature; the signature itself is verified by ANAF when the return is filed.
6. `declarations.recordSubmission` (human only) records the filing with ANAF's registration number from the receipt, and `declarations.recordOutcome` ANAF's acceptance or rejection with the message received.

To correct a filed period, `declarations.reopen.request` is a reviewed action that unlocks it in the name of the return that locked it. Corrections are then posted, and the rectification is prepared with `supersedesId`, which marks the earlier return superseded. A return can be superseded before it is submitted or once ANAF has answered, not while an answer is pending.

**D300 (VAT return)** is the first kind. It is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d300.ts` from ANAF's structure 12.0.0, in namespace `v11` for 08–12/2025 and `v12` from 01/2026, and computes the totals, the settlement rows, the control sum and the payment evidence number as ANAF's validator does. From the books:
- collected VAT goes by rate to rows 9 (21%) and 10 (11%), and 9.1 (19%) for 2025 periods;
- services bought from abroad with reverse charge go to row 7, and also to its subset 7.1 when the supplier is in the EU; the return repeats them as deductible on rows 20 and 20.1;
- services supplied abroad go to row 3, and to 3.1 when the customer is in the EU;
- purchases go by rate to rows 24 and 25 (24.1 for 2025) with all their VAT, and row 31 holds the VAT actually deducted. Whether non-deductible purchases belong on rows 24–25 is an interpretation awaiting the expert's review;
- every row is rounded to whole lei by the cited rule `ro.declaration.rounding` (half up at 50 bani);
- the header comes from the company's invoicing details and the options given when preparing (declarant, CAEN, bank account; pro-rata 100 as the printed form pre-fills it, unless given);
- the previous period's return in the engine supplies the negative balance carried forward (rd. 41) unless it requested a refund, and becomes an input: replacing it makes this return stale. Its payable still unpaid (rd. 38) is what the books have not paid of earlier periods' VAT settlements (4423, the payments matched by `tax.pay`), in whole lei and never more than its rd. 45; VAT differences established by a decision and unpaid (rd. 39) are the 4423 items `tax.decision` booked. Both read the 4423 items, so a payment booked after preparation makes the return stale. For the first period kept in the engine, the previous balances are given explicitly (`previous`). A refund is requested only for a negative balance of at least 5,000 lei, by the option `refundRequested` or a person's answer to `vat.refund` (`VAT_REFUND_CHOICE` without either). Rd. 42 (negative differences established by inspection) is not produced: a decision with negative principal is not booked.

A **change of regime** (V1): the return covers the days of the normal regime only, so the first one after a registration starts on the registration day and the last one before a cancellation ends the day before it (art. 310 alin. (7), art. 316 alin. (13)); a company registered during a period passes `NOT_VAT_PAYER`. The VAT of a late registration goes on rd. 16 (base and VAT) and rd. 33 (base and the VAT deducted), the capital goods' adjustment on rd. 34 (`R31_2`, signed), neither in rd. 31. The entries of the days a posted `vat.lateRegistration` declares are its own, not their period's; sales booked without VAT after the registration day and not declared by one block the return (`D300_LATE_REGISTRATION`). Where the late registration's amounts go (rd. 16 and 33) is an interpretation awaiting the expert's review (`ro.vat.late_registration`). The D394 of such a period lists the invoices of the days of the normal regime only, without the regularization entries, which are no invoices.

The domestic reverse charge (V3) goes on rd. 12 and its subsets by rate (acquisitions, `TI-A-<rate>`), repeated as deductible on rd. 26, and on rd. 13 (supplies, `TI-L`), with the header's reverse-charge flags; receipts deducted as simplified invoices are purchases at their rate (rows 24–25). Exempt operations (V2) go on rd. 1 (intra-community supplies of goods, `EX-IC`), rd. 14 (exempt with the right of deduction, `EX-294`, and exports, `EX-EXPORT`), rd. 15 (exempt without it, `EX-292`) and rd. 29 (exempt or not taxable purchases, `EX-IN`, and those outside the scope of VAT, `O`), base only; the year-end pro-rata adjustment (`ADJ-300`) and the adjustment of stock and services (`ADJ-304`) on rd. 34 with the capital goods'. The header's pro-rata is the year's provisional (`vat.prorata`), and the definitive in the return holding the year-end adjustment; 100 without one, as the printed form pre-fills it (a different `proRata` option is refused, `PRORATA_OPTION`). The special schemes (V4, [above](#special-vat-schemes-v4-the-margin-schemes-and-the-agricultural-flat-rate)) go on rd. 9 (the margins' base and VAT, `MS-311-21`, `MS-312-21`), rd. 29 (purchases under a scheme, `MS-311-A`, `MS-312-A`, and a farmer's products, `FR-315`), rd. 27 (the flat-rate compensation, VAT column only) and rd. 31 (the compensation deducted); a travel agency's sales (`MS-311-L`) have no row of their own. Goods across the border (G3, [above](#goods-across-the-border-g3-intra-community-supplies-and-acquisitions-imports-exports)): intra-community acquisitions (`AIC-<rate>`) on rd. 5 and 5.1, repeated on rd. 20 and 20.1; imports whose VAT was not paid at customs (`IMP-DEF-<rate>`, art. 326 alin. (4)–(5)) on rd. 7, repeated on rd. 22; imports whose VAT was paid at customs (`IMP-<rate>`) on rd. 24 (21%) or 25 (11%); rd. 31 the deducted. Operations without a row yet block the return rather than being left out. Monthly returns are type L and quarterly ones type T; semiannual and annual returns are not produced.

**D390 (VIES recapitulative statement)** lists a month's intra-Community operations by partner, from ANAF's structure "D390 XML 2020" (namespace v3): services supplied to EU businesses as type P and services acquired from EU suppliers as type S, each partner's bases summed and rounded to whole lei. It is monthly even for quarterly VAT payers, reads the month's entries by their tax point, and is informative: authorizing it locks no tax ledger. ANAF's validator checks every partner's VAT code against its country's algorithm. Intra-community supplies of goods are type L (`EX-IC`) and acquisitions type A (`AIC-<rate>`, the supplier's code, G3); triangular supplies (T) and the farmers' (R) are not booked. Outside VAT it is the art. 317 code holder's (art. 325 alin. (1)): without `vat.eu` `special` it is refused (`D390_NO_CODE`).

**D301 (special VAT return)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d301.ts` from ANAF's structure D301_A2.0.0 (from 01.01.2016, OPANAF nr. 592/2016, schema d301_20200130.xsd, namespace v1; validator J1.2.5, still the version ANAF's page publishes) and prepared for a month from the books of a company not registered under art. 316: the services bought from suppliers not established in Romania (the `RC-…` output entries whose tax point is in the month, on a day outside VAT) in section 4, those from suppliers established in the EU again in section 4.1, one line per invoice with its number and date, its value in its currency, the BNR rate the books used for VAT, and the base and VAT rounded to whole lei by `ro.declaration.rounding` (the structure's baza = round(val_valuta × curs, 0)). `pers_inreg` is 2 for the art. 317 code holder, 1 otherwise; nr_evid and the control sum are the structure's (ANAF's validator accepts a control sum one leu off, not more). It is due by the 25th of the next month, only for the months with such VAT (art. 324 alin. (2), `ro.deadline.D301`), and authorizing it locks the month's VAT ledger. A purchase reversed in a later month is a rectification of its month's return (`D301_REVERSAL`); intra-Community acquisitions of goods (sections 1–3) are not produced. In the month a company registers, a D301 for the days before and the D300 from the registration would both lock the month: not covered.

**D398 (the Union scheme's special VAT return, V4)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d398.ts` from ANAF's structure A1.0.10 and prepared for a quarter from the `EXP-OSS` entries, in euro, with the corrections of earlier quarters; nil returns included; ANAF's validator J1.0.11 runs in the SAF-T kit ([above](#sales-to-consumers-in-other-member-states-and-the-one-stop-shop-v4-invoiceseuconsumer-d398)). Sales to consumers in other Member States with the place in Romania are on D300 rd. 17 and its information section, the Union scheme's on no D300 row.

**D394 (domestic supplies and acquisitions)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d394.ts` from ANAF's structure of 15.09.2025 (namespace v5, periods from 08/2025), with the partner counts, summaries and control sum ANAF's validator checks, and read against the order's instructions as consolidated to 17.09.2025 (OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 anexa nr. 2, `OPANAF3769/2015-A`). From the books, for the company's VAT period (monthly L, quarterly T):
- domestic invoices of the period's VAT ledger, grouped by partner and rate: supplies (L) and acquisitions (A), with the number of invoices, and base and VAT rounded to whole lei; credit notes reduce their group; an invoice with operations at several rates counts once, for the rate with the largest VAT (the highest rate when it is the same), and 0 at the others (secțiunea a 2-a pct. 5);
- a partner is registered for VAT when its code on the invoice is RO followed by a valid CUI; other customers are reported with their CUI or CNP, or, without one, by the address of their invoicing details; a customer abroad with a code on the invoice is a taxable person not established in Romania (secțiunea 1 lit. E–F, secțiunea a 2-a pct. 3): of another Member State by its VAT code there (tip_partener 3), from outside the Union by its code (4); a supplier charging VAT without a VAT code blocks the statement;
- the series are those the engine issued in the period (the range allocated and the numbers issued), the issued credit notes are listed as storno invoices, and invoices issued outside them (the previous invoicer) must be declared with their ranges and count;
- an issued invoice not yet in the VAT ledger, and a refund requested by the period's D300 without the nature of the operations (or the reverse), block it;
- the header takes the company's invoicing details and, as options, the CAEN code, phone, representative, who prepared it, and the consent and affiliated-persons answers.
- receipts that are simplified invoices (`simplified: receipt`) go to rezumat2 by rate (`bazaBFAI`, `TVABFAI`), not to op1;
- the company's own receipts (G2): its Z reports by month in op2 I1 (the registers, the receipts, the total, each rate's base and VAT, a rate not sold at as 0; a report reversed by storno counts neither its receipts nor its register), rezumat2 (`baza_incasari_i1`, `tva_incasari_i1`, with a rezumat2 for each rate op2 writes, as ANAF's validator requires, R246–R255) and `informatii` (`nr_BF_i1`, `incasari_i1`); the receipts an invoice was issued for are taken out, the invoice listed among the supplies;
- the domestic reverse charge (V3): supplies as V at rate 0 and acquisitions as C at their rate, per partner, each with its products (op11: the D394 product codes, cereals by NC code; on an invoice holding several, the invoice counts for the largest, by base for V and by VAT for C, and an invoice with acquisitions at several rates counts once, as above) and rezumat1's detaliu by product, as ANAF's validator J8.0.2 takes them (no `tip_N`, no `tvaV`, V without `tva`).
- services bought from outside the Union whose VAT the company self-assesses (`invoices.crossBorder`, art. 307 alin. (2)): not in D390, so acquisitions C with the supplier as tip_partener 4, by its code on the invoice (`D394_PARTNER_CODE` without one), at the standard rate of the tax point, without products (anexa nr. 2 pct. 1 lit. b), secțiunea 1 lit. F pct. 8–10); nrCui3 and nrCui4 count these partners and the control sum includes them. Services bought from the Union are in D390, not here.
- the special schemes (V4): supplies under the margin schemes as LS (at rate 0, without VAT, at their price as invoiced: a single service's, an item's) and acquisitions from registered persons applying them as AS, per partner, summed in rezumat1's facturiLS, bazaLS, facturiAS, bazaAS (for a partner not registered, LS with the invoices of type N, document_N 1; a sale and its storno in the period cancel out, the item's price signed as its entry is, and leave no operation): the instructions ask for the values on the invoices, margin and VAT included (secțiunea a 2-a), though lit. C pct. 7 calls them a base. The margins' fields of `informatii` from the period's entries (secțiunea 1 lit. I pct. 6): `incasari_ag`, `costuri_ag`, `marja_ag` (the base, the margin without VAT), `tva_ag`, and `pret_vanzare`, `pret_cumparare`, `marja_antic` (the difference between the two prices, VAT included, as pct. 6.2 defines it: with an item sold at a loss, less than the sum of the margins taxed), `tva_antic`; an item bought from a person not registered for VAT and a farmer's products under the flat rate as N (the item's nature 34 unless given). ASI and the special schemes with partners established abroad (LS to them is declared as to a person not registered) are not produced; the self-invoice a dealer issues to the individual it buys from (norme pct. 86 alin. (6) lit. d)) is not listed among the invoices issued on the suppliers' behalf (lit. I pct. 2.2.4).
Services bought from the Union are in D390, and services supplied across the border in D390 and D300; intra-community acquisitions of goods (`AIC-<rate>`, D390) and imports (`IMP-<rate>`, `IMP-DEF-<rate>`, the customs declaration's) are not listed either; simplified invoices other than receipts are not produced yet. Exempt supplies and exempt or out-of-scope purchases from suppliers registered for VAT are not listed (the lists are of taxable operations, OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 anexa nr. 2 secțiunea a 2-a); an exempt invoice the engine issued counts as booked.
- **Acquisitions from persons not registered for VAT (type N).** The statement declares acquisitions "de la orice persoană" (OPANAF nr. 3.769/2015 art. 1: individuals included), type N being those from persons not registered (anexa nr. 2 secțiunea a 2-a pct. 2):
  - **What:** exempt purchases (`EX-IN`) and purchases outside the scope of VAT (`O`) from a Romanian supplier without a valid RO VAT code, by invoice (tip_document 1); the rent and intellectual property owed to an individual under a contract (`payees.recognize`, tip_document 4), services (Codul fiscal art. 271 alin. (3) lit. a), b)). One record per partner and document type, at rate 0, without VAT, the value rounded to the leu.
  - **Who:** a company outside VAT by its CUI; an individual by the CNP (the landlord's or author's recorded once with `parties.recordCnp` and opened for the declaration only; `D394_CNP` without it) or, without any code, by the address.
  - **The nature** of what an individual or a partner without a code sold (op11; anexa nr. 1 lit. D pct. 6 and the structure's product list for those partners: cereals by NC code, 22 waste, 23 timber, 32 land, 33 buildings, 34 other goods, 35 services): rent and intellectual property are services; an invoice takes its nature from the option `natures` (by the document's id; `D394_NATURE` without it). Only these partners' acquisitions carry it (validator R233.4, R233.6).
  - **Summaries:** rezumat1 per document type (facturiN, document_N, bazaN, and detaliu's nrN and valN by product, the cereals under 21), with facturiLS and bazaLS 0 for invoices and absent for any other document, as validator J8.0.2 requires; no rezumat2 (rate 0); each record counts in nrCui2; N stays outside the control sum.
  - **Contracts:** the consolidated instructions name the contracts concluded with individuals among the documents of N (anexa nr. 2 pct. 1 lit. b), secțiunea a 2-a pct. 2, 5 and 11) and ask for the individual's CNP when it was collected from a contract (pct. 3): rent and intellectual property owed to an individual are N with tip_document 4.
  - **A supplier abroad:** its exempt or out-of-scope invoice is not listed. N is for persons not registered (secțiunea 1 lit. D), and with partners not established in Romania only the taxable operations with the place in Romania whose VAT the beneficiary owes are declared (lit. E–F pct. 10).
  - **Not covered:** purchase slips and agricultural booklets as documents of their own.

**D112 (contributions, wage tax and the insured)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d112.ts` from ANAF's structures for the months of 2026 (D112_A7.2.6, namespace v7): of 30.03.2026 for 01–06/2026 (salMin 4.050 lei, Suma300 300 lei), of 07.09.2026 from 07/2026 (4.325 lei, Suma200 200 lei), each with its months' working days. The minimum wage and the relief are the register's for the month (`ro.payroll.minimum_wage`, `ro.payroll.minimum_wage_relief`), refused when they are not the structure's (`D112_PARAMETERS`); they set the limit sum of a contract below the minimum, (salMin − relief) × days worked / NZL, and the ceiling of a certificate's base. Medical leave and work accidents in 01–06/2026 are declared in the structure of those months (structura_D112_0126_300326, `ANAF/D112-0126`): the same sections, indemnity codes (Nomenclatoarele 9–10), C2 rows and bases (B3_7 = B3_12 + B3_13, the accident in B3_8–B3_11 and C3) as from 07/2026, without the fields 07/2026 added (D_9a, D_9b, D_14a, D_15a, D_16a, D_20a, D_21a, B3_7D, C2_155, C2_156), which both validators refuse there as unknown attributes; D_14 and D_15 are the days each payer bears, the day OUG nr. 91/2025 leaves unpaid only left out of them (from certificates issued on 1 February 2026: those issued in January lose none, even when they start in February, `D112_SICK_DAYS` otherwise), and D_17 is at most 12 × 6 × 4.050 = 291.600 lei. ANAF's J27.0.8 (the general kit) validates both halves, each with its own field set, and J26.0.3, published for 01–06/2026, is pinned in its own kit (`anaf-d112-0126`) for the official tests; a January 2026 with sick leave (code 01, with FNUASS's share), maternity and a confirmed work accident passes both, from the books and in the official tests. A month before 2026 or after 2026 is a FORMAT_GAP. From the books it declares the month's posted payroll run (the latest round standing, after a correction): each employee's insured type (51 with the minimum-wage relief, 6 for an administrator under a mandate), contract and hours, days, bases, contributions withheld and borne by the employer (obligations 458 and 459), deductions, tax and the amount paid, the benefits in kind by their E3 fields, the CNP opened from its seal for the declaration only; CAM is computed once on the employer's total. Amounts must be whole lei. It locks no tax ledger. The unemployment base (A_9) is declared as the salary paid in money (the untaxed indemnities excluded), an interpretation awaiting the expert's review. A rectifying D112 (d_rec 1) is declared as [Corrections](#corrections-of-a-posted-month-w1) says.

A **quarterly** employer (`payroll.period`, art. 80 alin. (2), art. 147 alin. (4)–(5)):
- It keeps the monthly computation and files one D112 per month, each by the 25th after the quarter.
- A month with sick leave or a contract ended is filed by the 25th after it, together with the quarter's months before it (art. 147 alin. (11); OPANAF 605/2026 anexa 6 cap. I). The text says so for the end of insurance in the second month; the engine reads sick leave the same way, an expert question (`ro.deadline.D112.quarterly`).
- The payroll posting gives the tax and contributions owed their payment day, after the month or the quarter by the year's own `payroll.period`, which alin. (11) does not move.
- The prepared D112 states its filing schedule in its summary (`filing`). The XML is the same in both regimes.
- The monthly option (art. 147 alin. (8)) is the year's determination as confirmed.

**D100 (obligations to the state budget)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d100.ts` (namespace v2, periods from 01/2026) and prepared for a month from the books: the micro-enterprise tax posted for the quarter (121, the amount on 4418, not computed again), in its last month; the profit tax of quarters I–III (103, model 8#: what the quarter's `tax.profit` booked on 4411, zero for a memorandum; the fourth quarter is D101's, refused in December); the dividend tax withheld in the month, for individuals (604) and companies (150), with, in December, the tax on dividends unpaid at year end; the tax withheld on interest paid to individuals (605); and (O1) on rent paid to individuals (628) and on non-residents' dividends, interest, royalties, commissions and services (631, 632, 633, 634, 636), each the sum of the month's postings (`withholding` in their explanation), model 1#, budget 5503, due the 25th of the next month. Intellectual property is not in D100 (D112's). The fourth quarter's micro tax is due by 25 January in ANAF's structure and by 25 June in its validator; the company chooses (`fourthQuarterDue`). A correction of a filed D100 is a separate form (D710): the worklist asks for it when the books change after filing, and a person records it filed outside the engine ([The tax account](#the-tax-account-t2)).

**D101 (annual profit tax return)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d101.ts` (OPANAF nr. 206/2025, ANAF's structure of 26.01.2026, namespace v10 as ANAF's validator J11.0.4 requires; years ending from 2024) and prepared for a year from the year's posted computation (`tax.profit` for the year, or its rectification, which makes the return rectifying, `d_rec` 1): every row as the position recorded it, never computed again; the period from the quarter of the micro exit (`trim_micro`, `Data_I`) to 31 December; the due date 25 June of the next year (`scadenta`, the structure's rule since 26.01.2026), the payment number and the control sum (the main rows P1–P53, as the validator's R19 adds them); the CAEN code is an option. `checkD101` repeats the structure's formulas and its validations V1–V8. Not written: a modified fiscal year, dissolution, the fiscal group, BNR/ASF entities, the art. 225/227 regularisation.

**D205 (tax withheld, by beneficiary)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d205.ts` (namespace v3, years 2025–2026) and prepared for a year from the posted withholdings: one record per individual shareholder, with the dividends distributed, paid and unpaid at year end and their tax; one per lender, with the interest paid and its tax (tip 09); one per landlord (O1, tip 29), with the net income of the year's rent payments (each rounded half up to the leu) and the tax, VB and GAR 0; companies are not declared in it, and intellectual property neither (its code, 01, is among those the structure abolished: art. 132 alin. (2) sends it to D112). An individual's CNP is recorded once (`parties.recordCnp`, kept sealed like employees'). Whether the dividends paid are declared gross or net the instructions do not say: the company chooses (`paidAs`).

**D207 (tax withheld from non-residents, by beneficiary)** (O1) is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/declarations/d207.ts` from ANAF's structure of 20.02.2026 (OPANAF nr. 179/2022, years from 2021; schema d207_20025020.xsd, namespace `mfp:anaf:dgti:d207:declaratie:v2`; validator J2.0.2, pinned in the general DUKIntegrator) and prepared for a year from the posted `nonresidents.withhold`: one record per beneficiary and income type (tip_venit1), with the state of residence (the certificate's, or the partner's country), the foreign tax code, the gross (baza1, rounded half up to the leu over the year), the tax withheld (imp1) and the act applied (Act_N 1 the Codul fiscal: 01 dividends, 02 interest, 03 royalties, 04 commissions, 07 services; 2 a treaty: 22–25, and 19–21 for a treaty's exemption, its gross in Tscutit). Section II totals each type; the control sum is the validator's R16 (nrben, Tscutit, Tbaza, Timp, Timps). The tax borne by the payer (Timps, imps1) is 0, that case being refused. Due by the last day of February (art. 231 alin. (1), `ro.deadline.D207`). `checkD207` repeats the structure's checks; passes ANAF's validator from the books and in the official tests.

**D406 (SAF-T)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/saft/d406.ts` (schema 2.4.9, periods from 07/2025) and prepared for each VAT period (L monthly, T quarterly; it must match `vat.period`), for each quarter outside VAT (T: "Contribuabilii care nu sunt înregistrați în scopuri de TVA, transmit Declarația informativă D406 trimestrial", OPANAF nr. 1.783/2021 anexa nr. 4 pct. 3, which OPANAF nr. 407/2025 left unchanged), and for each financial year as the annual assets file (A, below).
- **What it holds:**
  - accounts with their opening balances and turnover, equal to the trial balance;
  - customers and suppliers from the open items, one entry per partner and account, equal to their open items;
  - one journal per journal code, each entry with its justifying document and its currency side;
  - the invoices from the retained UBL, with the booked account;
  - products from the invoice lines;
  - the bank entries settling partners, as payments.
- **The regime** (Header, TaxRegistration/TaxType, Nomenclator_Regim_fiscal): 100010 registered, 100040 under VAT on collection, and 100020 outside VAT ("Persoană impozabilă ce aplică regimul special de scutire pentru întreprinderile mici", art. 310).
- **Partners:** a Romanian entity 00 with its CUI, of another Member State 01 with its VAT code, outside the Union 02 with the code its details record, an individual 03 with the CNP. A partner abroad without a VAT code of its state takes a code the company assigns it, its id in the books (S.C.1; ANAF's answers v1.2, MF.C.3, 6: one per person, never shared): 04 for a consumer of another Member State (the customers of `invoices.euConsumer`: "pers. fizice care nu își declară CNP-ul pe tranzacții (exemplu: comerț online)"), 05 with its country for an economic operator of the Union not registered for VAT, 06 for one outside it. A refund a credit note opened beyond its original's balance (462 owed to a customer, 461 owed by a supplier) is its partner's line too, in the period it opens and in every later one (6 November 2026): the master files list the partner on 462 or 461 with the refund's balance at the period's start while it is open, the ledger's lines that revalue, pay back or offset it name the partner, and its payment's line its credit note (SourceDocumentID). A partner's AccountID is the account the transactions with it are recorded on (ANAF's answers v1.2, MF.C.5, whose question names 4111 and 461). On the Payments' lines the partner is who paid or was paid: "întotdeauna plata se face de la Customer (identificat prin CustomerID) către Supplier (identificat prin SupplierID)", so a customer paid back is the SupplierID and a supplier paying back the CustomerID, the other 0 (Răspuns 21, 2.c; extracted, and the writer takes either id among the master files' partners). Which account holds a refund, 462 or 461, is the expert's question ([`invoices.credit`](#invoicescredit-credit-note-against-the-original)).
- **Currency lines:** each line in a currency states the rate its lei were measured at (ExchangeRate: "CurrencyAmount x ExchangeRate = Amount", the schema and the workbook's S.AM.4): a collection releases the receivable at its carrying rate (`bank.settle`), the bank at the day's, the realized difference in lei only; the Payments line mirrors the partner's, the difference left out (ANAF's answers v1.2, Payments 20). A TaxAmount states its own ExchangeRate when its VAT is not at its line's rate: a credit note's VAT at its invoice's rate (art. 282 alin. (9)) on a revenue or expense line at the note's (each AmountStructure's "Amount (LEI) = CurrencyAmount x ExchangeRate", ANAF's answers v1.2, MF.C.6); in SalesInvoices and PurchaseInvoices that VAT is converted at its own rate too. ANAF's validator does not check the conversion (S.AM.1); the writer does (`D406_CONVERSION`), each amount at its own rate. How amounts in a currency are written is `ro.d406.conversion` (6 November 2026, extracted from the definitions' S.AM.1–S.AM.4 and S.TI.4 and ANAF's answers v1.2, MF.C.6; an interpretation with a review packet, `reviews/specs/saft.ts`):
  - **A line no rate gives** (`unrated: split`): a receivable or payable carried at no single rate (its revenue at the invoice's rate, its VAT in lei at the VAT rate, BT-111) has lines whose lei are no currency amount at a rate of four decimals (SAFexchangerateType): its own, and those that release it in proportion (`bank.settle`, a credit note) before a revaluation brings it to one rate. Each is written in two: its currency at the rate the item was recorded at (its origin's line), and the lei that leaves in lei only (CurrencyCode RON), in the ledger and in Payments alike; the ledger is unchanged, and so are the partner's balances. The other reading (`lei`) writes the line in lei alone, as a lease's principal released at its carrying amount is written.
  - **The TaxBase of an invoice line in a currency** (`taxBase: tax`) is its share of its tax entry's base, the VAT base in lei at the tax's rate: "Cursul de schimb utilizat pentru determinarea in RON a bazei impozabile a taxei este cursul din data exigibilitatii taxei" (S.AM.4), "Baza pe care se calculează impozitul" (S.TI.4), and for a credit note its invoice's (art. 282 alin. (9)), as the ledger's base line gives it. So the code's rate applied to it gives the VAT, as ANAF's consistency tests 20–22 read it (`D406_VAT`): a credit note in euro at another rate than its invoice's, an invoice whose supply came before it, and a consumer's credit note at the place of origin on a revalued receivable no longer differ. The other reading (`line`) is the line's lei at the invoice's rate, which those tests flag.
- **Advances:** an advance invoice's lines are reported on 419 (or 4091/4092) and the final invoice's deduction as its negative line, with negative base and VAT. Advances paid count as goods (4091) or services (4092); what customers' advances (419) are for is the option `advances`.
- **VAT codes** go on the base line, through a mapping table (`saft/tax-codes.ts`) in which each row quotes ANAF's description. A purchase with half its VAT deducted (vehicles) takes ANAF's 50% codes (34xxxx). A purchase deducted under a pro-rata takes ANAF's base-rate codes (36xxxx) with the pro-rata as the TaxTable's BaseRate ([Exempt supplies](#exempt-supplies-the-pro-rata-and-capital-goods-v2)); the domestic reverse charge its simplification-measure codes ([`invoices.reverseCharge`](#invoicesreversecharge-the-domestic-reverse-charge-v3)); a purchase outside the scope of VAT (`O`) the exempt purchases' 308302; a late registration's regularization (`REG-310`) at the rate of its range, which its entry records, "Regularizări taxă colectată" (310345 at 21%, 310352, 310317) on its expense and "Regularizări taxă dedusă" (309106, 309107, 309101) on its income, by the words of D300 rd. 16 and 33 (the sheets' own row numbers lag the return: the expert confirms). Outside VAT, no row of ANAF's sheets names the special scheme, and every line needs its VAT information (GL.33, S.I.49): a sale (category O, no VAT entry) takes 310326, "scutite fără drept de deducere", on its revenue and invoice lines (the expert's choice against 310324, "nu intră în sfera de aplicare", which art. 268 and 310 contradict), and so does a sale invoiced without VAT on a day a posted late registration declares, whose VAT is the regularization's; a purchase with VAT it cannot deduct the 3511xx rows; services from abroad whose VAT it owes through D301 (`regime: special`, art. 307 alin. (2)) the 3563xx rows "pentru care beneficiarul este obligat la plata TVA cf. art. 307 alin. (2)" (356319/356320/356306 from the Union, 356317/356318/356304 from outside it), on the expense, the VAT expensed beside it and its 4423 item no base. A sale declared in the Union scheme is 310340 at the consumer state's rate (`ro.d406.oss`, [OSS](#sales-to-consumers-in-other-member-states-and-the-one-stop-shop-v4-invoiceseuconsumer-d398)): the one code of the sheets whose rate is a Member State's (`d406MemberStateRateCodes`, its rate column "În funcție de Cota TVA din statul membru"), so each line gives the rate (TaxPercentage, as the guide's 2.5 reads it: "procentul de taxa aplicabil pentru codul de taxă respectiv") and the TaxTable a TaxCodeDetails per state and rate, the state as its Country (MF.TT.12 takes any ISO 3166 code, RO its default); the invoice totals one TaxInformationTotals per rate; the writer refuses such a code without its rate or state, a state's rate on a code that has its own, and checks the VAT against the state's rate (`D406_VAT`). ANAF's validator (J2.2.8) checks neither the line's rate against the table nor the Country. A code with no certain SAF-T code (a payer's sale in `O`, 9%, 5%, other partial deductions) is a problem, never a guess.
- **Validation:** ANAF's D406 validator runs only in ANAF's SAF-T kit, pinned separately (`anaf-saft`). The engine sends D406 there and every other return to the general DUKIntegrator, and records which kit ran.
- **The validator doesn't check** that entries balance, the control totals, or currency conversion. Those are the writer's own checks (`checkD406`).
- **Empty sections:** Customers, Suppliers and Products may hold no entry (ANAF's definitions MF.C.1, MF.S.1, MF.P.1: 0..*), as a shop's month on Z reports alone has none; ANAF's validator accepts the file.
- **Lines that are no VAT base:** besides the partner's and the VAT accounts', an authority's tax item (an import's customs debt on 446, the Union scheme's VAT on 4423) and the exchange difference a credit note realizes (665, 765).
- **Goods' lines are no VAT base** (G1–G4): a reception at sale price reports its VAT on the cost's line, not on the VAT included and the markup added on top (371 against 4428 and 378); a count's shortage whose deducted VAT is adjusted on the adjustment's 635 line, not on the goods' (3xx against 60x or 711); a sale of products on its revenue, not on 711.
- **AccountID** of an account whose code has no numeric form (a retail warehouse's `371.M2-21`, `378.M2`, `4428.M2-21`): the option `accountIds` gives it, digits beginning with its synthetic, as the company's chart numbers it.
- **Taxes other than VAT:** payroll's lines (CAS, CASS, the income tax, CAM) carry TaxType 000 and TaxCode 000000. For taxes other than VAT and those withheld at source, ANAF lets the company report either the tax's own TaxType (412, 432, 602, 480, 458, 459) with TaxCode 000000 or the generic 000, and recommends the generic one (ANAF's answers v1.2, MF.TT.2, option 2).
- **Tax withheld at source** (O1): the line it is withheld from (the debit of the beneficiary's item: 457, 456, 462, 401, an interest account) carries its D100 obligation as TaxType (604, 150, 605, 628, 631–636) and ANAF's WHT code of its rate as TaxCode (`src/ro/withholding/codes.ts`, every code of the sheet „WHT - nomenclator” of the pinned definitions of 16.02.2026, which add 604040 for an individual's dividends at 16%), with the base and the tax, as ANAF's guide says (sections 2.5 and 3: on the line relevant to the base, the payment made); the 446 line keeps 000. The TaxTable lists each WHT TaxType used with its codes (BaseRate 1). A treaty's dividend rate the sheet lists only as a domestic code (5%, 10%) takes that code. Intellectual property, declared in D112, keeps 000. The Payments section carries no WHT information yet (the guide asks for it there too; ANAF's validator accepts the file without it). Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator from the books.
- **The definitions workbook:** RO_SAFT_SchemaDefCod_16.02.2026.xlsx (sha256 050508bf…) is pinned; every sheet but its history and the WHT nomenclature reads as the 05.02.2026 one, so the extracted charts and VAT codes are unchanged.
- **Lease instalments:** see [Loans and leases](#loans-and-leases-loans-leasescommence).
- **A rate's lines on several accounts:** an invoice split by rate is reported, each line on its rate's account and with its rate's VAT; within a rate, each line goes to the account of the goods it received or returned (G1: the item its stock input names, on the account the entry's moves booked), else to the one grouping of the rate's lines whose amounts make up each part's (a lease's instalment, two materials or services at one rate). Lines nothing tells apart (equal amounts on two accounts without goods, or one line split over accounts) are refused (`D406_SPLIT`).
- **Not produced yet:** the stock file, invoices with document-level allowances, WHT on the Payments' lines, and invoices without UBL XML. The import scheme (IOSS) is not built ([OSS](#sales-to-consumers-in-other-member-states-and-the-one-stop-shop-v4-invoiceseuconsumer-d398)), so no D406 holds its sales.

**D406 for assets (D406 Active)** is written by `xfactura-core/src/ro/saft/assets.ts` and prepared as D406 for a period `YYYY`: the financial year ending in it, from 2025. It is filed once a year by the statements' deadline (OPANAF 1783/2021 anexa 4 pct. 1, 7–8) as an independent declaration, HeaderComment A.
- **What it holds:** the Header; the GeneralLedgerAccounts of the fixed assets and their depreciation (20x, 21x, 280x, 281x) with the year's opening and closing balances, as ANAF's worked examples list them; every asset of the register held in the year; their movements (AssetTransactions). The other sections are written empty: ANAF's validator wants them present.
- **Each asset:** inventory number, account, description, supplier (the purchase invoice's partner, or the lessor), date of acquisition (the purchase's or the lease's entry), commissioning date, the catalogue code of HG 2139/2004 as ValuationClass, life in years (AssetLifeYear) or months, the value at the year's start and end, the year's addition and disposal, the net value at both ends, the depreciation of the year and accumulated (negative), straight-line (LINIARĂ) with its rate.
- **Movements:** a purchase (10) from its entry; a depreciation (30) per depreciation entry; a disposal by how it left (option `disposals` by inventory number: `sale` with its invoice, whose revenue on 7583 gives the gain or loss, `scrap` (50) or `other` (130), at its net value, negative). Each names the journal entry that booked it.
- **Readings** ANAF's documents disagree on are the parameter `ro.d406.assets.presentation`: the rate in percent (ANAF's answer to MF.A.26) and the disposal at its gross value (ANAF's examples). The rest follows ANAF's answers and, where they are silent, its examples. A review packet asks the expert.
- **Checks** (`checkD406Assets`): a year of twelve months (the validator refuses any other: a shorter first year is UNSUPPORTED), each asset's values add up, its movements are the year's, and the register agrees with every asset and depreciation account at both ends of the year (`D406A_REGISTER`). Refused: a disposal not yet derecognized, an asset entering without its entry, revaluation reserves (105), impairment (290x, 291x) and investment subsidies (475x).
- **Order:** ANAF takes it after the year's periodic files (its clarification of 14.11.2022). Authorizing it is blocked while a periodic D406 of the year prepared in the engine is not filed (`D406A_PERIODIC`).
- Passes ANAF's SAF-T validator, from the books and in the official tests.
- **Agent-ready** (`ro/saft/agent.ts`): the documents (ANAF's classification as a large or medium taxpayer, the disposal record or sale invoice), `profile.fact` for `taxpayer.category`, the `obligation.due` detector with the rules it enforces (`ro.deadline.D406`, `ro.deadline.D406.assets`, `ro.d406.start`), no template fields.

**Obligations calendar.** `obligations.calendar` lists the returns whose periods end in a range of months, with their deadlines and where each one stands: due, overdue, prepared, authorized, filed, accepted, rejected, or blocked. A return is blocked when its deadline isn't sourced for the period, when whether it applies depends on a fact nobody recorded (the row names the question to ask), or when the engine doesn't cover it (the row names the case). A blocked return whose deadline's rule is beyond the register's horizon for its period (a new year before its rollover, [ROLLOVER.md](ROLLOVER.md)) carries `waiting`, the year whose rules it waits for: what else it lacks (that year's VAT period, D406's start) waits on them too. Each row gives its `basis`: the closure rule, the facts it read and the clauses. **Filed outside the engine:** a return a person recorded with `obligation.filed` (its registration number, the date and ANAF's receipt as evidence) is `filed`, whatever the return: D700 and INS's Intrastat, which the engine never prepares, and any other the company filed through ANAF's portal (a month whose format the engine does not write, a return filed before the books moved here). The engine's own filing of it stands over the record.
- **Which returns apply** is the obligation closure's ([Determinations and the obligation closure](#determinations-and-the-obligation-closure)), from the recorded facts, the yearly determinations and the books:
  - VAT payers file D300 and D394 for each VAT period, and D390 for the months with services across the EU border. A company leaving VAT files its last D300 and D394 for the period up to the day before the cancellation, by the 25th (D394 the 30th) after the month the decision was communicated in, whatever its period (art. 310 alin. (7), art. 316 alin. (13)).
  - Outside VAT, D301 for each month with services bought from abroad (art. 324 alin. (1) lit. d), with the art. 317 code or without), and D390 for the months with services across the EU border when the company holds the art. 317 code (art. 325 alin. (1)); without the code that D390 is UNSUPPORTED (`EU_SERVICES_NON_VAT`).
  - Every company files D406, for each VAT period, or quarterly outside VAT (anexa 4 pct. 3); a PFA, II or IF does not (OPANAF 1783/2021 anexa 5 pct. 4). From 1 January 2025 every category files; before it, the taxpayer category (`taxpayer.category`: large, medium, small, dated from ANAF's classification) decides, by the reference dates of `ro.d406.start` (anexa 5 pct. 1.1–2, as OPANAF 407/2025 replaced it). Unknown, it is asked.
  - A company holding fixed assets files D406 for assets for each financial year its D406 covers whole.
  - Employers file D112 monthly, or, when `payroll.period` is quarterly, each month's D112 by the 25th after the quarter (`ro.deadline.D112.quarterly`, art. 147 alin. (4)), except a month with sick leave or a contract ended and the quarter's months before it, due by the 25th after that month (alin. (11)).
  - Microenterprises file D100 for each quarter; it's also due in the months with dividend tax withheld, and D205 follows for those years. Any other month with tax withheld at source (interest, rent, non-residents: O1) has its D100 (`ro.deadline.D100.withholding`, art. 84^1 alin. (6), 97 alin. (2), 224 alin. (5)), and its year D205 (residents) or D207 (non-residents, `ro.deadline.D207`, art. 231 alin. (1)); intellectual property's D112 is listed as unsupported. A profit-tax payer files D100 for quarters I–III (`ro.deadline.D100.profit`, art. 41 alin. (1)) and D101 for the year, by 25 June of the next year (`ro.deadline.D101`, art. 42 alin. (1) as OUG nr. 8/2026 rewrote it, from the return for 2026).
  - A change of the income tax regime is declared on D700 (art. 55): entering micro by 31 March of the year, leaving it during the year by 31 March of the next, leaving it from 1 January shown with both readings of the text. The engine does not prepare D700: the company files it through ANAF's portal and a person records it (`obligation.filed`), which marks it filed.
  - A company registered in the Union scheme (`vat.oss` `union`) files D398 for each quarter registered on some day, with or without sales (art. 315 alin. (6)); the import scheme's monthly returns are listed UNSUPPORTED (`D398.import`).
  - A provider of Intrastat data (`intrastat.arrivals`, `intrastat.dispatches`, G3) files INS's declaration for each month it provides, by the 15th of the next month, outside the engine: marked filed once a person answers `obligation.filed`. D390 is also due for the months with intra-community acquisitions of goods.
  - Annual statements are due for each financial year kept in double entry, by the year's size category: S1005 for a microentity, S1003 for a small entity; medium, large and audited entities are not covered. A company that a person states has carried out no activity since it was set up (`activity.none`) files S1046 instead.
- **The deadlines** are cited parameters (`ro.deadline.*`, `xfactura-core/src/ro/obligations.ts`):
  - D300 (Codul fiscal art. 323), D301 (art. 324 alin. (2), OPANAF 592/2016 anexa 2 pct. II), D112 (art. 147), D100 (art. 56, 43 and 97) and D390 (OPANAF 591/2016): the 25th of the next month;
  - D406: the last day of the next month (OPANAF 1783/2021 anexa 4); D406 for assets (`ro.deadline.D406.assets`): the statements' deadline (anexa 4 pct. 1, 7), so the same two readings for 2025 and 150 days from 2026;
  - D205 and D207: the end of February (art. 132, art. 231);
  - S1005 and S1003: for 2025, a `conflict` deadline: 150 days after the year (Legea 82/1991 art. 36 alin. (1) lit. a)) against 31 May (OMF nr. 2.036/2025 anexa nr. 1 pct. 3.1 alin. (1) lit. a)), both shown and the earlier to file by (2 June 2026 for both, after the weekend and 1 June); from 2026, with no order published, the law's 150 days;
  - S1046: 60 days after the year (art. 36 alin. (2); for 2025 also OMF pct. 3.3).
- **How deadlines move:**
  - A deadline on a non-working day moves to the next working day: the procedure code's art. 75 refers to the Civil Procedure Code, whose art. 181 is still to be read.
  - What falls due on 25 December is due on 21 December, or the working day before it (procedure code art. 155(2), read in ANAF's 2019 consolidation; the text in force couldn't be read).
- **D398:** the last calendar day of the month after the quarter, even a non-working one (`fixed`, art. 315 alin. (6^1)): the one deadline not moved.
- **D394:** the 30th of the next month, or the end of February for January (OPANAF 3769/2015 art. 8 as rewritten by OPANAF 2328/2016 and the instructions' pct. 2, unchanged in the consolidation of 17.09.2025). The management procedure (anexa nr. 3 pct. 1) still says the 25th: the text of 2015, never amended, which the engine does not follow.
- **Past the horizon:** the deadlines were read through 2026, so later periods show as blocked.

Steps are recorded in order, and the database allows only the documented transitions; a tax period is locked only in the name of an authorized return of that tax and period. A rectification is a new return that supersedes the earlier one.

## Determinations and the obligation closure

Which regime a company is in is decided each year, from its books, its facts and its owner's answers, under cited rules. The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/determinations` (`determine.ts`, `closure.ts`, `agent.ts`); the engine module `modules/determinations.ts` feeds it. Nothing is defaulted: a determination is a **proposal** of a dated profile fact that a person confirms, with its computation shown, or it says what it lacks.

**Operations:**
- `determinations.evaluate {year}` (read): each determination with its status, value, changes within the year, computation (Romanian lines), inputs with their source (books, an answer), clauses, parameter versions and fingerprint; what is recorded for the year and whether it agrees; and the thresholds reached.
- `determinations.propose {year}` (agents may): opens what a person must answer. Each computed determination not recorded, or contradicted by the recorded facts, becomes a `determination.confirm` question holding the computation and its fingerprint; each unknown input, the question it names. A question still open about an older computation is withdrawn; an answer that still holds is returned.
- `closure.evaluate {from, to}` (read): the obligation closure ([CONFORMANCE.md](CONFORMANCE.md#the-closure-as-built-t1-30-october-2026)), over the months the books cover: before the company's first financial year (one starting mid-month covers that month) it lists no return and asks nothing, nor the annual returns of a year before it, and the years before it are not determined. A company with no financial year at all is read from its facts alone. D212 and D204 wait for a known PFA, II or IF form; a PFA, II or IF files no D406 (OPANAF 1783/2021 anexa 5 pct. 4, as OPANAF 407/2025 replaced it). Before 2025, a company's D406 waits on its taxpayer category.

**Answering:** only a person (an owner or reviewer) answers. Confirming records the value from 1 January and each change within the year, as profile facts citing the answer, which holds the computation shown. Correcting records another value, citing the document that supports it (a D700, the registration). A recorded fact confirmed against the current computation's fingerprint is not questioned again; a fact recorded otherwise that the computation contradicts is a conflict until confirmed.

**The yearly axes** (`income_tax`, `vat.period`, `payroll.period`, `size.category`) need a fact effective within the year; one carried from an earlier year is not the year's determination. `vat.threshold`, `vat.cash`, `vat.status` and `vat.eu` are checked each year but hold until changed; for them a recorded change the computation does not make (a cancellation the law does not allow) disagrees too, and a value that starts during the year (a registration, a special code obtained) is compared from the day it starts. Two scope facts are asked of every company: `activity.goods` (goods or stock: not covered yet; `margin_goods`, only individual items under the margin scheme, is covered, V4) and `regime.excluded` (excise, a fiscal group, IFRS: out of scope).

| Determination | Computed from | Rule (parameter) | Asked when |
|---|---|---|---|
| `income_tax`: micro or profit (SRL) | The previous year's turnover (70x net of 709) with the linked enterprises', against 100,000 € at the BNR rate valid at that year's close (art. 47 alin. (1) lit. c), alin. (1^1), art. 54); at least one full-time equivalent under contract at 31 December (lit. g), art. 51 alin. (4)); the owner's answer for lit. d), e), h), i) and alin. (3). Eligible, the previous year's regime carries (art. 48). During the year, the exit from the quarter the limit is crossed, own and linked revenue together (art. 52 alin. (1), (5^1)–(5^2), a quarter of a linked norm per quarter elapsed), from the quarter after the only employee left and was not replaced in 30 days (alin. (3)), from the quarter the statements became late (alin. (2)), and from the quarter the company started an excluded activity or its shareholders chose it to leave for holding other micro-enterprises (alin. (4), (7), dates in the owner's answer). The consulting share is no condition from 2025 (lit. f) repealed by OUG nr. 156/2024). | `ro.micro.revenue_limit_eur`, `ro.micro.employee`, `ro.micro.linked` | the owner's conditions and linked enterprises (`micro.conditions`), a previous year outside the books (`year.figures`), the option when eligible without a regime the year before (`profile.fact`) |
| `vat.period`: monthly or quarterly | The previous year's taxable, exempt-with-deduction and abroad-with-deduction supplies (the bases of the output tax entries) against 100,000 € at the rate of 31 December, and no EU goods acquisitions (art. 322 alin. (2); norme pct. 103 (2)–(3)). The year of a registration: the turnover obtained before it, recalculated for a whole year (`ro.vat.registration.annualized`: by days of activity; by months is the alternative reading), from the registration day (art. 322 alin. (4)); the year after: the turnover from the registration to 31 December, recalculated the same way (alin. (5)). An ex officio registration is monthly (norme pct. 103 alin. (1)): the decision shows it. | `ro.vat.period.limit_eur`, `ro.vat.registration.annualized` | a registration while the year is not kept from 1 January, a previous year outside the books |
| `payroll.period`: monthly or quarterly D112 | A PFA, II or IF with employees: quarterly (art. 80 alin. (2) lit. d)). An SRL: the previous year's mean headcount over the months with a D112 below 3, and for a profit-tax payer total revenues (class 7) up to 100,000 € (lit. b)–c), art. 147 alin. (6)–(7)). The option for monthly (art. 147 alin. (8)) is the confirming person's to state. | `ro.payroll.quarterly` | the year's income tax not recorded, a previous year outside the books |
| `size.category`: micro, small or medium/large | Total assets (balance sheet A–C from the year-end balances with the S1005 formulas, OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 15), net turnover (70x − 709 + 7411) and the 12-month mean headcount, for the previous and the current year. The category changes only when both years are beyond it (pct. 12–13): up to the lower, down to the higher. Medium or large, or above the audit thresholds in both years, is UNSUPPORTED. | `ro.size.thresholds` | the previous year's category, a previous year outside the books; a crossing year before the current one ends |
| `vat.threshold`: under or exceeded (non-payers) | The year's revenue on 70x, net of 709, by day, less supplies abroad (a non-payer's sales have no VAT entries; asset sales on 7583 are left out, as art. 310 alin. (2) leaves them); the day it exceeds 395,000 lei, counted while the company was outside VAT the day before (a registration valid from the crossing day does not stop the count). A company set up during the year (its first financial year starts then, Legea 82/1991 art. 27 alin. (8)) counts from its first day against the whole threshold (art. 310 alin. (5)). | `ro.vat.exemption.threshold` | the year not kept from 1 January nor started in it |
| `vat.status`: registered or not | From the status at the year's start: registered from the crossing day when the threshold is exceeded (art. 316 alin. (1^1) lit. b), f); a later recorded date is said and stays a conflict); a recorded registration before any crossing as an option (art. 310 alin. (3)); a recorded cancellation when neither the previous year's nor this year's turnover up to it exceeded the threshold (art. 310 alin. (7)), otherwise not (the recorded fact then conflicts). | `ro.vat.exemption.threshold` | the status at the start not recorded; the previous year's turnover for a cancellation |
| `vat.eu`: the art. 317 code (non-payers) | The year's invoices from suppliers established in another member state and to customers there, while outside VAT: the code is needed before the first (art. 317 alin. (1) lit. b)–c)), valid from the request (norme pct. 90 alin. (4)); held from a recorded `special` fact on or before it. Services to or from outside the Union need none. | (clauses) | — |
| `vat.distance`: the place of distance sales and TBE services to EU consumers (V4) | The year's such sales by tax point, cumulated in lei, against 46.337 lei, and the previous year's total (art. 278^1 alin. (1)–(2), (5)); the option for at least two years (alin. (3)) | `ro.vat.distance.threshold`, `ro.vat.distance.option_years` | the previous year outside the books (`year.figures` `distanceTurnover`) |
| `vat.oss`: the Union scheme (V4) | Needed from the first day at the destination; `none`, a later registration or `non_union` UNSUPPORTED (art. 315, 314) | (clauses) | `vat.oss` not recorded once at the destination |
| `vat.ioss`: the import scheme (V4) | Recorded `registered` or `intermediary`: UNSUPPORTED (art. 315^2) | `ro.vat.ioss` | a company with goods (`activity.goods`) |
| `intrastat.arrivals`, `intrastat.dispatches`: an Intrastat data provider (G3) | The year's intra-Union acquisitions (AIC) and supplies (EX-IC) of goods by the month they moved, against INS's threshold: the whole year after a previous year over its threshold (the order's art. 2), else from the month of crossing (art. 3); not applicable without the flow | `ro.intrastat.thresholds` | the previous year outside the books (`year.figures` `intrastatArrivals`, `intrastatDispatches`) |
| `vat.cash`: eligibility (VAT payers) | The previous year's VAT bases against the ceiling in force at the year's end (art. 282 alin. (3^1), (4)); during the year, the day the ceiling is crossed: out from the day after the next VAT period (alin. (5)). | `ro.vat.cash.ceiling` | `vat.cash` not recorded |

**Figures of a year the engine does not keep** (no financial year from 1 January) come from a person's answer to `year.figures`, with the filed statements and returns as evidence; a figure the books cannot place (total assets with an account split between an asset and a liability row) is asked the same way. The BNR rate of a year's close must be imported (`fx.importBnr`).

**The worklist** shows `determination.missing` for each yearly axis without its fact or contradicted by the books (blocking when contradicted), and `threshold.approaching` for the micro-enterprise limit, the small-enterprise threshold, the VAT on collection ceiling and the threshold of art. 278^1 (V4) at 80% (warning) and over 100% (blocking); 80% is a product choice, not a legal value. Both move on with `determinations.propose`.

**Agent-ready** (`agent.ts`): the documents it takes (the trade register's certificate, the fiscal registration and vector, D700, the shareholders' register, the filed annual statements), its questions and who answers them, its two detectors and the rules they enforce, and no template fields.

**Parameters** (all `extracted`, reviewed through 2026-12-31): `ro.size.thresholds` (OMF nr. 2.036/2025 anexa 1 pct. 2.1–2.3 and 2.6; OMFP nr. 1.802/2014 pct. 12–13 and 15; from 2025), `ro.vat.period.limit_eur`, `ro.vat.exemption.threshold`, `ro.payroll.quarterly`, `ro.micro.employee`, `ro.micro.linked` and `ro.deadline.D112.quarterly` (Codul fiscal in ANAF's consolidation; from 2026, earlier years are gaps). Four readings have review packets for the expert contabil: the linked norm during the year, the mean headcount of the quarterly D112, the two-year rule, and when a supply counts towards the small-enterprise threshold.

## The tax account (T2)

What the company owes the state and is owed by it, as the single treasury account (contul unic) settles it. The pure code is in `xfactura-core/src/ro/taxaccount` (`obligations.ts`, `distribution.ts`, `documents.ts`, `reconcile.ts`, `treatments.ts`, `agent.ts`); the engine module is `modules/taxaccount.ts`. Sources: the Codul de procedură fiscală in the legislative portal's consolidation of 08.08.2026 (`L207/2015`), ANAF's methodology of distribution (OPANAF nr. 1.613/2018 as OPANAF nr. 146/2025 amended it), its nomenclator (OPANAF nr. 1.612/2018, annex replaced by OPANAF nr. 551/2021 and amended by OPANAF nr. 874/2024), the payment record number (OMFP nr. 3.832/2015), the decision models (OPANAF nr. 967/2017, 727/2019, 352/2022) and OMFP nr. 1.802/2014.

**Obligations.** Every open item of a tax authority (a public institution) on a tax account is one obligation (`obligationOf`): its type from the account (`ro.taxaccount.obligations`: 444 salary tax, 4315 CAS, 4316 CASS, 4411 profit tax, 4418 micro tax, 4423 VAT, 436 CAM, 446 split by the treatment that booked it, and the tag of its line where one treatment books several, into dividend, interest, rent, intellectual property and non-residents' dividend, interest, royalty, commission and services tax), its budget (the state budget, BASS for CAS, FNUASS for CASS, Codul fiscal art. 2 alin. (2)), its category (withheld at source, other principal, accessory), whether it is paid into the single account (VAT and CAM are not in the nomenclator: each is paid into its own account, art. 163 alin. (7)), its period and due date from the posting that opened it (`OpenItemQuery.origins`; `vat.settle`, `tax.micro`, `tax.profit`, `dividends.withhold`, `payees.withhold`, `nonresidents.withhold` and `loans.withhold` give their items these dates when they open them, so the worklist reads the same: the D300 deadline for `vat.settle`, D100's for `tax.micro` and the profit tax's quarters, D101's for the year, D100's for dividend tax, the withholding day for interest tax by art. 97 alin. (2), the 25th of the month after it was withheld for rent and the non-residents' income (`ro.deadline.D100.withholding`, art. 84^1 alin. (6), art. 224 alin. (5)) and for intellectual property (D112's deadline, which is art. 72 alin. (5)'s, art. 147 alin. (1)), the item's own due date for payroll and decisions; rent tax is in the nomenclator from 09.05.2024, OPANAF nr. 874/2024 poz. 33^3, paid into its own account before) and its age (the due date; for what a decision established, its communication date, art. 165 alin. (3) lit. b)). Accessories and assessed differences carry their obligation on the line that opened them (`analytics.item` `taxaccount:<type>:<period>:<kind>`).

**`tax.pay`: a payment to the state** (or money from it), subject `movement:<id>` like `bank.settle`:
- **Input:** the movement, the authority, `to` (the single account, or the type whose own account the order paid: `vat`, `cam`), `designations` (the obligations the payer chose, with the payment record number) and `dueDates` (an obligation the books cannot date, an opening balance; acknowledged).
- **Into the single account** (`ro.taxaccount.distribution`, art. 163 alin. (3)–(5), OPANAF 1.613/2018 pct. 4–7): what the payer designated first, where the law lets them choose (obligations from an inspection, a decision's accessories, fines: art. 165 alin. (8), NEP); then the obligations due on the payment's date: all of them when the payment covers them, otherwise between budgets in proportion to what each is owed, within a budget withheld taxes and contributions, then other principal obligations, then accessories, and within a category the payment does not cover in proportion between types, each type oldest first. Shares are split by largest remainder, so they add up to the bank.
- **Beyond what is due** (`ro.taxaccount.advance`: `at_due_date`): the obligations the books hold falling due later, each due date in turn, in the same order: ANAF keeps the money in the single account and settles them as they fall due (pct. 7–9.1). What no obligation takes is a payment not yet distributed: Dr 4482, an open item of the authority (`taxaccount:undistributed`), with an info issue; no accessories are owed for a sum paid before the obligation was declared (art. 173 alin. (3)).
- **Into a type's own account:** that type's principal obligations by age, then its accessories (art. 165 alin. (1) lit. a)); the excess is paid in excess (4482). A type that is paid into the single account is refused (`TAX_SINGLE_ACCOUNT`).
- **Money received** settles the claims it repays (VAT to recover, sums paid in excess): those designated, else the one claim it equals (`TAX_REFUND_ITEM` otherwise).
- **Refused:** without the fact `tax.facilities` (a `profile.fact` question, `TAX_FACILITIES`); with payment facilities, insolvency or state aid to repay (`TAX_FACILITIES_UNSUPPORTED`: art. 163 alin. (10), art. 165 alin. (4)–(6); state aid and de minimis to recover are extinguished first, OPANAF nr. 1.613/2018 pct. 3^1 as introduced by OPANAF nr. 146/2025); a PFA, II or IF (`TAX_ACCOUNT_INDIVIDUAL`: their single account 55.04 has its own methodology, OPANAF nr. 2.937/2018); a currency other than lei; an obligation without a due date (`TAX_DUE_UNKNOWN`); a designation the law does not allow (`TAX_DESIGNATION`).
- **Reads** the authority's open items: a new obligation, or a payment of one, makes the proposal stale.
- **Templates:** `tax.pay` templates fix only `to` (scope: the authority, `movement.paid`, words of the text); the distribution is always the engine's, so a template earns autonomy like a bank match, and recurring payments to the authority are `template.candidate`s. The worklist proposes `tax.pay` for an unexplained transfer to a Treasury IBAN (bank code TREZ).
